## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [1] [OPLD: On-Policy Latent Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28154)
*Shoutai Zhu, Tianyang Xu, Bin Sun, Mingyuan Xu, Yu Liu, Qinzhen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 高优先级 (95.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OPLD 提出一种在线策略潜蒸馏框架，将特权多模态 CoT 诱导的推理能力迁移到连续 latent 推理表征中，在多个多模态基准上超越现有潜推理方法并达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交错多模态 CoT 依赖外部定义的推理轨迹和视觉操作，限制了灵活抽象的视觉思维发展。而现有视觉-latent 方法主要通过压缩辅助视觉特征的对齐来监督 latent 状态，将其视为视觉观测的代理而非主动推理状态，因此只能捕获给定证据而无法内化多模态 CoT 诱导的抽象推理过程。

Method: OPLD 提出 On-Policy Latent Distillation 框架，在推理过程层面监督 latent 表征，将特权多模态 CoT 的推理能力蒸馏到连续 latent 推理表征中，而非传统的特征级对齐。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上的大量实验表明，OPLD 一致优于现有潜推理方法，并在多个基准上达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 在推理过程层面监督 latent 表征比传统特征级对齐为多模态潜推理提供了更有效的范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 OPLD（On-Policy Latent Distillation），将多模态 CoT 的推理能力蒸馏到连续 latent 推理表征中，属于连续潜空间中的多模态推理，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 continuous_multimodal_reasoning（连续多模态推理与潜空间思维）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent multimodal reasoning via on-policy distillation, a top-priority direction with strong benchmark gains. Signals: continuous latent reasoning, multimodal CoT distillation, SOTA on multimodal benchmarks, on-policy training, latent representation supervision

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal Chain-of-Thought (CoT) improves visual reasoning by incorporating auxiliary visual evidence into intermediate reasoning. However, existing approaches remain constrained by externally defined reasoning traces and visual operations, limiting their ability to develop flexible and abstract visual thinking. Reasoning with latent has recently offered a promising direction by internalizing intermediate computation into continuous representations. Nevertheless, existing visual-latent methods mainly supervise latent states through alignment with compressed auxiliary visual features, treating them as proxies for visual observations rather than active reasoning states. Consequently, they capture the provided evidence but fail to fully internalize the abstract reasoning process induced by multimodal CoT. In this paper, we propose OPLD (On-Policy Latent Distillation), a simple framework that transfers the reasoning capability induced by privileged multimodal CoT into latent reasoning representations. Extensive experiments on diverse multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that OPLD consistently outperforms existing latent reasoning methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks. The results suggest that supervising latent representations at the reasoning-process level provides a more effective paradigm for multimodal latent reasoning than conventional feature-level alignment.

</details>


<div id='direction-continuous_language_multimodal-continuous_language_flow'></div>

## 连续语言模型与语言流 [[Back]](#toc)

连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模，包括 ELF、CoLa、扩散语言模型与 flow matching language model。

### [2] [Latent-Kernel Discrete Flow Maps for Few-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27529)
*Mansoor Ahmed, Yue-Tsz Fan, Hemanth Venkateswara, Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (94.22) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Latent-Kernel Discrete Flow Maps (LKF)，一种从零训练的离散 flow-map 核，通过 M 个因子化组件共享单一潜变量来表达 token 间的相关更新，使少步生成无需蒸馏即可保持相关性，在 LM1B 和 WikiText-103 上生成困惑度提升 2.1x-3.3x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散与 flow-matching 模型在每一步将 token 独立决策，导致少步生成时难以处理需要位置间耦合的目标（如主谓一致），需要大量函数评估修复不匹配。现有少步方法通过蒸馏或整流慢速教师模型来弥补相关性损失，但继承了教师模型的质量上限。作者希望模型能原生表达相关步骤。

Method: 提出 Latent-Kernel Discrete Flow Maps (LKF)，一种 flow-map 核，是 M 个因子化组件的混合，由单一共享潜变量绑定。在给定潜变量条件下每个组件计算廉价，混合在潜变量上以闭式求和（小 M 时）。每序列只采样一个潜变量并在整个去噪轨迹中复用，因此单步放置相关补全的采样时间复杂度与因子化模型相同。MDLM 是 M=1 时的特例。

Result: 在 One-Billion-Word (LM1B) 和 WikiText-103 的无条件文本生成实验中，LKF 学习到强异构组件，生成困惑度相对似然基线提升 2.1x-3.3x 且不损失多样性。增益随 M 增大，M=8 时超越蒸馏和整流少步采样器。

Conclusion: LKF 证明离散 flow-map 模型可以原生表达 token 间的相关更新，无需蒸馏即可实现高质量少步文本生成，为离散 flow matching 语言模型提供了新的建模范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Latent-Kernel Discrete Flow Maps (LKF)，一种离散 flow-matching 语言模型，通过共享潜变量表达相关 token 更新，属于连续/潜空间语言建模与 flow matching 语言模型范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 continuous_language_flow。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous/discrete flow matching for few-step text generation with a novel latent-kernel design, strongly matching the user's top-priority continuous language model direction. Signals: continuous_language_model, flow_matching, discrete_flow_maps, few-step_generation, latent_kernel

Abstract: Discrete diffusion and flow-matching models denoise a sequence over many steps, but to keep each step cheap, they factorize the transition across positions and decide every token independently. This makes few-step generation challenging for text when the target couples two positions, such as a subject and a verb that must agree. An independent update commits to them separately, and many function evaluations are spent repairing the mismatch. Existing few-step methods buy back the lost correlation by distilling or rectifying a slow teacher, and so inherit the teacher's quality ceiling. We ask instead whether a model can express correlated steps natively, and answer with Latent-Kernel Discrete Flow Maps (LKF), a from-scratch flow-map kernel that is a mixture of M factorized components tied by a single shared latent. Conditioned on the latent, each component is cheap, and the mixture is summed over the latent in closed form for small M. We show that a single step places mass on correlated completions with the same sampling time complexity as a factorized model, since one latent is drawn per sequence and reused across the entire denoising trajectory. We also show that the Masked Diffusion Language Model (MDLM) is a special case of our LKF model at M=1. The experiments for unconditional text generation on the One-Billion-Word (LM1B) and WikiText-103 benchmarks show that our LKF model learns strongly heterogeneous components and improves generative perplexity by 2.1x to 3.3x over the likelihood baselines without losing diversity. The gain grows with M, and at M=8, it surpasses distilled and rectified few-step samplers. The source code is available at:

</details>


### [3] [Beyond the Bidirectional Promise: Re-evaluating the Robustness of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27386)
*Saurabh Yadav, Badri Narayana Patro, Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了扩散语言模型（DLM）在自然噪声与对抗攻击下的鲁棒性和校准性能，与自回归基线（LLaDA-8B vs LLaMA-3-8B、Dream-7B vs Qwen2.5-7B）进行参数匹配对比，覆盖32种自然扰动、对抗梯度探针与机制性隐状态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型通过双向上下文和迭代精炼提供了自回归生成的替代方案，但其在自然输入噪声和对抗攻击下的可靠性尚未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用参数匹配的成对设计（LLaDA-8B vs LLaMA-3-8B、Dream-7B vs Qwen2.5-7B），在32种自然扰动条件下评估鲁棒性，结合对抗梯度探针和机制性隐状态分析，隔离架构固有属性与权重依赖行为。

Result: 发现DLM具有细微的鲁棒性特征：高度随机的损失景观能自然抵抗基于梯度的对抗后缀，但对自然噪声无保证防御；DLM表现出系统性过度自信；机制性探针显示所有模型都能完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性完全源于解码器路由失败。

Conclusion: DLM的鲁棒性无法通过表面层面的提示修补来改善，必须从根本上集成到迭代解码循环中；日常鲁棒性是权重依赖而非架构固有的。

Classification reason: 论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的鲁棒性与校准，属于连续语言建模范畴，与 CANONICAL 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"（continuous_language_flow）高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly evaluates continuous/diffusion language model robustness and calibration, a key priority subtopic, though it focuses on robustness rather than generation capability. Signals: diffusion language model, robustness, calibration, LLaDA, Dream

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation by enabling bidirectional context and iterative refinement. However, their reliability under natural input noise and adversarial attacks remains under-explored. To address this, we systematically evaluate DLM robustness and calibration against AR baselines, using two parameter-matched pairs (LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B and Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B) across 32 natural perturbation conditions, adversarial gradient probes, and mechanistic hidden-state analyses. This paired design effectively isolates architecture-intrinsic properties from weight-dependent behaviors. We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [4] [Where and When to Commit: Candidate-Aware Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28166)
*Chia-Ming Lee, Ming-Ching Chang, Xin Li, Yu-Lun Liu, Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (95.0) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 提出 LATCH，一种免训练的候选感知早期退出框架，用于加速扩散语言模型（DLM）解码。通过 Confidence-Verified Commit (CVC) 控制何时停止序列，Block-Wise Early Commit (BWEC) 控制何处加速，在 LLaDA 和 Dream 上实现 9.3-17.8x 短答案任务和 2.0-3.3x 长推理任务的端到端 TPS 加速，同时保持与全解码精度相差 2.0 个百分点以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在每个去噪步骤都会暴露临时预测，为生成时提前退出提供了机会。但现有早期退出门控依赖固定区域置信度统计或调度相关规则，证据过于粗糙，在长思维链输出上会过早触发；自适应采样虽能加速位置提交但无法验证输出是否稳定。需要一种将"何时停止"与"何处加速"两个轴分离、并匹配各自证据范围的免训练加速框架。

Method: 提出 LATCH 框架，包含两个组件：Confidence-Verified Commit (CVC) 通过验证置信度和动态提取候选跨度上的持续 argmax 稳定性来决定序列何时停止，使用从任务输出格式确定的确定性解析器；Block-Wise Early Commit (BWEC) 对非最终块应用更廉价的局部规则来加速，而最终块和全局终止仍由 CVC 控制。该方法无需后缀提示构造，是 prompt-anchor-free 但 format-aware 的。

Result: 在 11 个任务的零样本设置下使用 LLaDA 和 Dream 评估，LATCH 在全部 22 个评估设置中保持在全解码精度 2.0 个百分点以内，使用一组冻结的超参数可跨骨干网络无调参迁移，短答案任务实现 9.3-17.8x 端到端 TPS 加速，长推理任务实现 2.0-3.3x 加速。

Conclusion: LATCH 通过将"何时停止"与"何处加速"两个决策轴分离并匹配各自证据范围，实现了扩散语言模型的高效免训练加速，在保持精度的同时显著提升解码速度，且无需针对不同骨干网络调参。

Classification reason: 论文研究扩散语言模型（DLM）的生成时早期退出与加速解码，属于连续语言模型/扩散语言模型范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 continuous_language_flow（连续语言模型与语言流）。

Importance reason: Directly targets continuous language model decoding acceleration with strong speedups and format-aware early exit, highly aligned with the user's top-priority continuous language model and efficiency subtopics. Signals: diffusion language model, early exit, training-free acceleration, LLaDA, Dream

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) expose a provisional prediction at every denoising step, creating an opportunity for generation-time early exit that stops decoding before the schedule is exhausted. Existing early-exit gates decide termination from fixed-region confidence statistics or schedule-dependent rules, evidence too coarse for a decision that freezes every remaining position at once, so they fire prematurely on long chain-of-thought outputs whose answers stabilize only near the end. Adaptive sampling, the other axis of training-free acceleration, paces how quickly positions commit while decoding continues but never verifies that the output itself has stabilized. We introduce a training-free, candidate-aware early-exit framework that keeps the two axes separate and matches each decision to evidence of its own scope. Confidence-Verified Commit (CVC) governs when the sequence may stop by verifying confidence and sustained argmax stability over the dynamically extracted candidate span using a deterministic parser specified from each task's output format. Block-Wise Early Commit (BWEC) governs where to accelerate by applying a cheaper local rule to non-final blocks, while leaving the final block and global termination under CVC. We refer to their combination as LATCH (Localized Acceleration with Tracked-Candidate Halting). Unlike prior methods, LATCH needs no suffix-prompt construction; it is prompt-anchor-free but format-aware. We evaluate LATCH end to end on 11 tasks under zero-shot settings using LLaDA and Dream. LATCH stays within 2.0 percentage points of full-decoding accuracy across all 22 evaluation settings, with one frozen hyperparameter set that transfers cross-backbone untuned, while achieving end-to-end TPS speedups of 9.3-17.8x on short-answer tasks and 2.0-3.3x on long-reasoning tasks.

</details>

<div id='direction-world_model-embodied_world_model'></div>

## 具身世界模型与规划控制 [[Back]](#toc)

面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型。

### [5] [QQWorld: Quantile-Quantile Matching for World Model Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28415)
*Zhoushun Yu, Xiaoyu Hu, Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (83.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: QQWorld 提出用分位数-分位数匹配目标替代 Epps-Pulley 目标来正则化潜在世界模型的潜在分布，解决 EP 目标在尾部样本上梯度消失的问题，并通过跨批次 QQ 扩大有效排序池，提升规划成功率与高斯对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型通过在紧凑表征空间中预测未来状态实现高效规划，但其性能严重依赖所学潜在分布的质量。LeWorldModel 使用 Epps-Pulley 目标将潜在分布正则化为各向同性高斯，但 EP 的校正梯度对孤立尾部样本会迅速消失，导致重尾偏差无法被充分控制。

Method: 提出 QQWorld，用分位数-分位数匹配目标替代 EP 目标，直接对齐投影后的潜在样本与秩匹配的高斯分位数，从而在尾部保持有效的校正梯度。进一步提出跨批次 QQ（cross-batch QQ），利用先前批次的分离样本扩大有效排序池，并刻画其偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在四个控制环境中，QQWorld 有效提升了 LeWM 的平均规划成功率，同时持续获得更好的高斯对齐和更薄的潜在分布尾部。

Conclusion: QQWorld 通过分位数-分位数匹配正则化有效解决了 EP 目标在尾部梯度消失的问题，显著改善了潜在世界模型的规划性能与潜在分布质量。

Classification reason: 论文提出 QQWorld，通过分位数-分位数匹配正则化改进潜在世界模型（LeWorldModel）的规划能力，属于世界模型方向。其核心是提升潜在世界模型在控制环境中的规划成功率，与"具身世界模型与规划控制"这一 CANONICAL 子主题高度契合。

Importance reason: Solid world-model regularization work, but it is an incremental improvement on a niche baseline (LeWM) rather than a major advance in the user's priority areas. Signals: world model, latent regularization, quantile-quantile matching, planning, Gaussian alignment

Abstract: Latent world models enable efficient planning by predicting future states in a compact representation space, but their performance depends critically on the quality of the learned latent distribution. LeWorldModel (LeWM) regularizes its latents toward an isotropic Gaussian using the Epps-Pulley (EP) objective. We show that the corrective gradients of EP rapidly vanish for isolated tail samples, leaving heavy-tailed deviations insufficiently controlled. To address this limitation, we propose QQWorld, which replaces EP with a quantile-quantile matching objective that directly aligns projected latent samples with rank-matched Gaussian quantiles, thereby maintaining effective corrective gradients in the tails. We further develop cross-batch QQ, which enlarges the effective ranking pool using detached samples from previous batches, and characterize its bias-variance trade-off. Across four control environments, QQWorld effectively improves the average planning success rate of LeWM, while consistently yielding better Gaussian alignment and thinner latent tails.

</details>

Abstract: Imitation learning has shown increasing promise for autonomous robotic surgery, yet safe deployment remains challenging due to the safety-critical nature of surgical tasks and the complexity and variability of surgical environments. Failure detection is therefore an essential safeguard, but its development remains difficult due to the challenges of scarce failure data, highly variable manipulation dynamics, and the need to balance missed detections against disruptive false alarms. To address these challenges, we introduce FoMo-FD (Flow-Matching World Model for Failure Detection), a failure detection method that learns nominal short-horizon visual dynamics with an action-conditioned flow-matching world model. FoMo-FD scores the inverse-transport nonconformity of observed endpoint latents, enabling window-level detection of visual-action inconsistencies without requiring failure demonstrations. Detection thresholds are obtained by conformal calibration on successful executions, yielding task-specific alarms without assuming future failure types. We evaluate FoMo-FD on four surgically relevant manipulation tasks with twenty failure modes across simulation and real-world experiments using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). Results show that FoMo-FD outperforms observation-level anomaly baselines and a prediction-error variant of the same world model, with the wrist-camera view achieving the strongest performance, including a 96.6% failure detection rate (FDR) at a 1.3% false alarm rate (FAR).

</details>

Classification reason: 论文探讨将 VLM 作为协调智能体嵌入人-UAV 回路，涉及无人机任务协调、任务分配与规划控制，属于具身智能/机器人规划控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision Language Models (VLMs) have created new opportunities for disaster response, where responders must interpret large volumes of sensor data under time pressure. Current VLM applications include social media monitoring for situational awareness, generation of draft action plans, and translation of technical alerts into public-facing messages. While these efforts can accelerate information flow, they remain largely limited to decision-support roles. Such approaches can increase operator burden because humans must still translate outputs into coordinated actions across teams and robotic assets. This study explores the viability of embedding VLMs as coordination agents within the human-UAV loop. The proposed architecture integrates natural language interaction, mission-level task coordination, software-in-the-loop implementation, and communication aligned with the Incident Command System (ICS). Rather than functioning solely as advisory tools, VLMs facilitate communication between human operators, mission control logic, and UAV task execution. The framework was developed using a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) approach, with use case and block definition diagrams representing system roles, internal structure, and component interactions. Three key elements, the VLM Coordinator Agent, UAV Mission Control, and Task Allocator, were implemented within an integrated simulation and control environment. A preliminary human-factors evaluation with seven participants showed reduced perceived workload across mental demand, effort, and frustration, along with high ratings for AI trust and communication clarity. By integrating MBSE, software-in-the-loop testing, and human-factors evaluation, this work advances scalable human-autonomy teaming for high-stakes disaster response, with broader implications for aerospace autonomy and civil safety.

</details>

Abstract: Building generalizable agents for diverse applications remains a fundamental challenge. While imitation learning-based policies succeed in specific training environments, they often fail to generalize to novel scenes and tasks. In this work, we propose World Action Planner, a robot planning system that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and the physical grounding of a multi-task pose-image conditioned world model. Our system enables an agent to propose initial action plans and iteratively refine them via optimization and search, reasoning over imagined world model rollouts. We demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance across compositional tasks, new layouts, and zero-shot generalization scenarios, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art end-to-end policy models such as VLAs and WAMs. Project website at

### [9] [The Geometric Nature and a Free Proxy for Flow-Matching Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27933)
*Ziyang Rao, Yiren Zhao, Weiyu Guo, Ben Fei, Yandong Guo, Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (89.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度解释 flow matching（FM）在速度场中的不确定性，提出 denoising acceleration（accel）作为无需额外训练、重采样或多次模型评估的免费不确定性代理，用于具身模型在线失败检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching 已成为现代具身模型常用的动作头范式，但作为条件生成模型它不显式暴露内在不确定性，在场景误判或 OOD 输入时会产生错误动作块。现有不确定性估计方法存在额外训练开销、高计算成本和低泛化能力等问题，因此需要一种高效、通用且免费的 FM 不确定性代理来保障安全部署。

Method: 作者对 FM 不确定性给出几何解释：不确定性表现为速度场偏离理想仿射-各向同性收缩场。基于此提出 denoising acceleration（accel），通过单次前向传播测量去噪轨迹的弯曲程度，无需额外模型评估、训练或重采样，即可作为 FM 不确定性的忠实代理，并用于在线失败检测。

Result: 理论与实验均证明 accel 是 FM 不确定性的忠实代理。在现实部署预算下，accel 能在终止前识别失败 rollout，匹配甚至超越基于重采样和训练的昂贵基线方法。

Conclusion: accel 提供了一种高度泛化、零成本且无需额外训练的 FM 不确定性代理，为具身模型的安全部署提供了实用的在线失败检测手段。

Classification reason: 论文研究 flow matching 作为具身模型动作头（action head）的不确定性估计与在线失败检测，属于具身智能动作规划与控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Provides a cost-free uncertainty proxy for flow-matching action heads, directly relevant to embodied world models and continuous latent reasoning. Signals: flow matching, uncertainty estimation, embodied model, online failure detection, world model

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) has become a popular action head paradigm for modern embodied models. However, as a conditional generative model, it does not explicitly expose its inherent uncertainty, producing faulty action chunks even when it misinterprets the scene or encounters out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs. Therefore, determining when an FM-generated action can be trusted is essential for safe deployment, yet existing uncertainty estimation methods on real-time control suffer from several issues: extra training budget, high computational overhead, and low generalization ability. In this work, we provide a geometric interpretation of FM uncertainty in the velocity field, showing that uncertainty manifests as deviation from an ideal affine-isotropic contraction field. Building on this observation, we introduce denoising acceleration ($\mathrm{accel}$), a highly-generalizable and cost-free uncertainty proxy that measures the bending of the denoising trajectory from a single forward pass, without additional model evaluations, training, or resampling. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that $\mathrm{accel}$ is a faithful proxy for FM uncertainty and further test its utility in online failure detection. Results show that $\mathrm{accel}$ identifies failing rollouts well before termination, matching or even outperforming costly resampling- and training-based baselines across settings under realistic deployment budget. Code and demos available at: .

</details>


### [10] [MARS-RA: Rank Aggregation for Credit Assignment via Multimodal Comparisons in Embodied Multi-Agent Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27967)
*Dawei Wang, Di Zhao, Xinyuan Liu, Marci Chi Ma, Xiaoyang Liu, Chengming Zhou, Gary Ushaw, Richard Davison*

TL;DR: MARS-RA 将具身多智能体协作中的信用分配问题重构为基于大多模态模型贡献比较的排序聚合问题，将绝对估计转为相对估计以增强对噪声和动态智能体参与的鲁棒性，并用于基于势能的奖励塑形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作多智能体强化学习中的信用分配是根本性挑战，尤其在具身 AI 场景下反馈有限且延迟、活跃智能体数量动态变化，传统绝对估计方法对噪声和动态参与不鲁棒。

Method: 提出 MARS-RA 框架，将信用分配重构为排序聚合问题：利用大多模态模型生成基于贡献的智能体两两比较，将比较结果转换为贡献分数用于基于势能的奖励塑形，并提供收敛性与鲁棒性的理论证明，以 Shapley 值作为可解释参考。

Result: 在多种不同类型的挑战性任务上实验表明，MARS-RA 能有效引导智能体实现协作。

Conclusion: 从绝对估计转向相对估计的排序聚合方法能提升具身多智能体协作中信用分配的鲁棒性，有效应对噪声与动态智能体参与，促进智能体间的有效合作。

Classification reason: 论文研究具身多智能体协作中的信用分配问题，属于具身智能环境下的多智能体强化学习与协作控制，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent RL credit assignment, which is outside the user's core continuous multimodal, video generation, and world model priorities. Signals: embodied multi-agent, credit assignment, rank aggregation, multimodal comparisons, reward shaping

Abstract: Credit assignment is a fundamental challenge in cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, particularly in embodied AI settings characterized by limited and delayed feedback as well as dynamically changing numbers of active agents. We propose MARS-RA, a framework that reformulates credit assignment as a rank aggregation problem using contribution-based pairwise comparisons among agents generated by large multimodal models. This shift from absolute to relative estimation ensures robustness against noise and dynamic agent participation, converting comparison results into contribution scores for potential-based reward shaping. We provide theoretical justification for the convergence and robustness of the proposed framework, and show that Shapley values can be used as an interpretive reference. Experimental results on challenging tasks of different types indicate that MARS-RA can guide agents toward effective cooperation.

</details>

*Zexuan Yan, Yuzhou Wu, Yue Ma, Zonghang He, Kaibo Yin, Xiaobing Tu, Yinggui Wang, Jinkui Ren, Xiantao Zhang, Shijian Wang, Jinghong Liu, Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

TL;DR: EgoGenesis 是一个自我中心世界-动作模拟器，基于预训练视频生成先验，引入在线锚定投影记忆（OAPM）和动作-3D旋转位置编码（A3D-RoPE）两种几何感知条件机制，合成可控的高质量操作视频以扩充稀缺的真实世界训练数据，显著提升下游真实机器人任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频为具身智能提供了丰富的操作经验，但跨场景、物体、动作和具身形态收集多样化的自我中心数据成本高昂且稀缺。作者希望构建一个能够合成可控、高质量操作视频的模拟器，以扩充真实训练数据，提升下游机器人任务的泛化能力。

Method: EgoGenesis 构建在预训练视频生成先验之上，引入两种几何感知条件机制：(1) 在线锚定投影记忆（OAPM）在自回归生成过程中保留首帧 3D 场景锚点，同时周期性刷新近期状态；(2) 动作-3D 旋转位置编码（A3D-RoPE）将末端执行器运动编码为相机感知的 3D 旋转坐标，将动作几何注入骨架到视频的交叉注意力中，实现精确控制。

Result: 在长自我中心 rollout 中提升了视觉保真度、几何稳定性和动作对齐。用 400 条生成轨迹增强 400 条真实轨迹后，单臂任务的分布外真实机器人成功率从 77% 提升到 84%，双臂任务从 53% 提升到 70%，证明合成数据显著改善了下游 WAM 泛化。

Conclusion: EgoGenesis 通过几何感知条件机制有效提升了自我中心世界-动作模拟的视觉质量与动作对齐，且合成数据能显著提升下游真实机器人任务的分布外泛化能力，验证了自我中心世界-动作模拟器作为数据扩充手段的实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EgoGenesis，一个面向具身智能的自我中心世界-动作模拟器，通过视频生成合成可控操作视频来扩充真实机器人训练数据，并验证了合成数据能提升下游真实机器人任务成功率。这属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] embodied_world_model。

Importance reason: 该工作将视频生成先验与几何感知条件机制结合，用于具身世界模型与数据增强，与用户世界模型和视频生成方向高度相关。 Signals: 世界模型, 视频生成, 具身智能, 数据增强, 动作条件生成

Abstract: Egocentric video offers rich manipulation experience for embodied AI, yet collecting diverse egocentric data across scenes, objects, motions, and embodiments remains costly. We present \method, an egocentric world-action simulator that synthesizes controllable, high-quality manipulation videos to expand scarce real-world training data. \method{} builds on a pretrained video generation prior and introduces two geometry-aware conditioning mechanisms. Online Anchored Projective Memory (OAPM) preserves a first-frame 3D scene anchor while periodically refreshing a recent state during autoregressive generation. Action-3D Rotary Position Embedding (A3D-RoPE) encodes end-effector motion with camera-aware 3D rotary coordinates, injecting action geometry into skeleton-to-video cross-attention for precise control. Together, these components improve visual fidelity, geometric stability, and action alignment in long egocentric rollouts. Moreover, augmenting 400 real trajectories with 400 \method-generated trajectories improves out-of-distribution real-robot success from 77\% to 84\% on single-arm tasks and from 53\% to 70\% on dual-arm tasks, demonstrating that the synthesized data substantially improve downstream WAM generalization.

</details>


### [12] [QuantWAMs: Calibrating at the Right Granularity for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28405)
*Jiacheng Zhou, Jinfan Lv, Ruixuan Li, Longtai Zhang, Yan Wang, Wenqiang Zhang, Lizhe Qi*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (65.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: QuantWAMs 是一个面向 World Action Models (WAMs) 的后训练量化 (PTQ) 框架，通过共享基异常校准、联合训练目标显著性、固定干预 rollout 审计三种策略，在 W4A4 主导设置下将模拟结果与 FP16 的差距控制在 0.2-0.7 个百分点，并将峰值权重-激活内存降至 FP16 的约 29%，提供 1.4-1.6 倍块级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models (WAMs) 联合预测未来观测与动作，但其迭代去噪和闭环执行使得高效部署成本高昂。现有 PTQ 方法依赖开环目标、同质模型假设和不符合部署场景的校准分布，难以适用于 WAMs。

Method: QuantWAMs 提出三种策略：(1) 共享基异常校准，仅跨坐标兼容模块池化激活证据；(2) 联合训练目标显著性，从视频-动作联合梯度计算经验 Fisher 分数，在校准稳定的层粒度分配权重精度；(3) 固定干预 rollout 审计，利用可达闭环状态修订去噪步保护调度而不改变精度预算。

Result: 在 Fast-WAM 和 LingBot-VA 上，跨 RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO 及 AgiBot G2 真实机器人操作任务评估。W4A4 主导设置下模拟结果与 FP16 相差 0.2-0.7 个百分点；真实机器人实验验证了三个操作任务的部署可行性；峰值权重-激活内存降至 FP16 的约 29%，块级加速 1.4-1.6 倍。

Conclusion: QuantWAMs 证明将量化决策与模型结构、rollout 分布和任务目标定义的校准上下文对齐，可显著提升 WAMs 的量化部署效率，同时保持接近 FP16 的性能。

Classification reason: 论文针对 World Action Models (WAMs) 的量化部署优化，WAMs 联合预测未来观测与动作，属于具身智能/机器人世界模型范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Quantization for world action models is a niche efficiency contribution, but its W4A4 memory/acceleration gains and closed-loop calibration insights are valuable for efficient world-model deployment. Signals: world model, efficient inference, quantization, W4A4, closed-loop rollout

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, but their iterative denoising and closed-loop execution make efficient deployment costly. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods are poorly suited to WAMs because they rely on open-loop objectives, homogeneous model assumptions, and calibration distributions that do not reflect deployment. We present QuantWAMs, a PTQ framework that aligns quantization decisions with the calibration context defined by model structure, rollout distribution, and task objective. QuantWAMs introduces three strategies: shared-basis outlier calibration, which pools activation evidence only across coordinate-compatible modules; co-training-objective saliency, which computes empirical-Fisher scores from the joint video--action gradient and assigns weight precision at a calibration-stable layer granularity; and fixed-intervention rollout auditing, which revises denoising-step protection schedules using reachable closed-loop states without changing the precision budget. We evaluate QuantWAMs on Fast-WAM and LingBot-VA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot manipulation with an AgiBot G2. Under a W4A4-dominant setting, the reported simulation means differ from FP16 by 0.2--0.7 percentage points. Real-robot trials further establish deployment feasibility on three manipulation tasks. For the targeted video and action blocks, QuantWAMs reduces peak weight-and-activation memory to about 29\% of FP16 and provides 1.4--1.6$\times$ block-level speedups.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能面临根本性的数据瓶颈：模型需要捕捉第一人称感知、全身动作、灵巧操作、物体状态、声音与触觉在人类追求目标过程中的协同演化，而现有数据集在视角、模态或空间尺度上割裂了这种体验，导致完整的感知-动作回路只能被部分观测。

Method: 提出 Ambient Capture Engine (ACE)，一种人体中心数据引擎，将真实家庭环境转化为空间校准、时间同步的录制工作室。ACE 在两种互补尺度上运行：桌面尺度解析手-物操作，房间尺度捕捉全身动作、移动与交互。系统同步记录第一人称与多视角外视角视频、全身与关节手部动作、物体几何与 6-DoF 轨迹、音频与触觉信号，形成统一多感官流。基于 ACE 构建 ACE-Data-0 数据集，并引入从信号到场景组件再到交互的分层评测基准。

Result: ACE-Data-0 包含 150 小时、1700 万帧视频，覆盖 200 个任务类别，由 50 名参与者在 2 个环境中完成，共 7.5 万交互片段，涵盖原子操作、长时序家庭活动链与人-场景交互。评测显示现有 SOTA 方法在接触、遮挡、自我运动与长时序场景下存在显著差距。

Conclusion: ACE-Data-0 提供同步的人体演示，带有对齐的感知、运动学与接触监督，为模仿学习、世界模型、视觉-语言-动作系统与具身 AI 提供可扩展的基础，同时揭示现有方法在真实家庭环境复杂交互中的不足。

Classification reason: 论文提出面向具身智能的人体中心数据引擎 ACE 与数据集 ACE-Data-0，提供同步的多模态感知-动作数据，用于模仿学习、世界模型与 VLA 系统，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。

Importance reason: Strong embodied world-model data engine, but lacks direct continuous-language/multimodal or autoregressive generation focus. Signals: embodied data engine, world model, multimodal sensory streams, hierarchical benchmark, no continuous token/flow matching

Abstract: Embodied intelligence faces a fundamental data bottleneck. Models must capture how first-person perception, whole-body motion, dexterous manipulation, object state, sound, and touch evolve together as humans pursue goals over time. Existing datasets fragment this experience across viewpoints, modalities, or spatial scales, leaving the full perception-action loop only partially observed. We introduce the Ambient Capture Engine (ACE), a human-centric data engine that transforms real home environments into spatially calibrated, temporally synchronized recording studios. ACE operates at two complementary scales: a table-scale configuration resolves hand-object manipulation, while a room-scale configuration captures whole-body motion, locomotion, and interactions across a furnished home. ACE records egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, full-body and articulated hand motion, object geometry and 6-DoF trajectories, audio, and tactile signals as a unified multisensory stream. Using ACE, we build ACE-Data-0, comprising 150 hours and 17M video frames across 200 task categories, performed by 50 participants in 2 environments, for a total of 75,000 interaction episodes. The dataset spans atomic manipulation, long-horizon chains of household activities, and human-scene interaction, while preserving natural behavioral variation through goal-level rather than step-by-step instructions. We further introduce a hierarchical benchmark that progresses from signals to scene components and then to interactions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art methods expose substantial gaps under contact, occlusion, egomotion, and long temporal horizons. ACE-Data-0 provides synchronized human demonstrations with aligned perceptual, kinematic, and contact supervision, offering a scalable foundation for imitation learning, world models, vision-language-action systems, and embodied AI.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型为具身智能提供预测核心，但其预测层引入了新的安全边界——妥协可从数据、传感器、提示或反馈传播到物理动作。现有研究缺乏对世界模型安全威胁的系统性梳理。

Method: 提出生命周期分类法，将威胁按数据构建、表征学习、状态接地、想象、轨迹评估、执行与长期适应等阶段组织；映射攻击到世界模型安全属性；概述评测协议与防御结构（来源追踪、鲁棒接地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计、部署保障）。

Result: 系统梳理了投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示注入、轨迹操纵、供应链攻击等攻击家族在世界模型语境下的独特含义，并指出世界模型可作为运行时安全盾牌，但被妥协或过度信任时会产生预测性安全幻觉。

Conclusion: 世界模型安全需要全生命周期视角，防御应覆盖来源追踪、鲁棒接地、不确定性感知、轨迹门控、反馈审计与部署保障，同时警惕世界模型作为安全盾牌时的双重性风险。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦世界模型驱动的具身智能安全威胁、防御与评测，属于具身世界模型方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: A security-focused survey on world models, but it is a broad lifecycle taxonomy rather than a core technical advance in your priority directions. Signals: world model, embodied AI, security survey, lifecycle taxonomy, threat-defense-evaluation

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>

Conclusion: 静止状态公式能够在不依赖显式运动观测的情况下重建可动关节物体，验证了几何、语义与运动先验可以补偿运动线索缺失，为可交互数字孪生构建提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文关注从静止状态重建可交互数字孪生（articulated object），涉及物理一致性与交互式模拟器，属于可交互与物理世界建模范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] interactive_physical_world。同时使用视频扩散模型合成关节运动假设，也涉及多模态生成方向。

Signals: articulated object reconstruction, rest-state formulation, video diffusion for joint synthesis, explicit mesh intermediate representation, world model adjacent

Abstract: Building interactive digital twins requires recovering both 3D geometry and the kinematic structures that govern how objects articulate. Yet existing methods for articulated object reconstruction require explicitly observable motion from multiple articulation states. We introduce a rest-state formulation that reconstructs articulated objects from a single closed configuration, an inherently ill-posed setting where geometry, semantics, and motion priors compensate for the absence of motion cues. Our framework adopts an explicit mesh as an intermediate representation for cross-model verification and fusion, reconciling noisy outputs from vision-language and segmentation models into spatially consistent part structures. To estimate joint parameters without observed motion, we use a video diffusion model to synthesize articulation hypotheses and validate them through geometric consistency. Our approach achieves accurate part decomposition and physically plausible articulation, performing competitively with motion-observing reconstruction-based, generation-based, and modular pretrained-model baselines.

</details>

Importance reason: Focuses on drone-view human intent via 3D avatar placement, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, 3D avatar placement, monocular depth, UAV footage, human intent recognition

Abstract: Perceiving human motion and intent at long range is a prerequisite for socially intelligent aerial robots, yet the data to learn it barely exists. We introduce Drones2BodyLanguage, a dataset grounding human motion in real UAV footage: avatars manifesting ten communicative intents are placed into unmodified 4K drone scenes with metrically correct position, scale and orientation, maintained over hundreds of frames of camera motion. Enabling it is a lightweight geometric world model of the local scene - semantically selected anchors lifted to 3D through streaming monocular depth - in which a placement point is predicted as an affine anchor combination with provably rigid-invariant weights, and re-rendered under an SVD-fitted ground rotation. Across twelve architectures on scene- and motion-disjoint splits, training on placed data lifts mean intent accuracy by a wide margin for real, retargeted and generated motion alike, with gains confirmed on two in-the-wild scenes.

</details>


### [17] [AuricularWorld: Hierarchical Action-Guided World Modeling for Fine-Grained Auricular Structure Segmentation from CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28487)
*Jingwen Yang, Senmao Wang, Luoyao Kang, Runmeng Cui, Keying Zhang, Yunjia Bao, Haifan Gong, Lin Lin, Haiyue Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

TL;DR: AuricularWorld 提出一种基于世界模型的医学图像分割框架，在编码器-解码器架构的中间潜空间引入确定性循环状态空间模型，通过分层解剖动作更新循环状态并逐步细化潜表征，实现迭代式解剖推理，显著提升 CT 中耳廓精细结构分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT 中耳廓精细结构分割极具挑战：耳部在图像中占比小、软骨边界极不规则、软骨与周围软组织界面模糊，且临床标注包含嵌套重叠的复合结构与软骨区域。传统前馈式预测难以处理这些复杂解剖结构。

Method: 基于编码器-解码器架构，在中间潜空间引入确定性循环状态空间模型。多尺度编码器特征与部分解码表征融合形成结构观测以初始化潜动力学。推理时执行三步无真值引导的潜 rollout，分层解剖动作更新循环状态并逐步细化潜表征，最终将潜轨迹投影回解码器并与高分辨率特征结合产生分割结果。引入平衡分层动作目标以处理前景稀疏、解剖组缺失及增删操作不平衡问题。

Result: 实验表明该框架持续提升分割精度，对 CT 中小型、不规则、重叠的耳廓结构将 HD95 降低超过 43%。

Conclusion: 潜空间世界模型推理对具有挑战性的医学图像分割任务有效，验证了超越传统前馈预测的迭代解剖推理范式的价值。

Classification reason: 论文提出基于世界模型的医学图像分割框架，在中间潜空间引入确定性循环状态空间模型进行迭代解剖推理与潜空间 rollout，属于世界模型方向。虽然应用场景是医学图像分割而非物理仿真，但其核心是"潜空间世界模型推理"，与可交互/物理世界建模中的动力学建模思想相关，故映射到 CANONICAL 子主题 interactive_physical_world。

Importance reason: Medical CT segmentation with a world-model framing is far outside your core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative video/image modeling. Signals: world model framing, medical image segmentation, CT scans, recurrent state-space model, negative keyword: medical diagnosis

Abstract: Fine-grained segmentation of auricular structures in CT is challenging because the ear occupies a small image region, cartilage boundaries are highly irregular, and interfaces between cartilage and surrounding soft tissues are often ambiguous. Clinical annotations may also include both composite structures containing cartilage and adjacent skin and their corresponding cartilage-only regions, producing nested and overlapping labels. We propose a world-model-based segmentation framework that enables iterative anatomical reasoning beyond conventional feed-forward prediction. Built on an encoder-decoder architecture, the framework introduces a deterministic recurrent state-space model into the intermediate latent space. Multi-scale encoder features and partially decoded representations are fused to form a structural observation that initializes the latent dynamics. During inference, the model performs a three-step latent rollout without ground-truth guidance. Hierarchical anatomical actions update the recurrent state and progressively refine the latent representation. The resulting latent trajectory is projected back into the decoder and combined with high-resolution features to produce the final segmentation. To learn reliable latent transitions, we introduce a balanced hierarchical action objective that addresses foreground sparsity, missing anatomical groups, and imbalance between add and remove operations. Extensive experiments show that the proposed framework consistently improves segmentation accuracy and reduces HD95 by more than 43% for small, irregular, and overlapping auricular structures in CT. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of latent world-model reasoning for challenging medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [18] [Tycho: Active Abstraction with Programmatic World Models for ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28287)
*Jens Lehmann, Andrei Aioanei, Sahar Vahdati*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.SC, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

TL;DR: Tycho 是一个编码智能体系统，在 ARC-AGI-3 交互式环境中通过构建、测试、规划、修复或绕过程序化世界模型（参数化渲染的确定性 Moore machines）来获取技能，并提出"主动抽象"（active abstraction）问题——即决定何时构建、使用或绕过模型以平衡动作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC-AGI-3 将抽象转化为交互式技能获取问题，智能体需在动作预算受限的情况下推断游戏规则、隐藏状态与目标。现有方法缺乏在交互中构建并利用可测试世界模型的系统化框架。

Method: Tycho 将环境形式化为参数化渲染的确定性 Moore machines，分离可操作观察与中间动画/关卡完成/游戏结束帧，从结构化历史中构建可执行假设模型，并支持建模、测试、规划、修复或绕过模型。比较四种编排策略（含 actor 请求委托给模型构建器），在 25 个公开游戏上使用 Claude Opus 4.8 在匹配推理预算下评估。

Result: Actor 请求委托策略获得最高平均 RHAE 88.49；GPT-5.6 Sol 与 Opus 5 均达到 100.00 RHAE 并完成全部 183 关，人类重放中位排名分别为 98.5 和 100.0；Opus 5 比官方人类基线少用 61% 的计分动作。自动修复虽使模型更准确复现转换，但仅达 83.07 RHAE。

Conclusion: 转换匹配度只反映模拟器是否复现观测动力学，而非是否识别目标或改进下一步动作。强玩法还需决定何时构建、修复、使用或绕过模型，这一联合问题被定义为"主动抽象"。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Tycho 系统，在 ARC-AGI-3 交互式环境中构建并使用游戏特定的程序化世界模型（Moore machines）进行技能获取与动作规划，属于可交互世界建模与动力学建模范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] interactive_physical_world。

Importance reason: Focuses on programmatic world models for ARC-AGI-3, but lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: world model, active abstraction, programmatic world model, ARC-AGI-3, interactive skill acquisition

Abstract: ARC-AGI-3 turns abstraction into an interactive problem of skill acquisition. A player must infer an unfamiliar game's rules, hidden state, and goal while maintaining action efficiency because every move counts. We formalize these environments as parameterized rendered deterministic Moore machines and introduce Tycho, a coding-agent system that constructs and uses game-specific models during interaction. Tycho separates actionable observations from intermediate animation, level-completion, and game-over frames. From this structured history, an agent can model, test, plan with, repair, or bypass a free-form executable hypothesis. In one matched public-set run per policy, we compare four orchestration policies on all 25 public games using Claude Opus 4.8 under matched inference budgets. Actor-requested delegation to a model builder obtains the highest observed mean Relative Human Action Efficiency (RHAE), 88.49. With this selected policy, GPT-5.6 Sol and Opus 5 both reach 100.00 RHAE and complete all 183 levels. Their game-balanced first-run human-replay midranks are 98.5 and 100.0. Opus 5 uses 61% fewer scored actions than the aggregate official human baselines. Automatic repair after verification failures produces models that reproduce observed transitions much more accurately, yet reaches only 83.07 RHAE. Transition match indicates whether a simulator reproduces observed dynamics, not whether it has identified the objective or improves the next action. Strong play also requires deciding when to construct, repair, use, or bypass a model. We call this joint problem active abstraction: generating a testable model from costly interaction and deciding when acquiring or using it is worth its cost.

</details>

### [19] [AdaAnchor4D: Anchor-Conditioned Spatiotemporal Feature Aggregation for Monocular UAV 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28320)
*Peiyi Xu, Junpeng Zhang, Guanbin Li, Ronghua Shang, Mingtao Feng, Le Dong, Weisheng Dong, Guangming Shi, Jie Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 空间 3D/4D 世界模型

Importance: 普通相关 (55.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AdaAnchor4D 提出一种自适应锚点变形框架，用于单目无人机视频的动态 4D 场景重建，通过锚点条件特征聚合、解耦局部几何变形和密度自适应坐标扭曲，解决无人机场景中时空异质性导致的动态细节模糊与重影伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目无人机视频可用于复杂城市场景的动态重建，但此类场景具有显著的时空异质性：不同区域遵循不同的时间活动模式，且部分动态区域的运动状态会随时间演化。现有基于分解共享时空特征场的动态高斯方法采用固定的平面特征组合机制，难以适应无人机场景的异质局部动态，常导致重影伪影和动态细节模糊。

Method: 提出 AdaAnchor4D 自适应锚点变形框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Anchor-Conditioned Feature Aggregation (ACFA) 使用锚点特定的聚合嵌入和时间信息自适应聚合共享时空特征，使不同局部单元获得适配其局部与时间状态的动态表征；(2) Decoupled Local Geometry Deformation (DLGD) 将锚点状态变形与局部高斯几何变形解耦；(3) Density-Adaptive Coordinate Warping (DACW) 根据轴向锚点分布对特征查询坐标进行重参数化，缓解非均匀几何采样与均匀网格参数化之间的不匹配。

Result: 在 UAV-Arc4D、VisDrone 和 UAVDT 数据集上的实验表明，AdaAnchor4D 相比代表性动态高斯方法取得了更高的渲染质量，同时保持实时渲染性能。

Conclusion: AdaAnchor4D 通过自适应锚点机制有效应对无人机场景的时空异质性，在动态 4D 重建中兼顾了渲染质量与实时性能，代码将公开。

Classification reason: 论文关注单目无人机视频的动态 4D 场景重建，属于动态场景中的世界建模，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 spatial_3d_4d_world_model（空间 3D/4D 世界模型）。

Importance reason: Focuses on UAV 4D reconstruction with dynamic Gaussians, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: 4D reconstruction, dynamic Gaussian, UAV scene, anchor-conditioned aggregation, real-time rendering

Abstract: Monocular UAV videos provide valuable observations for dynamic reconstruction of complex urban scenes. However, such scenes exhibit pronounced spatiotemporal heterogeneity: different regions follow distinct temporal activity patterns, while the motion states of some dynamic regions may further evolve over time. Although dynamic Gaussian methods based on decomposed shared spatiotemporal feature fields have achieved efficient and accurate reconstruction in object-centric or relatively compact scenes, their commonly adopted fixed plane-wise feature combination mechanisms are less suited to the heterogeneous local dynamics of UAV scenes, often leading to ghosting artifacts and blurred dynamic details. To address this challenge, we propose AdaAnchor4D, an adaptive anchor deformation framework for monocular UAV dynamic scene reconstruction. At its core, Anchor-Conditioned Feature Aggregation (ACFA) adaptively aggregates shared spatiotemporal features using anchor-specific aggregation embeddings and temporal information, allowing different local units to obtain dynamic representations tailored to their local and temporal states. Decoupled Local Geometry Deformation (DLGD) separates anchor-state deformation from local Gaussian geometry deformation, while Density-Adaptive Coordinate Warping (DACW) reparameterizes feature-query coordinates according to the axis-wise anchor distributions, alleviating the mismatch between non-uniform geometric sampling and uniform grid parameterization. Experiments on UAV-Arc4D, VisDrone, and UAVDT show that AdaAnchor4D achieves higher rendering quality than representative dynamic Gaussian methods while maintaining real-time rendering performance. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>

### [20] [ODEWorld: A Continuous Predictive Architecture via Physical-Time Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27924)
*Dongxiu Liu, Haoyi Niu, Peng Cheng, Yuan Gao, Xirui Kang, Sangli Teng, Koushil Sreenath, Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: ODEWorld 提出 Physical-Time Flow (PT-Flow)，将时序数据动力学参数化为嵌入在结构化表征空间中的 ODE，通过 ODE 求解器在压缩潜空间中进行时间积分实现未来预测，构建了连续时间潜世界模型，兼顾视频生成与机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界中的空间与时间是连续的基本属性，但现有世界建模的机器学习范式大多局限于离散时间预测，在捕捉物理世界动力学方面效率低下，且潜世界模型常面临表征坍缩问题。

Method: 提出 PT-Flow，学习一个在物理时间中运行的连续潜速度场，将时序数据底层动力学参数化为嵌入在良好结构化表征空间中的 ODE。构建 ODEWorld 连续时间潜世界模型，通过提取时变特征并在动力学表征空间与潜速度场上施加 ODE 性质，解决表征坍缩问题，支持任意时间分辨率与反向预测。

Result: 实验表明 ODEWorld 成功调和了利于规划的动力学抽象与视觉真实感，在视频生成与机器人控制任务上均表现优异，长时序预测后仍能高质量重建图像。

Conclusion: 连续时间世界模型能够更高效地捕捉物理世界动力学，ODEWorld 通过 ODE 化的潜空间动力学同时实现视觉真实感与规划友好的抽象，为世界模型提供了一种连续时间范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 ODEWorld，一种基于物理时间流的连续时间潜空间世界模型，用于视频生成与机器人控制，属于视频世界模型范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] video_world_model。同时其连续潜空间/flow 思想与 continuous_language_multimodal 方向相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent world models via ODE-based physical-time flow, aligning strongly with top-priority continuous multimodal and world model directions. Signals: continuous latent world model, ODE flow in physical time, world model for video generation and control, addresses representation collapse, arbitrary temporal resolution and backward prediction

Abstract: In the physical world we inhabit, space and time are fundamentally continuous. However, existing machine learning paradigms for world modeling are largely confined to discrete-time prediction, thereby exhibiting significant inefficiency in capturing the dynamics of physical world. We introduce Physical-Time Flow (\textbf{PT-Flow}), a novel approach that learns a continuous latent velocity field operating in physical time. Crucially, the underlying dynamics of sequential data are parameterized by an ordinary differential equation (ODE) embedded in a well-structured representation space. Under this paradigm, the prediction of future can be recast as temporal integration via an ODE solver in the compressed latent space. Building upon PT-Flow, we construct \textbf{ODEWorld}, a continuous-time latent world model that is both efficient and versatile. By extracting time-variant features and enforcing ODE properties on both the dynamical representation space and the latent velocity field, ODEWorld effectively addresses the long-standing representation collapse issue in latent world model literature. This also enables high-quality image reconstruction even after long-horizon prediction. Moreover, its continuous nature allows for arbitrary temporal resolution and even backward prediction, which is impossible for most discrete-time models. Lastly, ODEWorld can provide rich planning-oriented information to facilitate downstream policy learning. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that ODEWorld successfully reconciles planning-conducive dynamics abstraction with visual realism, excelling in both video generation and robotic control. \href{ }{Project Website}.

</details>


### [21] [ShadowDancer: Teaching Video World Models Any Action by Learning Unified Dynamics Representations from a Video and Its Shadow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28362)
*Jin Cao, Zian Meng, Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ShadowDancer 提出一种任意动作、帧级控制的交互式视频世界模型方法。通过构建"shadow pairs"（同一动力学在不同外观下重放的视频对）并利用 cross-shadow prediction 学习统一动力学表征，使任何演示视频片段都能成为可复用的动作资产，无需动作标签、运动估计器或微调即可在新场景中重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型的接口存在表征障碍：要么对动作编码松散，让模型自行发挥；要么通过结构化信号精确编码但只适用于单一动力学族且难以获取。演示视频虽能逐帧指定任意动力学，但视频只展示动力学的一种外观（"单一影子"），从演示学到的动作难以迁移到新场景。

Method: ShadowDancer 包含两个关键创新：(1) shadow pairs——在独立重采样外观下重放同一动力学的视频对，由 Shadow Library 大规模构建，使动力学族在可构造此类视频对时即可精确控制；(2) cross-shadow prediction——通过从一个影子预测另一个影子来学习动作，配对重采样的内容被构造性丢弃，保留的内容成为动作，从而得到驱动 block-causal 世界模型的统一动力学表征。

Result: 实验表明，ShadowDancer 在多种动力学族上相比强 latent-action 和交互式世界模型基线，实现了更好的动作迁移和长动作 rollout，在 rollout 对比中平均盲评胜率达 86%。

Conclusion: ShadowDancer 证明通过 shadow pairs 与 cross-shadow prediction 可以学习与外观解耦的统一动力学表征，使任意演示片段成为可复用的动作资产，实现无需动作标签的精确帧级控制，为交互式视频世界模型提供了新的表征范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 ShadowDancer，一种任意动作、帧级控制的交互式视频世界模型，通过 shadow pairs 与 cross-shadow prediction 学习统一动力学表征，属于动作条件视频生成与视频世界模型范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] video_world_model。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models with a novel unified dynamics representation and action transfer, strongly matching the user's top-priority world model and continuous representation interests. Signals: world model, video world model, unified dynamics representation, action transfer, frame-level control

Abstract: We present ShadowDancer, a novel approach to any-action, frame-level control of interactive video world models. The obstacle is representational: existing interfaces either encode an action loosely, leaving how it unfolds for the model to improvise, or encode it exactly through structured signals that serve one family and are hard to acquire, so precise control across diverse dynamics remains impractical. Demonstration videos are the natural remedy, specifying any dynamics frame by frame; yet a video shows its dynamics only through one particular appearance, a single shadow of the underlying dynamics, so actions learned from demonstrations transfer poorly to new scenes. ShadowDancer addresses this with two key innovations: (1) shadow pairs, video pairs that replay the same dynamics under independently resampled appearance, constructed at scale by our Shadow Library, so that a dynamics family becomes controllable exactly when such pairs can be constructed for it; and (2) cross-shadow prediction, which learns actions by predicting one shadow from the other, so that whatever the pairing resamples is discarded by construction and whatever it preserves becomes the action, yielding a unified dynamics representation that drives a block-causal world model. Any demonstrated clip thus becomes a reusable action asset, replayed in new environments without action labels, motion estimators, or fine-tuning. Experiments demonstrate improved action transfer and long action rollout over strong latent-action and interactive world model baselines across diverse dynamics families, with an average blinded win rate of 86% in rollout comparisons. We show video results at

</details>


### [22] [PhiZero: A World Model Built Around Physical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28624)
*Shuyao Shang, Yuqi Wang, Ruopeng Gao, Xu Chen, Tieniu Tan, Lue Fan, Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: PhiZero 是一个围绕"物理语言"构建的物理世界模型，通过自监督从野外视频学习紧凑离散的世界状态转移表征，采用 reason-then-render 范式先推理未来演化序列再渲染为视频，实现物理一致的世界演化建模、交互式模拟与动作条件仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理世界模型通常在像素空间直接预测未来视频，将底层世界动力学隐含在高维视觉预测器中，缺乏显式的物理推理结构。作者受人类从视觉经验中抽象预测结构并以自然语言组织显式推理的能力启发，希望引入一种紧凑离散的"物理语言"表征来显式推理物理世界如何演化。

Method: PhiZero 首先通过自监督从野外视频中学习"物理语言"——一种紧凑离散的世界状态转移表征；然后采用 reason-then-render 范式：先推断未来世界演化作为物理语言序列，再将推断出的转移渲染为视频。该方法支持生成与理解基准上的物理一致世界演化建模，并具备交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件仿真与零样本运动迁移能力。

Result: 在生成与理解基准上的大量实验验证了 PhiZero 建模物理一致世界演化的能力，并展示了其在真实交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过将世界动力学抽象为显式的物理语言序列并采用 reason-then-render 范式，PhiZero 能够更物理一致地建模世界演化，为可交互、动作条件的世界模拟提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出 PhiZero 物理世界模型，采用 reason-then-render 范式，先推理未来世界演化的物理语言序列再渲染为视频，属于视频生成式世界模型与动作条件视频生成，映射到 [CANONICAL] video_world_model。

Importance reason: Directly targets the user's top-priority continuous/world-model direction with a novel discrete physical-language reasoning paradigm, despite not using continuous tokens. Signals: world model, reason-then-render, physical language, discrete world-state representation, self-supervised video learning

Abstract: We introduce PhiZero, a physical world model built around physical language, a compact discrete representation of world-state transitions. Existing physical world models typically predict future videos directly in pixel space, leaving the underlying world dynamics implicit within high-dimensional visual predictors. Motivated by humans' ability to abstract predictive structure from visual experience and organize it in natural language for explicit reasoning, we learn physical language from in-the-wild videos through self-supervision and use it to explicitly reason about how the physical world evolves. Accordingly, PhiZero adopts a reason-then-render paradigm: it first infers future world evolution as a physical-language sequence and then renders the inferred transitions into videos. Extensive experiments across generation and understanding benchmarks validate the ability of PhiZero to model physically coherent world evolution. We further show its potential for realistic and interactive world modeling, fine-grained action-conditioned simulation, and zero-shot motion transfer.

</details>

### [23] [ProgFormer: Hierarchical Voxel Diffusion Transformer for Longitudinal Brain MRI Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27537)
*Dexuan Ding, Yuankai Qi, Luping Zhou, Jian Yang, Quan Z. Sheng, Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ProgFormer 是一种层级体素空间 Diffusion Transformer，通过粗-细双路径在体素空间直接估计速度场（conditional flow matching），用于纵向脑 MRI 的未来结构预测，无需单独学习的图像自编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向脑 MRI 预测中，疾病进展变化通常细微且局限于特定解剖区域，而大部分脑结构保持稳定。现有潜空间方法在压缩-重建过程中存在信息损失，而直接体素空间方法使用统一预测路径，细微的局部变化可能被主导的稳定脑结构掩盖。因此需要一种既能保持全局结构一致性又能敏感捕捉细粒度疾病进展的模型。

Method: ProgFormer 采用层级体素空间 Diffusion Transformer 架构，包含两条路径：粗路径（coarse pathway）从 3D patch token 进行主要的体积预测，建模整体脑结构与纵向上下文；细路径（fine pathway）利用粗路径的表征作为时空锚定，在单个 patch 内进行体素级细化。两条路径联合在体素空间通过条件 flow matching 直接估计速度场，实现端到端预测，无需单独学习的图像自编码器。预测的未来扫描通过从高斯噪声出发，沿估计的速度场进行一系列 Euler 步积分生成。

Result: 在 ADNI、AIBL 和 OASIS 三个广泛使用的基准上，在成对（pairwise）和轨迹（trajectory）两种设置下，ProgFormer 相比多种最先进方法取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 层级粗-细双路径设计能够有效平衡全局脑结构一致性与局部疾病进展的敏感性，在体素空间直接进行 flow matching 预测避免了潜空间方法的信息损失，为纵向医学影像预测提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ProgFormer，一种基于体素空间的层级 Diffusion Transformer，用于纵向脑 MRI 预测，属于 3D 体素/体积数据生成任务。这与 [CANONICAL] 的 three_d_generation（3D 生成与重建）方向合理相关，因为其核心是 3D 体积数据的生成式建模与预测。

Importance reason: Medical imaging prediction with negative keyword overlap and no direct alignment with priority continuous/multimodal generation topics. Signals: diffusion transformer, conditional flow matching, voxel-space generation, longitudinal MRI, hierarchical generation

Abstract: Predicting future structural MRI of a brain is challenging because longitudinal changes are often subtle and confined to specific anatomical regions, while most subject-specific brain structure remains stable over time. An effective model should therefore preserve global brain structural consistency while remaining sensitive to fine-grained disease progression. Existing latent-space-based methods improve computational efficiency, but suffer from information loss during their compression-reconstruction procedure. In contrast, direct voxel-space methods avoid latent reconstruction but commonly use a unified prediction pathway to model brain structure and progression-related changes. Subtle local changes may therefore be overshadowed by the dominant stable brain structure. To address these challenges, we propose ProgFormer, a hierarchical voxel-space Diffusion Transformer for longitudinal brain MRI prediction. ProgFormer uses a coarse pathway to perform the primary volumetric prediction from 3D patch tokens. This pathway models overall brain structure and longitudinal context. The fine pathway then uses the coarse representations as spatio-temporal grounding for voxel-level refinement within individual patches. The two pathways jointly estimate a velocity field directly in voxel space through conditional flow matching, enabling end-to-end prediction without a separately learned image autoencoder. The predicted future scan is then generated from Gaussian noise by integrating the estimated velocity field over a sequence of Euler steps. Extensive experimental results on three widely used benchmarks, ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS, under both pairwise and trajectory settings demonstrate favourable performance compared against several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [24] [S-Avatar: Diffusion-Guided Gaussian Head Avatars from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28164)
*Hail Song, Seokhwan Yang, Jiwon Yang, Woojin Cho, Woontack Woo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

TL;DR: S-Avatar 提出一种从单张图像生成逼真 3D 头部头像的方法，通过扩散引导的 3DGS 生成模块与 FLAME 参数模型绑定模板实现动态头像的实时渲染，在未见视角与表情生成上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张图像头部头像重建对 VR 应用至关重要，但现有方法在未见视角下难以保持 3D 一致性，需要更高效且准确的解决方案。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：首先用扩散引导的 Gaussian splat 生成模块从单图合成高分辨率 3DGS；然后通过优化参数与空间变换将 FLAME 参数化头部模型与生成的 3DGS 对齐；最后构建绑定模板编码初始 splat 与 FLAME 的空间关系，通过绑定模板形变 3DGS 实现实时动态渲染。

Result: 在公开数据集上的评估表明，S-Avatar 在新视角与表情生成方面优于现有最先进方法，实现了更高的真实感与一致性。

Conclusion: 结合扩散引导的 canonical 3DGS 生成与 FLAME 控制，S-Avatar 实现了高效准确的头部头像重建，为 VR/AR 应用提供了可访问的头像创建方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出从单张图像生成 3D 头部头像，使用 3D Gaussian Splatting 与扩散引导生成，属于 3D 生成与重建方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] three_d_generation。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D avatar generation, which is outside the user's core continuous multimodal and autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: 3D head avatar, single-image reconstruction, diffusion-guided 3DGS, FLAME binding, real-time rendering

Abstract: We propose S-Avatar, a novel method for generating photorealistic 3D head avatars from a single image using a diffusion-guided 3D model generation module and strategies for animating 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). While single-image head avatar reconstruction is crucial for lifelike Virtual Reality (VR) applications, existing approaches often struggle to preserve 3D consistency under unseen viewpoints. S-Avatar addresses this limitation through a three-stage pipeline. First, a high-resolution 3DGS is synthesized directly from a single image using a diffusion-based Gaussian splat generation module. Next, the parametric head model FLAME is aligned with the generated 3DGS by optimizing its parameters and spatial transformations. Finally, to adapt the 3DGS to FLAME variations, we construct a binding template that encodes the spatial relationship between the initial splats and FLAME. The dynamic 3D head avatar can then be rendered in real time by deforming the 3DGS with the binding template. By combining diffusion-guided canonical 3DGS generation with FLAME-based control, our method achieves efficient and accurate reconstruction with enhanced 3D consistency. Evaluations on public datasets demonstrate that S-Avatar outperforms state-of-the-art methods in novel-view and expression generation, achieving superior realism and consistency. Consequently, our approach represents a significant advance in accessible avatar creation, applicable to a wide range of VR/AR applications. The project page is available at .

</details>

*Xiao Luo, Mingyang Du, Xin Zhou, Tianrui Feng, Xiwu Chen, Xiaofan Li, Jiangning Zhang, Dingkang Liang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真 3D 生成主要依赖扩大模型容量和数据规模，计算成本高昂，且通常需要从零学习几何，忽略了判别式 3D 基础模型已封装的丰富语义与结构先验。作者认为利用这些判别模型对 3D 世界的深刻理解可以显著降低生成成本。

Method: 提出 ROAD 框架，将判别式 3D 基础模型的先验迁移到扩散 Transformer 中。针对生成与判别潜空间之间的语义-结构异质性，引入互惠目标对齐策略，包含两部分：Holistic Semantic Condensing 强制全局语义一致性，以及 Structural Optimal Alignment（形式化为二部图匹配问题）严格对齐不同潜空间之间的微观几何细节。判别式 3D 基础模型仅用于训练时对齐监督，推理时不使用，不增加推理成本。

Result: 与工业基线 Step1X-3D 相比，ROAD 仅用其 1.5% 的训练数据即可达到极具竞争力的生成性能，显著降低训练成本，有效减少高保真 3D 生成的计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过迁移判别式 3D 基础模型的先验知识，可以在不牺牲生成质量的前提下大幅降低 3D 生成的训练成本，为高效高保真 3D 生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出 ROAD 框架，通过将判别式 3D 基础模型的语义先验迁移到扩散 Transformer 中，降低 3D 生成训练成本。这属于 3D 物体生成方法，映射到 [CANONICAL] three_d_generation（3D 生成与重建）子主题。

Importance reason: Efficient 3D generation via discriminative prior transfer is solid but falls outside your core continuous/multimodal/video generation focus. Signals: 3D shape generation, diffusion transformer, training efficiency, discriminative prior transfer, bipartite matching

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D generation predominantly relies on scaling model capacity and data, which incurs prohibitive computational costs. This paradigm typically requires learning geometry from scratch and overlooks the rich semantic and structural priors already encapsulated in discriminative 3D foundation models. We contend that leveraging the profound understanding of the 3D world possessed by these discriminative models can significantly reduce generative cost. To this end, we propose ROAD, a framework that reduces the training cost of 3D generation by transferring these rich discriminative priors into diffusion transformers. To address the inherent semantic-structural heterogeneity between generative and discriminative latents, we introduce a reciprocal-objective alignment strategy. This method synergizes Holistic Semantic Condensing to enforce global semantic coherence and Structural Optimal Alignment, which is formulated as a bipartite matching problem to rigorously align microscopic geometric details between disparate latent spaces. The 3D foundation model is only used for training-time supervision of alignment and is not used at inference, incurring no additional inference cost. Compared with the industrial baseline Step1X-3D, the proposed ROAD achieves highly competitive generation performance with only 1.5% of the training data and significantly reduces training costs, effectively reducing the computational overhead of high-fidelity 3D generation. Code is available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在 EndoNeRF 基础上扩展，引入多分辨率哈希网格编码以同时捕获粗细解剖细节，采用时序特征融合保证组织变形与工具遮挡下的稳定重建，并提出不确定性引导的自适应采样，将更多采样点分配到不确定区域以提升渲染质量与几何一致性。

Result: 在机器人手术视频序列上，相比 EndoNeRF 基线，不确定性引导的自适应采样使 PSNR 提升最高 1.22dB（4.3%），SSIM 提升最高 5.3%，LPIPS 降低最高 55.1%。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的自适应采样与多分辨率哈希编码能有效提升动态手术场景重建的精度与时序一致性，验证了该框架在机器人辅助手术场景重建中的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Endo-NeRF++，基于 NeRF 的动态手术场景神经渲染与重建框架，属于 3D 神经渲染与重建范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] three_d_generation（3D 生成与重建）。

Importance reason: Surgical scene reconstruction is far outside the user's configured focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: neural rendering, dynamic scene reconstruction, uncertainty-aware sampling, multi-resolution hash encoding, surgical video

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic surgical scenes is crucial for robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery; however, it continues to be difficult because of tissue deformation, occlusions, specular reflections, and restricted viewpoints. In this study, we introduce Endo-NeRF++, a neural rendering framework that accounts for uncertainty in the reconstruction of dynamic surgical scenes. Expanding on EndoNeRF, the suggested approach incorporates multi-resolution hash-grid encoding, temporal feature merging, and uncertainty-informed adaptive sampling to enhance reconstruction accuracy and temporal coherence in deformable endoscopic multi-resolution hash-grid representation within the framework effectively captures both coarse and fine anatomical details, while temporal feature blending ensures stable reconstruction during tissue deformation and surgical tool occlusions. Additionally, uncertainty-driven adaptive sampling assigns more samples to uncertain areas to enhance rendering quality and geometric coherence. Experiments on robotic surgical video sequences demonstrate that the proposed uncertainty-guided adaptive sampling improves PSNR by up to 1.22\,dB (4.3\%), increases SSIM by up to 5.3\%, and reduces LPIPS by up to 55.1\% compared with the EndoNeRF baseline.

</details>

*Renlong Wu, Haoran Chen, Yuxiang Wei, Xiaowei Jin, Wangmeng Zuo, Hui Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常先合成单目或多视角视频再拟合 4D 表示，成本高昂且常导致几何不完整或视角不一致的渲染。作者希望直接从文本生成高质量、视角一致、时间连贯的 360 度动态人体资产。

Method: 采用 3D U-Net 骨干网络结合时序注意力进行运动感知生成，直接建模 4DGS 表示空间。构建了包含 60,000 对文本-4DGS 的大规模数据集，并引入 2D 正则化和免训练的 4D 插值以提升渲染质量和运动平滑度。

Result: 4DHumanDiff 能在一分钟内生成一致的 360 度动态人体，在时序和多视角一致性上表现更好，推理时间相比现有方法降低 10 倍以上。

Conclusion: 直接端到端生成 4DGS 表示相比"先视频后重建"的管线更高效且一致性更好，为文本驱动的动态人体资产生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 论文直接从文本生成 4D 高斯泼溅表示的动态人体，属于动态 3D/4D 场景生成，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 dynamic_4d_generation 子主题。

Importance reason: Solid 4D human generation work, but it is a niche application (dynamic humans) rather than core continuous/multimodal modeling or video world models. Signals: 4D Gaussian Splatting, text-to-4D, diffusion framework, 10x faster inference, 360-degree consistency

Abstract: Generating high-quality 360-degree dynamic human assets from text prompts is challenging. Existing methods usually synthesize monocular or multi-view videos first and then fit a 4D representation, which is expensive and often causes incomplete geometry or view-inconsistent renderings. We present 4DHumanDiff, a diffusion framework that directly generates dynamic humans represented by 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) from text prompts. By modeling the structured 4D representation space end-to-end, 4DHumanDiff avoids video pre-generation and per-scene reconstruction, making it better suited for view-consistent and temporally coherent asset generation. The model uses a 3D U-Net backbone with temporal attention for motion-aware generation. We further construct a large-scale text-to-4DGS dataset with 60,000 high-quality pairs, and introduce 2D regularization and training-free 4D interpolation to improve rendering quality and motion smoothness. Experiments show that 4DHumanDiff generates consistent 360-degree dynamic humans within one minute, achieves better temporal and multi-view consistency, and reduces inference time by more than 10x.

</details>

### [28] [VETO: Towards Protecting Images From Frontier AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27292)
*Jonas Grebe, Hossein Shakibania, Tobias Braun, Marcus Rohrbach, Anna Rohrbach*

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: VETO 是一种针对现代图像编辑模型的微妙反编辑防御（anti-edit cloak），通过干扰联合注意力块中模型读取源图像的内部机制来阻止有害编辑；同时引入 VetoBench 基准，不仅评测传统局部编辑还覆盖更广泛的上下文重语境化编辑。在两个当代编辑模型和三个基准上，VETO 一致优于现有防御方法，并提供更强的保护-保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 FLUX.2 等强大图像编辑模型的普及，其能力已从局部修改扩展到在全新场景中提取和重语境化物体与身份。现代模型允许 prompt 和生成 token 直接关注参考图像 token，模糊了传统编辑与文生图合成的边界，扩大了潜在滥用空间。现有反编辑防御仅针对传统扩散管线中参考图像编码的语义瓶颈，而新编辑器通过联合注意力块蒸馏参考信息，往往绕过这些保护。

Method: VETO 是一种微妙的反编辑伪装（cloak），通过干扰现代模型读取源图像的内部机制（联合注意力块）来破坏有害编辑。同时提出 VetoBench 评测基准，不仅评估传统局部编辑，还覆盖更广泛的上下文重语境化编辑场景。

Result: 在两个当代编辑模型和三个基准上，VETO 一致优于现有防御方法，同时提供更强的保护-保真度权衡。

Conclusion: VETO 有效填补了现有反编辑防御在现代联合注意力编辑模型上的空白，为图像编辑安全提供了更鲁棒的防护方案，并借助 VetoBench 建立了更全面的评测框架。

Classification reason: 论文提出 VETO 反编辑防御方法，针对现代图像编辑模型（如 FLUX.2）的联合注意力机制进行干扰，并引入 VetoBench 评测基准。这属于图像编辑领域的安全防御方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）子主题。

Importance reason: Solid but niche defense paper on image editing, far from the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: image editing defense, anti-edit cloak, joint-attention disruption, VetoBench benchmark, FLUX.2

Abstract: The rise of powerful, accessible image-editing models such as FLUX.2 has brought high-fidelity editing within broad reach. Their capabilities now extend beyond localized modifications to extracting and recontextualizing objects and identities in entirely new scenes. By allowing prompt and generation tokens to attend directly to reference-image tokens, modern models blur the boundary between conventional editing and text-to-image synthesis. This expanded generative freedom also broadens the space of potential misuse, as harmful transformations are no longer confined to a predictable set of localized edits. Existing anti-edit defenses are designed to disrupt the semantic bottleneck of the reference-image encoding in legacy diffusion pipelines. However, newer editors distill reference information through joint-attention blocks, thereby often circumventing these protections. We therefore introduce VETO, a subtle anti-edit cloak that disrupts this inner mechanism through which modern models read the source image. Additionally, as existing editing benchmarks leave comprehensive recontextualizations largely untested, we introduce VetoBench, which evaluates defenses not only on conventional localized edits but also on broader contextual shifts. Across two contemporary editing models and three benchmarks, VETO consistently outperforms existing defenses while providing a stronger protection-fidelity trade-off.

</details>

*Chuanzhi Xu, Ziyuan Tao, Jean Julien KNell, Yanrong Chen, Haolan Guo, Xuanhua Yin, Adnan Mahmood, Weidong Cai*

TL;DR: FedPAIE 是一个联邦个性化美学图像增强框架，通过联邦学习在不上传原始照片或评分的情况下，训练轻量级双线索美学评分器并校准为个性化评分器，引导 CLUT 增强器进行用户自适应的色彩分级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化图像增强应反映个体审美偏好，但学习这些偏好通常依赖不适合集中收集的私人照片和评分。现有方法难以在资源受限的用户设备上从稀疏、异构的反馈中推断偏好并将其转化为自然的色彩变换。

Method: FedPAIE 采用联邦学习框架：训练一个轻量级双线索美学评分器，在小规模本地支持集上校准为个性化评分器并冻结，用于引导轻量级 CLUT 增强器从无配对本地照片进行正则化适配。通过保真度约束和 excess-gap 惩罚来正则化评分器引导的适配，限制代理分数过度优化，同时保持内容和自然外观。

Result: 在 MIT-Adobe FiveK 和 Flickr-AES 数据集上验证了有效的开放世界个性化能力，在用户偏好与图像保真度之间取得了良好平衡。训练过程轻量：评分器学习最多更新 0.787M 参数，增强器适配更新 0.265M，推理仅保留 0.293M 参数的个性化增强器。

Conclusion: FedPAIE 将去中心化偏好学习与高效的个性化图像变换连接起来，无需配对用户修图数据即可实现用户自适应的色彩分级，在隐私保护与个性化图像增强之间建立了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究个性化图像增强与色彩分级（color grading），属于图像编辑/增强范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）子主题。

Importance reason: Federated aesthetic image enhancement is peripheral to your core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: federated learning, image enhancement, color grading, lightweight CLUT, personalization

Abstract: Personalized image enhancement should reflect individual aesthetic taste, yet learning such preferences commonly depends on private photos and ratings that are unsuitable for centralized collection. The task must infer preference from sparse, heterogeneous feedback and translate it into natural-looking color transformations on resource-constrained user devices. We introduce FedPAIE, a federated personalized aesthetic image enhancement framework for user-adaptive color grading without centralizing raw photos or ratings. FedPAIE trains a lightweight dual-cue aesthetic scorer, calibrates it into a personalized scorer on a small local support set, and freezes it to guide regularized adaptation of a lightweight CLUT enhancer from unpaired local photographs. Fidelity constraints and an excess-gap penalty regularize scorer-guided adaptation to limit proxy-score over-optimization while preserving content and natural appearance. Training remains lightweight throughout the pipeline: scorer learning updates at most 0.787M parameters, enhancer adaptation updates 0.265M, and inference retains only a 0.293M-parameter personalized enhancer. Experiments on MIT-Adobe FiveK and Flickr-AES demonstrate effective open-world personalization and a favorable balance between user preference and image fidelity. FedPAIE thus connects decentralized preference learning with efficient personalized image transformation without requiring paired user retouches.

</details>

*Chunpeng Wang, Yuxin Li, Xiaoyu Wang, Jidong Yang, Suo Gao, Qi Li*

Result: 在 CelebA 和 ImageNet 上针对四种代表性水印方案的大量实验表明，FDDWAN 在水印去除效果与视觉保真度之间取得了比传统及学习式攻击方法更优的权衡。

Conclusion: 频率解耦与残差扩散精修相结合能有效提升水印去除性能，同时最小化对图像内容的修改，实现更好的水印抑制与视觉质量平衡。

Importance reason: Watermark removal is a niche security topic with no direct alignment to your priority areas of continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: diffusion model, watermark removal, frequency-domain decomposition, image editing, no autoregressive

Abstract: Existing invisible watermark removal methods often struggle to accurately capture the watermark-bearing features, leading to an unfavorable trade-off between watermark suppression and perceptual fidelity. In this paper, we propose the Frequency-Decoupled Diffusion Watermark Attack Network (FDDWAN), a coarse-to-fine framework that performs watermark removal through wavelet-domain decomposition and residual diffusion refinement. In the initial stage, the Wavelet-based Frequency-domain Preliminary Attack Module (WFPAM) decomposes the watermarked image into low- and high-frequency subbands and applies frequency-specific attack strategies tailored to their respective contributions to watermark robustness and perceptual quality. In the next stage, the Frequency-domain Residual Diffusion Attack Module (FRDAM) separately models the residual distributions between the preliminarily attacked outputs and the corresponding watermark-free references during training. Rather than reconstructing the entire image, FRDAM selectively refines frequency-domain residuals, directing the diffusion process toward the remaining watermark related discrepancies while minimizing modifications to image content. Extensive experiments on CelebA and ImageNet across four representative watermarking schemes demonstrate that FDDWAN achieves a more favorable trade-off between watermark removal effectiveness and visual fidelity than conventional and learning-based attack methods.

</details>


### [31] [Now You Have My Healthy Attention: A U-DiT for Brain-MRI Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27974)
*Danilo Danese, Angela Lombardi, Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于 U-DiT 的确定性回归模型用于脑部 MRI Inpainting，通过约束注意力使掩膜 token 仅关注同体积内已知健康 token，并引入对侧对称先验，在 BraTS-2026 验证集上达到 SSIM 0.864、PSNR 24.7dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BraTS 局部合成（Inpainting）任务要求在 T1 加权 MRI 的掩膜区域内完成解剖学上合理的健康脑组织补全，为下游分析提供无肿瘤的解剖参考。由于任务以失真指标（SSIM、PSNR、MSE）评分，需要构建具有针对 Inpainting 归纳偏置的确定性回归模型。

Method: 采用 U-DiT 架构：体素编码器-解码器通过下采样全局自注意力块（带三维旋转位置编码）引入长程上下文，卷积与跳跃连接保留高频细节。两个关键设计：(1) 约束注意力使掩膜（void）token 仅关注同体积内已知健康 token，并加入朝向对侧同源区域的偏置，强制补全从观测解剖推断；(2) 加入对侧对称输入作为患者特定先验，利用大脑近似双侧对称且病灶通常单侧的特性。

Result: 在官方 BraTS-2026 验证排行榜上，219 例病例达到平均健康区域 SSIM 0.864、PSNR 24.7dB、MSE 4.6×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 通过注意力约束与对侧对称先验，确定性回归模型能在失真指标上取得良好 Inpainting 效果；同时分析了失真最优回归固有的残差平滑性及其对解剖真实性的影响。

Classification reason: 论文研究脑部 MRI 图像 Inpainting（掩膜区域健康组织补全），属于图像编辑/修复范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）子主题。

Importance reason: 脑部MRI修复任务与用户配置的连续多模态、视频/图像生成及世界模型方向关联度低，且含负面关键词“medical diagnosis”。 Signals: 医学图像修复, U-DiT, 注意力约束, 对侧对称先验, 确定性回归

Abstract: The ASNR-MICCAI BraTS Local Synthesis (Inpainting) task asks for the anatomically plausible completion of healthy brain tissue within a masked region of a T1-weighted MRI, providing a tumor-free anatomical reference for downstream analysis. As the task is scored by distortion metrics (SSIM, PSNR, MSE), we build a deterministic regression model and focus on giving it inductive biases tailored to inpainting. Our network follows the U-DiT principle of performing self-attention on a downsampled token grid: a volumetric encoder-decoder imports long-range context through a downsampled global self-attention block with three-dimensional rotary position embeddings, while convolutions and skip connections preserve high-frequency detail. Two ideas drive our results. First, we constrain the attention so that occluded ("void") tokens attend only to known-healthy tokens of the same volume, with a learned bias toward each query's contralateral homologue, forcing the completion to be inferred from observed anatomy rather than from other unknown regions. Second, we add a contralateral-symmetry input that supplies the mirrored healthy hemisphere as a patient-specific prior; since the brain is approximately bilaterally symmetric and lesions are typically unilateral, this prior improves the distortion metrics at matched structural similarity. On the official BraTS-2026 validation leaderboard our submission reaches a mean healthy-region SSIM of $0.864$, PSNR of $24.7$\,dB and MSE of $4.6{\times}10^{-3}$ over $219$ cases. We further analyse the residual smoothness inherent to distortion-optimal regression and discuss its implications for anatomical realism.

</details>
