### [32] [Bunraku: Turning a Single Illustration into an Editable Live2D Character](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27348)
*Junhao Chen, Jingjia Mao, Dayong Li, Chenghai Li, Saining Zhang, Zhihao Li, Hao Zhao, Yufei Wang, Ruqi Huang*

TL;DR: Bunraku 是首个从单张插画端到端生成完整 Live2D 结构化资产（有序 RGBA 图层、逐层变形网格、参数驱动的关键帧顶点偏移）的系统，并贡献了 Live2D-Bench 基准与 8884 个模型的语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Live2D 是动漫角色与虚拟主播的主流 2D 角色动画格式，但手工制作一个 Live2D 模型需要数周的图层分离、遮挡补全、网格放置与关键帧制作，且此前没有任何生成方法能端到端产出这种结构化资产。

Method: 两阶段方法：阶段一将图层分解建模为 Live2D 感知的器官级分类下的分层扩散过程，生成带隐藏区域补全的有序 RGBA 图层栈；阶段二仅从 alpha 通道为每层构建内容一致的三角网格，并联合预测所有图层的关键帧位移场——每层每个顶点作为一个 token，自注意力跨越图层边界，每个位移分解为有界方向与对数幅度。

Result: 在 50 个保留角色上，无教师强制的真实生成下，阶段二达到逐顶点方向余弦 0.768（中位数 0.828）。联合预测相比独立预测是最大增益来源，将网络规模扩大 112 倍无增益。服装层可依据自然语言指令重新贴图，同时网格与预测动画逐字节复用。

Conclusion: Bunraku 首次实现了从单张插画到完整可编辑 Live2D 角色的端到端生成，联合跨图层预测是生成连贯角色的关键，并提供了首个标准化基准 Live2D-Bench 与大规模语料库。

Classification reason: 论文从单张插画生成可编辑的 Live2D 角色，涉及虚拟人/角色动画资产生成，属于数字人与动作生成范畴，映射到 CANONICAL 子主题 avatar_motion_generation。

Importance reason: Novel structured-asset generation for Live2D avatars, but tangential to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive/diffusion video generation, and world models. Signals: single-illustration to Live2D, layered diffusion decomposition, joint mesh/keypose prediction, Live2D-Bench benchmark, 8884-model corpus

Abstract: Live2D is the dominant 2D character-animation format for anime characters and virtual avatars, representing each character as a stack of RGBA layers driven by per-layer mesh deformation. Despite its wide use in virtual streaming, mobile games, and interactive characters, authoring a Live2D model still demands weeks of manual layer separation, occlusion completion, mesh placement, and keyframing, and no prior generative method produces such a structured asset end-to-end. We present the first system that, from a single illustration, generates all the structured information a Live2D runtime consumes: ordered RGBA layers, a deformation mesh per layer, and the parameter-driven keypose vertex offsets that make the character move. Stage 1 casts layered decomposition as a layered diffusion process under a Live2D-aware organ-level taxonomy, producing an ordered RGBA stack with hidden-region completion. Stage 2 builds a content-conforming triangle mesh for each layer from its alpha channel alone, then predicts the keypose displacement field of all layers jointly: every vertex of every layer is one token, self-attention spans layer boundaries, and each displacement is factorised into a bounded direction and a log-magnitude. Joint rather than independent prediction is what makes the result a coherent character instead of separately plausible parts, and is our largest gain; scaling the network 112x yields none. On 50 held-out characters, under true generation with no teacher forcing, Stage 2 attains a per-vertex direction cosine of 0.768 (median 0.828). Because a layer's mesh derives from its alpha channel, a clothing layer can be re-textured from a natural-language instruction while the mesh and predicted animation are reused byte-for-byte. We further contribute Live2D-Bench, the first standardized benchmark for the task, and an 8,884-model Live2D corpus with layer and animation supervision.

</details>

TL;DR: EgoGVAE 提出一种基于引导变分自编码器（Guided VAE）的方法，仅从头部姿态轨迹重建全身人体网格。通过将引导网络（以全身姿态为输入）与头部到运动网络的潜空间分布对齐，实现一步采样快速推理，比扩散方法快50倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从仅有的头部姿态恢复全身人体网格是头戴设备与智能眼镜应用的关键任务，但难点在于仅凭单一关节轨迹估计未观测身体部位的姿态。现有基于扩散的头部条件生成模型虽然有效，但迭代过程成本高、耗时长，需要更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出 EgoGVAE，利用引导网络的潜空间。引导网络是一个以全身姿态为输入的变分自编码器（VAE），通过强制该引导网络与头部到运动网络（head-to-motion network）的潜分布相似，使从引导分布采样的潜特征即使仅输入头部姿态也能可靠解码为自然的全身姿态表示。采用一步采样方案实现快速推理。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，所提方法有效提升了 ego-body mesh 重建性能，且一步采样方案相比扩散方法推理速度快50倍以上。

Conclusion: EgoGVAE 通过引导 VAE 的潜空间对齐，在保持重建质量的同时大幅提升推理效率，为头部条件全身姿态重建提供了一种高效且实用的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从头部姿态重建全身人体网格（ego-body mesh reconstruction），属于人体动作/姿态生成与重建范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 数字人与动作生成（avatar_motion_generation）高度相关，故复用该子主题。

Abstract: We address the problem of recovering the full-body mesh from only the head pose. This task has become essential for various applications based on head-mounted devices or smart glasses. The challenge of this task lies in estimating the pose information of unobserved body parts based solely on a single joint (i.e., head) trajectory. Several studies have begun to adopt head-conditioned generative models, however, such previous methods are costly and time-consuming due to the diffusion-based iterative process. As an alternative, we propose a simple yet novel method that leverages the latent space of the guidance network, which is designed as a variational autoencoder taking full-body poses as inputs. By enforcing latent distributions of this guidance network and our head-to-motion network to be similar, latent features sampled from the 'guided' distribution, i.e., distribution learned in our head-to-motion network, can be reliably decoded for natural representations of full-body poses even only with the head pose. One important advantage of the proposed method is that one-step sampling scheme achieves remarkably fast inference (more than 50 times faster) compared to diffusion-based approaches. Experimental results on benchmark datasets show that the proposed method efficiently improves the performance of ego-body mesh reconstruction.

</details>


### [34] [Split and Drive: Dual-Axis Disentanglement for Real-Time Gaussian Head Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28032)

Method: 提出双轴解耦框架：计算轴将逐帧驱动内化到模型中，消除推理时对外部跟踪的依赖；特征轴将头像分解为三个专门的高斯分支，分别建模几何上不同的面部区域。

Result: 在多个数据集上相对 SOTA 方法表现稳定，且在单 GPU 上包含完整驱动管线的情况下达到所有对比方法中最快的推理速度。

Conclusion: 通过双轴解耦设计，SpiD 在保持高质量渲染的同时显著提升推理效率，为实时数字人头像合成提供了更实用的方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出单图驱动的 3D 高斯头部头像（Gaussian Head Avatar）框架，属于数字人/头像生成与面部动画领域，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"数字人与动作生成"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Solid real-time Gaussian head avatar work, but it is outside the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus. Signals: real-time inference, 3D Gaussian Splatting, single-image avatar, disentangled representation, digital human synthesis

Abstract: Creating photorealistic animatable head avatars from a single image remains a fundamental challenge in digital human synthesis. While recent 3D Gaussian Splatting methods have achieved promising results, they rely on external tracking pipelines whose latency is excluded from inference measurements. Furthermore, they adopt unified representations that entangle geometrically distinct facial regions, limiting both expressiveness and rendering fidelity. We propose SpiD (Split and Drive), a single-image Gaussian head avatar framework built on two disentanglement axes. The compute axis internalizes per-frame driving, eliminating external tracking dependency at inference. The feature axis decomposes the avatar into three specialized Gaussian branches, each modeling a geometrically distinct facial domain. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistently strong performance against state-of-the-art methods while achieving the fastest inference speed among all compared methods on a single GPU with the complete driving pipeline included.

</details>

Abstract: Modern face reenactment systems achieve impressive pose and expression transfer using geometry-driven representations. However, they largely ignore tongue dynamics, leading to anatomically inconsistent mouth interiors during speech and expressive motions. We introduce the first framework for cross-identity tongue dynamics transfer in face reenactment. We propose a foundation-model-assisted bootstrapping pipeline that produces a dedicated tongue segmentation model for in-the-wild reenactment without curated annotations. We further introduce a spatially constrained latent masked diffusion model for realistic tongue synthesis, with adaptive mask dilation for seamless mouth boundary transitions. Extensive experiments demonstrate improvements of more than two times over all baselines on every tongue-specific metric. We additionally propose a VLM-based evaluation protocol that replicates expert annotation at scale, confirming perceptual superiority across all ablation variants.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: MIND 使用 BioMedGPT 从源图像生成意图驱动的融合文本以引导融合过程；设计多尺度潜空间适配器在序列化前显式提取源图像特征并通过严格维度对齐注入网络，弥补 DiT 中 1D 序列展平导致的 2D 空间连续性损失；设计医学语义一致性损失，确保融合图像与融合文本之间的深层语义锁定，同时维持底层物理流形重建的稳定性。

Result: 在 Harvard、BraTS 和 GFP 数据集上的综合实验表明，MIND 实现了更优的融合质量，显著提升了下游脑肿瘤分割精度，并支持灵活的交互式融合。

Conclusion: MIND 展示了意图驱动的医学图像融合在智能临床决策支持系统中的潜力，通过将诊断意图融入扩散融合过程，实现了更符合临床需求的融合结果。

Classification reason: 论文提出基于 Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) 的医学图像融合方法，属于图像扩散/DiT 生成方法，映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生图与图像扩散 子主题。

Importance reason: Medical image fusion with diffusion transformers is methodologically interesting but falls outside your core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: diffusion transformer, medical image fusion, intent-driven generation, semantic consistency loss, BioMedGPT

Abstract: Medical image fusion aims to integrate complementary information from diverse imaging modalities to support clinical diagnosis. Existing methods typically apply uniform fusion rules globally, lacking a deep understanding of diagnostic intents and pathological structures. To address these limitations, we propose MIND, a Multimodal Intent-Driven Network via Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for medical image fusion. Specifically, we utilize BioMedGPT to generate intent-driven fusion texts from source images, guiding the fusion process with pathology-aware diagnostic intents. To combat the loss of 2D spatial continuity caused by 1D sequence flattening in DiTs, we design a Multi-scale Latent Adapter. This module explicitly extracts source image features before serialization, injecting them into the network via strict dimensional alignment to effectively supplement image features. To resolve the semantic shift caused by decoupling image outputs from diagnostic intents, we design a medical semantic consistency loss. This loss ensures deep semantic locking between fused images and fusion texts while maintaining the stability of the underlying physical manifold reconstruction. Comprehensive experiments on the Harvard, BraTS, and GFP datasets reveal that MIND delivers superior fusion quality, significantly improves downstream brain tumor segmentation accuracy, and enables flexible interactive fusion, holding significant promise for intent-driven intelligent clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [37] [Chimera: Designing and Chinchilla-Scaling Hybrid Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28611)
*Chongjian Ge, Hanwen Jiang, Tianyu Wang, Jiuxiang Gu, Yiran Xu, Ziwen Chen, Shaoteng Liu, Jing Shi, Yicong Hong, Zefan Cai, Hailin Jin, Hao Tan*

Subtopic: 文生图与图像扩散

Importance: 值得关注 (69.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Chimera 是一种混合视觉扩散 Transformer 骨干，结合 KDA 长上下文注意力、交错 MLA 全局交互与模态感知短卷积，并引入 HeteroP 模块级缩放方案，拟合 Chinchilla 风格计算最优定律，训练出 11B 参数（2B 激活）的模型，在扩散损失上比全注意力 Wan-2.1 2B 基线高效 7.3 倍，且可零样本外推到 30 秒视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成日益需要高分辨率图像、长视频与多模态上下文，而全注意力的二次方计算成本成为瓶颈。现有扩散架构缺乏针对长上下文的高效设计原则与可扩展的缩放配方。

Method: Chimera 将文本、图像、视频 token 以光栅顺序处理为单一流，无需位置嵌入。它组合了 Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) 用于 O(N) 复杂度的长上下文状态跟踪、交错的 Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) 用于全局交互、模态感知短卷积用于局部时空上下文，以及稀疏 MoE 层扩展容量。同时提出 HeteroP 模块级缩放方案，根据每个张量的功能 fan-in 与模型深度跨宽度和深度迁移超参数，用于拟合 Chinchilla 风格的计算最优定律。

Result: 预训练扩散损失上，稠密骨干比匹配的全注意力 Wan-2.1 2B 基线计算高效 1.7 倍，完整系统达 7.3 倍。无需长度特定微调即可从 5 秒训练片段零样本外推到 30 秒视频，最后 5 秒仅 6.5% FID 退化。拟合定律显示图像预训练在激活模型规模与训练 token 数间近乎均分计算，而视频预训练在高预算下略偏向模型规模。

Conclusion: Chimera 建立了高效长上下文扩散架构设计与缩放的基础，验证了混合注意力与模块级缩放方案在视觉生成中的有效性，为未来扩散模型的高效扩展提供了原则性指导。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Chimera，一种混合视觉扩散 Transformer 骨干架构，用于高分辨率图像、长视频与多模态上下文生成，属于扩散模型架构设计。映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生图与图像扩散（text_to_image_diffusion），该主题涵盖扩散模型与 DiT 等基础生成架构。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous visual latent flow and efficient diffusion transformer scaling, aligning with top-priority continuous multimodal and video generation directions. Signals: hybrid visual diffusion transformer, KDA long-context attention, interleaved MLA, modality-aware short convolution, HeteroP module-wise scaling

Abstract: Visual generation increasingly requires high-resolution images, long videos, and multimodal context, making the quadratic cost of full attention prohibitive. We introduce Chimera, a hybrid visual diffusion backbone with a principled scaling recipe. Chimera processes text, image, and video tokens in one raster-ordered stream without positional embeddings. It combines Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) for long-context state tracking with O(N) complexity, interleaved Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) for direct global interaction, and modality-aware short convolutions for local spatiotemporal context. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers expand capacity while controlling activated compute. To scale this heterogeneous architecture, we introduce HeteroP, a module-wise scheme that transfers hyperparameters across width and depth according to each tensor's functional fan-in and model depth. HeteroP yields a consistently tuned family used to fit Chinchilla-style compute-optimal laws for activated model size, training-token count, and image-video data ratio. Guided by these laws, we train an 11B-parameter Chimera with 2B activated parameters. Experiments show three results. First, measured by pretraining diffusion loss, the dense backbone is 1.7x as compute-efficient as a matched full-attention Wan-2.1 2B baseline, while the complete system reaches 7.3x. Second, without length-specific fine-tuning, Chimera extrapolates zero-shot from 5-second training clips to 30-second videos, with only 6.5% FID degradation in the last five seconds. Third, the fitted laws show that compute-optimal image pretraining divides compute nearly evenly between activated model size and training-token count, whereas video pretraining modestly favors model size at higher budgets. These results establish a foundation for designing and scaling efficient long-context diffusion architectures.

</details>

*Haodong Li, Tianfei Ren, Xiaoxiao Ma, Chunmei Qing, Zhen Fang, Sipeng He, Ziyu Guo, Haoyu Wu, Juanxi Tian, Yihang Zou, Ruichuan An, Dongzhi Jiang, Boxue Yang, Ji Xie, Xu Huang, Wenhao Yan, Jialv Zou, Zhengrong Yue, Yaxin Luo, Xiaotong Li, Yuzhu Wang, Junyan Ye, Jinjing Zhao, Zehui Chen, Lin Chen, Renye Yan, Feng Zhao, Pheng-Ann Heng*

TL;DR: VideoCoCo 提出一个智能体双引擎框架，用可执行的 Blender 代码作为过程级思维链（Code-as-CoT），将文本提示转化为显式描述场景及其时间演化的 Blender 程序，由模拟引擎生成确定性时空草稿，再经生成式视频引擎进行草稿条件编辑得到逼真视频，从而提升文生视频的物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型虽视觉质量高，但难以生成物理一致的动态，因为场景的时间演化需从高度压缩的文本提示中隐式推断。已有的思维链方法引入中间计划或视觉状态，但这些表示通常不可执行或时间上稀疏，难以实例化和控制完整的时空过程。

Method: VideoCoCo 采用智能体双引擎架构：编码智能体根据文本提示合成一个显式指定场景及其时间演化的 Blender 程序；可执行模拟引擎运行该程序产生确定性的时空草稿；生成式视频引擎通过草稿条件编辑将草稿转化为逼真视频。该分解将过程级推理与高保真视觉实现分离。为适配视频编辑器，构建了 VideoCoCo-3K 数据集，包含草稿-指令-目标三元组。

Result: VideoCoCo 将 OmniWeaving 基线在 PhyGenBench 上从 0.475 提升到 0.558，在 VBench-2.0 上从 52.18 提升到 77.88，在两个基准上均取得最佳平均分。

Conclusion: 实验表明，可执行代码为物理一致的视频生成提供了一种有效、可控且可检查的中间表示，将过程级推理与高保真视觉实现分离能够显著提升物理一致性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 VideoCoCo，通过可执行 Blender 代码作为过程级思维链，结合生成式视频引擎实现物理一致的文生视频。核心属于文生视频生成方法，映射到 [CANONICAL] text_to_video_diffusion；同时涉及物理一致性模拟，也匹配 world_model 方向的 video_world_model。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with priority on video generation world models and physically consistent video via executable code-as-CoT, though not directly on continuous latent models. Signals: video generation, world model, controllable generation, code-as-CoT, agentic dual-engine

Abstract: Text-to-video models have achieved remarkable visual quality, yet they still struggle to generate physically consistent dynamics because the temporal evolution of a scene must be inferred implicitly from a highly compressed text prompt. Existing chain-of-thought approaches introduce intermediate plans or visual states, but these representations are typically non-executable or temporally sparse, limiting their ability to instantiate and control the complete spatiotemporal process. To address this limitation, we introduce VideoCoCo, an agentic dual-engine framework in which executable Blender code serves as a process-level chain of thought. Given a text prompt, a coding agent synthesizes a Blender program that explicitly specifies the scene and its temporal evolution. The executable simulation engine runs the program to produce a deterministic spatiotemporal draft, which is subsequently transformed into a photorealistic video by a generative video engine through draft-conditioned editing. This decomposition separates process-level reasoning from high-fidelity visual realization. To adapt the video editor to simulated drafts, we construct VideoCoCo-3K, a curated dataset of draft-instruction-target triplets. VideoCoCo improves the OmniWeaving baseline from 0.475 to 0.558 on PhyGenBench and from 52.18 to 77.88 on VBench-2.0, achieving the best average score on both benchmarks. These results demonstrate that executable code provides an effective, controllable, and inspectable intermediate representation for physically consistent video generation.

</details>

*Jiwen Liu, Shujuan Li, Xiaohan Li, Zijie Meng, Xinyue Liu, Yulong Xu, Yan Zhou, Guoxin Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频重拍方法依赖显式 3D 先验或成对的相机轨迹视频，前者受重建质量限制且难以合成未见区域，后者数据稀缺阻碍泛化。作者希望摆脱这些限制，通过文本驱动的语义视角指定实现可控相机运动与视角的视频重拍。

Method: TARS 首先进行时间步敏感度分析，发现相机运动主要在高噪声阶段建立（此时形成粗粒度时空结构）。基于此洞察，采用自监督训练学习相机动力学与基础视觉表征，无需成对重拍数据或 3D 重建。通过数据缩放与文本-相机联合条件，支持鲁棒的相机与视角控制，可在大相机运动下合理合成源视角之外的区域，并支持反向角度重拍与视角切换。

Result: 大量实验表明 TARS 相比先前方法提供更准确且时间一致的相机控制，能够在大相机运动下合理合成未见区域，并支持反向角度重拍与第一/第三人称视角切换。

Conclusion: TARS 验证了无需 3D 先验或成对数据即可实现可控视频重拍的可行性，通过时间步敏感度洞察与自监督训练，在相机控制准确性和时间一致性上优于现有方法。

Classification reason: 论文提出 TARS，一种 3D-free 视频重拍（video re-shooting）方法，通过文本驱动的语义视角控制与扩散模型实现相机运动可控的视频生成。这属于视频生成/视频扩散范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题。

Importance reason: 该论文聚焦视频重拍与相机控制，虽涉及视频生成，但未触及用户高优先级的连续语言/多模态模型或世界模型方向。 Signals: 视频重拍, 相机控制, 时间步敏感度, 自监督训练, 文本-相机联合条件

Abstract: Video re-shooting aims to regenerate videos with controllable camera motion and viewpoint. Existing methods rely on explicit 3D priors, which are limited by reconstruction quality and often perform poorly when synthesizing previously unseen regions, or on paired videos with different camera trajectories, whose scarcity hinders generalization. We revisit video re-shooting through text-driven semantic viewpoint specification, enabling control over shot scale, viewing angle, and first-/third-person perspective. To this end, we propose TARS, a 3D-free video re-shooting paradigm. Timestep-wise sensitivity analysis reveals that camera motion is primarily established during high-noise stages, where coarse spatiotemporal structures are formed. Based on this insight, we introduce self-supervised training to learn camera dynamics and fundamental visual representations without paired re-shooting data or 3D reconstruction. Through data scaling and joint textual-camera conditioning, TARS supports robust camera and viewpoint control, plausibly synthesizing regions beyond the source view under large camera motions while enabling reverse-angle re-shooting and perspective switching. Extensive experiments show that TARS provides more accurate and temporally consistent camera control than prior methods. Project Page:

### [40] [SE(3)-MeanFlow: Few-Step Protein Backbone Generation on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27431)
*Yikun Bai, Binghang Lu, Yikai Liu, Elaheh Akbari, Soheil Kolouri, Linxuan Wang, Ping He, Shuchan Wang, Ruqi Zhang, Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (38.27) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SE(3)-MeanFlow 将 MeanFlow 从欧氏空间扩展到 SE(3) 李群几何，用于蛋白质骨架的少步生成。它原生在 so(3) 李代数和 R^3 中工作，推导出旋转和平移的闭式平均速度恒等式，提供无模拟训练目标，并引入 SE(3) alpha-Flow 目标作为预热阶段，随后切换到小 t 稳定的 MeanFlow 损失用于预训练和 rectification 后训练。在蛋白质骨架生成中，SE(3)-MeanFlow 以更少的采样步数匹配或超越 flow-matching 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散和 flow-matching 模型能在 SE(3)^N 上生成高质量蛋白质骨架，但推理需要对 ODE 进行数百次网络评估的数值积分，每次涉及李群指数映射，成为高通量设计流程的瓶颈。作者希望实现少步生成以加速蛋白质骨架生成。

Method: 将 MeanFlow 从欧氏空间扩展到 SE(3) 李群几何，原生在 so(3) 李代数和 R^3 中工作。推导旋转和平移的闭式平均速度恒等式，得到无模拟训练目标。引入 SE(3) alpha-Flow 目标，去除旋转分支的 Jacobian-vector 乘积并作为预热阶段，之后切换到小 t 稳定的 MeanFlow 损失用于预训练和 rectification 后训练。

Result: 在蛋白质骨架生成中，SE(3)-MeanFlow 以比 flow-matching 基线少数倍的采样步数匹配或超越其性能，在少步区间优势更明显，rectification 使其在每个匹配预算下都领先，代价是多样性略有下降。

Conclusion: SE(3)-MeanFlow 证明了将 MeanFlow 扩展到李群几何可实现蛋白质骨架的少步高效生成，为高通量蛋白质设计提供了更快的生成方案，同时保持与多步 flow-matching 相当的质量。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SE(3)-MeanFlow，一种少步（few-step）蛋白质骨架生成框架，通过将 MeanFlow 扩展到李群几何并引入 rectification 后训练，显著减少采样步数。这属于生成模型的少步采样与加速优化，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 efficient_generation（生成模型加速与高效化）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on protein backbone generation, which is outside the user's configured priority areas and matches a negative keyword. Signals: few-step generation, SE(3) Lie group, flow matching, protein backbone, negative keyword: protein

Abstract: Generative modeling of protein backbones promises the de novo design of proteins with prescribed structural and functional properties. Existing diffusion and flow-matching models produce high-quality backbones on SE(3)^N, but inference requires numerically integrating an ODE over hundreds of network evaluations, each involving a Lie group exponential map - a bottleneck for high-throughput design campaigns. We introduce SE(3)-MeanFlow, a few-step generative framework that extends MeanFlow from Euclidean space to the Lie group geometry of protein frames. Working natively in the Lie algebra so(3) and in R^3, we derive closed-form average-velocity identities for rotations and translations, giving simulation-free training targets. We further introduce an SE(3) alpha-Flow objective that removes the Jacobian-vector product from the rotation branch and serves as a warm-up stage, after which training switches to a small-t stabilized MeanFlow loss that is used for the remainder of pretraining and for rectification-based post-training. In protein backbone generation, SE(3)-MeanFlow matches or exceeds flow-matching baselines that use several times more sampling steps, and its advantage widens in the few-step regime, where rectification lets it lead at every matched budget - at a modest cost in diversity.

</details>


### [41] [ReGenVC: End-to-End Real-Time Generative Video Coding at Ultra-Low Bitrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28144)
*Zheyuan Zhang, Johnson Wu*

Main category: cs.CV, eess.IV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 普通相关 (60.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReGenVC 是一个端到端生成式视频编解码器，将说话头视频压缩到超低码率（77帧约26kB），并通过四步蒸馏扩散Transformer及八GPU并行、空间分割VAE、三级重叠流水线等系统技术实现实时解码（24fps），是首个结合超低码率编码与实时解码的生成式视频编解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频编解码器（如x264/x265）在超低码率下会产生严重的块状伪影，而生成式编解码器虽能利用生成先验保持清晰度，但多步采样带来的解码延迟使其无法用于交互式实时场景。作者旨在解决生成式视频编解码器部署的核心障碍——解码器延迟问题。

Method: 编码器将源片段压缩为紧凑比特流（神经压缩的首帧、逐帧姿态关键点及元数据，约26kB）。解码器采用四步蒸馏扩散Transformer，基于传输的姿态和参考帧重建视频。为达到实时性，采用三项系统技术：八GPU统一序列并行（Ulysses与Ring）、空间分割VAE、三级重叠流水线，并建立解析时序模型刻画实时可行性区域。混合CPU-GPU部署将编码器放在CPU上运行，并将解码端一次性条件编码器卸载到CPU以降低GPU内存峰值。

Result: 在8-GPU节点上系统维持24fps输出（25帧窗口972ms，在1000ms预算内），实现无帧欠载的实时浏览器流。相比x264/x265，码率降至约十分之一（26kB vs 250-280kB）且重建基本无伪影；在匹配的超低码率下传统编解码器崩溃为块状伪影而ReGenVC保持清晰。混合CPU-GPU部署将每GPU内存峰值从21.1GB降至约7.7GB。

Conclusion: ReGenVC 是首个将超低码率编码与实时解码相结合的端到端生成式视频编解码器，证明了通过蒸馏与系统级并行优化，生成式先验驱动的视频编解码可以在交互式实时场景中实用化。

Classification reason: 该论文提出端到端生成式视频编解码器，核心贡献在于通过四步蒸馏扩散Transformer和系统级并行/流水线技术实现实时解码，属于生成模型加速与高效化方向，映射到[CANONICAL] efficient_generation。

Importance reason: 该论文聚焦生成式视频编解码与系统加速，虽涉及扩散Transformer和高效推理，但核心应用（视频压缩）与用户优先方向（连续多模态、世界模型、生成）关联较弱。 Signals: 扩散Transformer, 四步蒸馏, 高效推理, 视频生成, 系统并行加速

Abstract: We present ReGenVC, an end-to-end generative video codec that compresses talking-head video to an ultra-low bitrate and decodes it in real time. The encoder reduces a source clip to a compact bitstream -- a neurally compressed first frame, per-frame pose keypoints, and metadata -- totaling about 26 kB for a 77-frame sequence. The decoder is a four-step distilled diffusion transformer that reconstructs the video conditioned on the transmitted pose and reference frame. Compared with x264/x265, ReGenVC reduces the bitrate to roughly one tenth of that required by traditional codecs (about 26 kB vs. 250--280 kB for essentially artifact-free reconstruction); at a matched ultra-low bitrate, conventional codecs collapse into blocking artifacts while ReGenVC stays sharp by exploiting a strong generative prior. The central obstacle to deploying such a codec is decoder latency: multi-step sampling with transformer and VAE components is too slow for interactive use. We make the decoder real-time through four-step distillation and three model-preserving system techniques: (i) eight-GPU unified sequence parallelism (Ulysses & Ring), (ii) a spatially-split VAE, and (iii) a three-stage overlapped pipeline; an analytical timing model characterizes the real-time feasibility region. On an 8-GPU node, the system sustains 24 fps output (972 ms per 25-frame window, within the 1000 ms budget), enabling a live browser stream without observed frame underruns. A hybrid CPU-GPU deployment further runs the encoder on the CPU at 24 fps and offloads the decoder-side one-shot conditioning encoders to the CPU, reducing the per-GPU memory peak from 21.1 GB to about 7.7 GB. To our knowledge, ReGenVC is the first end-to-end generative video codec to combine ultra-low-bitrate encoding with real-time decoding on an 8-GPU system.

</details>

*Alexi Gladstone, Heng Ji, Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

TL;DR: 本文提出 Explorative Modeling（XM）范式，通过分解训练循环而非生成过程，探索 K 个候选匹配并训练最优匹配，使预测承诺于模式而非模糊化，从而解锁端到端生成。该方法在图像、视频、语言等连续与离散域上作为新的预训练轴，随探索规模单调提升性能，并可作为独立的端到端重建生成范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽能力强大，但从未真正端到端训练——因为生成建模本质是处理多模态分布，现有可扩展方法都通过分解生成过程来处理，这阻碍了端到端生成。作者希望引入一种新的范式，通过分解训练循环而非生成过程来实现端到端生成。

Method: 提出 Explorative Modeling：在训练循环中探索 K 个候选匹配（模型生成与数据之间的匹配），并训练最优匹配，使预测承诺于模式而非模糊化。该方法作为新的预训练轴（探索轴），可与参数和数据规模并行扩展，并可作为独立的端到端重建生成范式。

Result: 探索规模随数据扩展从 7% 提升到 36%，随模型增长从 13% 提升到 23%；FLOP 效率提升 4.1 倍、样本效率提升 6.2 倍、参数效率提升 47%；在 ImageNet 上无需 guidance 达到接近 SOTA 的 1.43 FID；在控制任务上以 16-256 倍更少的推理步数匹配扩散模型。

Conclusion: Explorative Models 既可作为现有生成模型的新预训练轴，也可作为独立的端到端生成建模范式，在连续与离散域上均展现出显著优势，为生成模型训练提供了新的扩展维度。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Explorative Modeling 范式，通过探索 K 个候选匹配并训练最优匹配来改进生成模型训练，涉及训练循环分解、模式承诺与端到端生成，属于生成模型训练/对齐与可控性范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）。同时论文在连续与离散域（图像、视频、语言）上验证，与 continuous_language_multimodal 方向相关。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel third pretraining axis (exploration scaling) that improves continuous and discrete generative models end-to-end, directly aligning with the user's continuous multimodal and world-model priorities. Signals: continuous language model, continuous multimodal model, flow matching, autoregressive video generation, autoregressive image generation

Abstract: The deep learning revolution, kicked off by AlexNet, taught us that end-to-end training beats decomposing a problem into hand-designed stages. Generative modeling, however, has remained the exception-despite generative models being remarkably capable, they are still not trained end-to-end. This is because, at its core, generative modeling is about handling distributions with many modes, and existing scalable approaches handle this the same way, by factoring the generation procedure, which prevents end-to-end generation. In this work, we introduce Explorative Modeling, a new paradigm that instead factors the training loop, exploring K candidate matches between model generations and data, and training on the best, so predictions commit to modes rather than blurring them. We find Explorative Models (XMs) useful in two settings. First, increasing exploration adds a third pretraining axis beyond parameters and data for existing generative models-where scaling exploration monotonically improves performance across both continuous and discrete domains (images, video, and language). Notably, gains from exploration increase with scale, climbing from 7% to 36% as data scales and from 13% to 23% as models grow, with efficiency gains more than doubling at 3x the compute. Concretely, exploration improves FLOP efficiency by 4.1x, sample efficiency by 6.2x, parameter efficiency by 47%, lifts the strongest of image-generation recipes to a near-state-of-the-art 1.43 FID on ImageNet without guidance, enables scaling how end-to-end existing models are, and unlocks scaling generalization. Second, XMs enable end-to-end reconstructive generative modeling, matching diffusion on control tasks with 16-256x fewer inference steps. Together, these results establish XMs as both a new pretraining axis for existing generative models and a standalone end-to-end generative modeling paradigm.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: RefineSVG 在初始 SVG 生成后调用外部渲染引擎，将渲染输出与目标图像比较得到多维视觉残差图（Diff-Map），作为 ReAct 风格校正信号反馈给模型进行定向校正。引入 SVG 语义词汇表将 token 序列压缩超过 52%。训练采用渐进式管线：监督微调、拒绝采样冷启动数据构建、端到端 agentic 强化学习。

Result: 实验表明 RefineSVG 在重建保真度、结构准确性和代码质量上持续优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 闭环视觉反馈与强化学习相结合能有效提升 MLLM 在图像到 SVG 生成任务上的自校正能力，验证了 render-observe-correct 交互范式的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于视觉反馈的强化学习框架 RefineSVG，用于图像到 SVG 生成的自校正，核心是端到端 agentic 强化学习与视觉反馈信号对齐，属于生成模型对齐与奖励方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] generation_alignment。

Importance reason: Focuses on SVG generation via RL feedback, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video/image generation. Signals: image-to-SVG generation, visual feedback RL, MLLM self-correction, token compression, agentic RL

Abstract: We propose RefineSVG, a single-step closed-loop visual feedback framework that enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to perform high-fidelity image-to-SVG generation through self-correction. Existing MLLM-based approaches rely on single-pass open-loop inference, where the model receives visual input only once and must generate thousands of SVG code tokens without intermediate verification. This paradigm inevitably leads to geometric drift, error accumulation, and visual hallucination on complex images. RefineSVG overcomes this limitation by invoking an external rendering engine after an initial SVG generation pass to compare the rendered output against the target image. The comparison yields a multi-dimensional visual residual map (Diff-Map) that is fed back to the model as a ReAct-style correction signal, driving a targeted correction step. To support this render-observe-correct interaction, we further introduce an SVG-oriented semantic vocabulary that compresses token sequences by over 52%. A progressive training pipeline spanning supervised fine-tuning, rejection-sampling cold-start data construction, and end-to-end agentic reinforcement learning aligns the model with closed-loop visual correction. Extensive experiments show that RefineSVG consistently outperforms existing baselines in reconstruction fidelity, structural accuracy, and code is available at .

</details>

Motivation: 在专业文档和会议评审场景中，冗长文本带来高认知负担，将文本转化为逻辑清晰的图表至关重要。然而现有 Text-to-SVG 生成面临三大挑战：复杂逻辑图表数据集稀缺、缺乏显式布局先验导致空间排列混乱、缺少细粒度视觉反馈来验证渲染输出并修正美学缺陷。

Result: 大量实验表明 GVR-Coder 优于竞争基线，能够可靠地生成逻辑连贯且视觉美观的图表。

Conclusion: 通过数据、布局先验、强化学习奖励与智能体反馈循环的多层次设计，GVR-Coder 有效解决了复杂文档场景下结构化 SVG 图表生成的三大挑战，验证了视觉反馈机制对生成质量提升的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 GVR-Coder 框架，通过课程式拒绝采样微调、布局约束知识、双渲染反馈强化学习（RLHF 式奖励）以及生成-验证-修复智能体循环来生成结构化 SVG 图表。核心贡献在于利用奖励信号和反馈机制优化生成质量，属于生成模型对齐与奖励范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] generation_alignment。

Importance reason: Focuses on SVG diagram generation with RL feedback, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video/world-model generation. Signals: SVG generation, reinforcement learning, visual feedback, document/meeting scenarios, no continuous token/video/world-model keywords

Abstract: In demanding professional environments and meeting review scenarios, lengthy text often imposes a high cognitive load. To facilitate efficient information communication, transforming verbose text into logically clear diagrams is essential. Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) provide an effective representation for this purpose due to their editability and resolution independence. However, current research on Text-to-SVG generation remains hindered by three major challenges: (1) the scarcity of datasets for complex, logic-rich diagrams; (2) the absence of explicit layout priors, which leads to chaotic spatial arrangements; and (3) the lack of fine-grained visual feedback to validate rendered outputs and correct aesthetic defects. To address these challenges, at the data level, we introduce DocMeetSVG-100K, a large-scale SVG dataset tailored for document authoring and meeting review scenarios. At the model level, we propose GVR-Coder, a novel framework designed to generate high-quality logical diagrams from lengthy professional texts. Specifically, we adopt a curriculum-driven rejection sampling fine-tuning to progressively enhance the model's capability in modeling complex structures, while explicitly incorporating layout constraint knowledge during training. In addition, we introduce reinforcement learning from dual rendering feedback, a mechanism that provides implicit feedback through reward signals to jointly optimize structural complexity and visual aesthetics. Furthermore, we design a generate-verify-repair agent loop, which improves generation quality through explicit, fine-grained feedback and targeted refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GVR-Coder outperforms competitive baselines and reliably produces logically coherent and visually appealing diagrams. Code and data are available at .

</details>


### [45] [Temporal Concentration from Rollout Errors: Implicit Preference Optimization for Text-to-Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28058)
*Henglin Liu, Fangyuan Kong, Jing Wang, Yizhou Lin, Nisha Huang, Chang Liu, Xintao Wang, Pengfei Wan, Kun Gai, Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

Importance: 高优先级 (93.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 concentrated Implicit Preference Optimization (cIPO)，一种面向文生视频扩散模型的后训练对齐框架。它从去噪过程中直接推导隐式偏好信号（原视频为偏好样本、重建为不偏好样本），无需人工标注或外部奖励模型，并通过帧级时序重建误差将优化集中在高误差片段，从而精准修正运动塌缩、物体闪烁、颜色过饱和等时序稀疏伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 DPO 的视频扩散偏好对齐虽提升视觉质量，但难以解决运动塌缩、物体闪烁、颜色过饱和等时序稀疏伪影。原因在于：(1) 偏好归因瓶颈——离线人工标注成本高且无法捕捉学习动态，在线奖励信号虽 rollout-aware 但不稳定且有偏；(2) 时序信用错配——均匀施加的监督无法有效针对伪影出现的短暂片段。

Method: cIPO 从去噪过程直接推导隐式偏好信号：给定真实视频，模型添加前向噪声并通过迭代去噪重建，将原始视频视为偏好样本、重建结果视为不偏好样本。该公式无需人工标注或外部奖励模型即可捕捉推理时错误。同时利用原始与重建视频之间的帧级差异揭示失败发生位置，计算时序重建误差并将优化集中在高误差片段，实现更精准的失败区域修正。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，cIPO 持续提升视频真实性与时序连贯性，验证了隐式偏好与时序集中优化的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: cIPO 通过从去噪过程提取隐式偏好信号并集中优化高误差时序片段，有效解决了视频扩散模型偏好对齐中的归因瓶颈与时序信用错配问题，为视频生成对齐提供了一种无需人工标注的高效方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出 cIPO，一种面向文生视频扩散模型的隐式偏好优化对齐方法，属于生成模型对齐与奖励范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）。

Importance reason: Directly targets text-to-video diffusion alignment with a novel implicit preference method, strongly matching the user's video generation and world model priorities. Signals: text-to-video diffusion, preference optimization, DPO, temporal artifacts, no reward model

Abstract: Recent advances in preference alignment for diffusion-based video generation, particularly via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), have significantly improved visual quality. However, temporally sparse artifacts such as motion collapse, object flickering, and color oversaturation remain a major barrier to perceptual realism. Existing methods struggle with these issues due to two key limitations: (1) the preference attribution bottleneck, where offline human annotations are costly and fail to accurately capture learning dynamics, while online reward signals are rollout-aware but often unstable and biased; and (2) temporal credit misallocation, where uniformly applied supervision cannot effectively target the brief segments in which artifacts occur. To address these challenges, we propose concentrated Implicit Preference Optimization (cIPO), a post-training framework for video diffusion models. cIPO derives implicit preference signals directly from the denoising process: given a real video, the model adds forward noise and reconstructs it via iterative denoising, treating the original as the preferred sample and the reconstruction as the dispreferred one. This formulation captures inference-time errors without requiring human annotations or external reward models. Moreover, frame-level discrepancies between original and reconstructed videos reveal when failures occur. cIPO leverages this by computing temporal reconstruction errors and concentrating optimization on high-error segments, enabling more precise correction of failure-prone regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that cIPO consistently enhances video authenticity and temporal coherence across multiple datasets, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of implicit preference with temporally concentrated optimization.

</details>

*Jiajia Lin, Mingxuan Du, Tuowen Zhou, Benfeng Xu, Hongtao Xie*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图与个性化编辑模型在生成多人共享接触动作（如拥抱、背负、格斗）时仍存在肢体融合、虚构肢体、身体穿插等解剖学与几何错误，而现有评测大多忽略这些问题，VLM 作为评判者的清单式评测在 Interaction 维度上饱和，错误对人类明显却无法被自动评测捕获。

Method: 构建 MPIE-Bench：从视频中挖掘 2500 个编辑三元组，覆盖 405 个场景、14 类交互和四种接触密度（C0-C3）。提出 MPIE-Eval，利用冻结的公开多人网格重建方法，从两个新维度评分：Anatomy 检查每个人形质量是否被完整重建的人体集合解释，Interaction 检查重建人体之间的穿透和表面距离是否与指令要求的接触匹配。

Result: 在十个编辑模型上，网格 Anatomy 最高仅 0.65、网格 Interaction 最高仅 0.72，且分别由两个不同模型取得，说明没有单一编辑器在两个维度上都强；而 VLM 清单对同一批图像评分超过 0.95。五名标注者研究确认两个维度比零样本 VLM 评判更贴近人类判断，且排名在权重和阈值消融下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 现有编辑模型在多人交互场景的解剖学合理性上普遍不足，VLM 评判存在饱和问题，MPIE-Eval 提供的网格几何评测能更准确地反映人类判断，为多人交互编辑提供更可靠的评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MPIE-Bench 基准数据集与 MPIE-Eval 评测方法，用于评估多人交互图像编辑的解剖学合理性，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] generation_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: Benchmark/evaluation paper for multi-person interaction editing, which is outside the user's core continuous-model, autoregressive generation, and world-model priorities. Signals: benchmark, multi-person interaction editing, anatomy/interaction evaluation, mesh reconstruction, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Text-to-image and personalized editing models now synthesize high-fidelity single-subject images with ease. Yet placing multiple named people into shared contact actions such as embrace, carry, or grapple still exposes major failures: fused limbs, invented extremities, and interpenetrating bodies. Existing evaluations largely overlook these anatomical and geometric issues, and VLM-as-a-judge checklists often saturate on Interaction while the errors remain obvious to humans. We introduce MPIE-Bench, a 2,500-sample benchmark of video-mined editing triplets spanning 405 scenes, 14 interaction categories, and four contact densities (C0-C3). We also propose MPIE-Eval, whose two new axes score contact-time geometry from a frozen public multi-person mesh reconstruction. Anatomy asks whether every human-like mass is explained by a complete set of reconstructed bodies, and Interaction asks whether the penetration and surface distance between those bodies match the contact the instruction asked for. Across ten editors, mesh Anatomy tops out at 0.65 and mesh Interaction at 0.72 on two different models, so no single editor is strong on both, while VLM checklists rate the same images above 0.95. A five-rater study confirms that both axes track human judgement more closely than a zero-shot VLM judge, and the rankings hold under ablation of every weight and threshold.

</details>

*Joonhyung Bae, Dawon Park, Taegyun Kwon, Yoon-Seok Choi, Hyeon Hur, Satoshi Obata, Shigeru Kai, Yohei Wada, Yu Takahashi, Akira Maezawa, Jaebum Park, Jonghwa Park, Juhan Nam*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢琴演奏的 MIR 研究日益需要超越音频和 MIDI 的多模态数据与标注，但现有数据集缺乏同时包含动作、视频、音频、MIDI 和乐谱的完整多模态资源，限制了跨模态研究的发展。

Result: 提供了 11 小时、19 位演奏者（7 专业 + 12 业余）的多模态钢琴演奏数据，包含 Visual3D 身体段运动学数据、时间同步的多模态数据、交互式网页浏览器，以及指法标注工具和 MIDI-to-motion 生成用例。

Conclusion: SKY-Piano 为钢琴演奏的多模态研究提供了丰富的数据资源，支持动作生成、指法分析、演奏理解等多项研究任务，并通过 MIDI-to-motion 生成实验验证了数据集的可用性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出钢琴演奏多模态数据集（音频、MIDI、动作、多视角视频），并包含 MIDI-to-motion 生成用例，属于音频与多模态联合生成方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] audio_av_generation 子主题。

Importance reason: Dataset paper focused on piano performance, far from the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: multimodal dataset, piano performance, MIDI-to-motion, fingering annotation, no video generation

Abstract: Music information retrieval research on piano performance increasingly involves diverse modalities of data and annotations beyond audio and MIDI. We present SKY-Piano, a multimodal piano performance dataset that includes 11 hours of performance recordings of motion, multi-view video, audio, MIDI from 7 professional and 12 amateur pianists along with MusicXML scores. The performance pieces were selected considering playing technique, difficulty, and performer expertise on a shared core repertoire. The motion data include both hand and body motion, released in both flagged form, where samples lost to marker occlusion are marked as unreliable, and imputed form, where those gaps are reconstructed, together with Visual3D body-segment kinematics and other time-synchronized modalities. To easily browse different modalities of data at a glance, we provide an interactive web browser. In addition, we developed a fingering annotation model and tool for deriving pseudo fingering annotations from the MIDI and motion data. Lastly, we present MIDI-to-motion generation through a fine-tuning experiment as a use case of the dataset.

</details>

### [48] [Unifying Adversarially Robust Model Experts in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27897)
*Nguyen Duc Thai, Junhao Dong, Sua Qi Rong, Hua Yu, Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CARE（Collaborative Adversarial Robustness fine-tuning using Embedding alignment）框架，通过嵌入空间对齐实现多个对抗微调专家模型之间的知识交换与协同学习，最终整合为单一统一鲁棒模型，在图像分类和下游视觉语言任务上超越单独训练的专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（如 CLIP）易受对抗攻击威胁，对抗微调是主要防御手段，但不同微调策略产生的模型具有互补但各异的鲁棒性特征，单一模型难以在所有评测场景中表现优异，限制了防御能力。

Method: CARE 框架在训练期间维护多个鲁棒模型专家，通过嵌入空间协调（embedding-space harmonization）实现专家间知识交换，同时保留各自专长，最终将学习到的知识整合为单一统一鲁棒模型。

Result: 在经典图像分类和下游视觉语言任务上的大量实验表明，CARE 能够超越单独训练的模型专家，验证了跨专家协同学习对提升对抗鲁棒性的有效性。

Conclusion: 跨模型专家的协同学习是提升对抗鲁棒性的有前景方向，CARE 通过嵌入对齐实现专家互补鲁棒性继承，为 VLM 对抗防御提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于视觉语言模型（CLIP）的对抗鲁棒性防御，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_safety_attack。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial robustness via expert collaboration, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: adversarial robustness, CLIP, expert collaboration, embedding alignment, multimodal understanding

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, are vulnerable to adversarial attacks, posing a serious problem for real-life applications and deployment. Adversarial fine-tuning emerges as a prominent defense method; however, different fine-tuning strategies often produce specialized models with distinct robustness characteristics. Each fine-tuned model in turn thrives in some evaluation settings but falters on others, limiting their defensive capabilities. We refer to these specialized fine-tuned models as robust model experts and propose a collaborative adversarial fine-tuning framework: CARE - Collaborative Adversarial Robustness fine-tuning using Embedding alignment. CARE maintains multiple experts during training, enables knowledge exchange through embedding-space harmonization, and consolidates the learned knowledge into a single unified robust model. Experts benefit from one another while preserving their individual specializations, enabling the final model to inherit complementary robustness properties. In this paper, we demonstrate CARE on two different adversarial fine-tuning strategies with complementary robustness behaviors. Extensive experiments on classic image classification and downstream vision-language tasks display the effectiveness of our approach, with CARE being able to outperform individually learned model experts. The results suggest that collaborative learning across model experts is a promising direction for improving adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [49] [A Cross-Architecture Audit of Direction-Based Inference-Time Defences in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27910)
*Xiangyu Yin, Tora Bodin, Rohan Menon, Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文对视觉语言模型越狱的推理时方向减除防御方法进行了跨架构审计，在15个模型-层组合上比较五种防御候选，发现没有单一方法在所有指标上占优，且方向防御具有强架构特异性，需针对每个语言解码器家族单独校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时防御（如从残差流中减去校准方向）被广泛用于缓解视觉语言模型的越狱攻击，但缺乏跨架构的系统性比较，且不同防御方法之间的几何关系尚不明确。

Method: 在15个模型-层组合（来自四个架构家族）上，采用幅度受控协议比较五种防御候选：均值图像条件偏移、CMRM风格拒绝方向、ShiftDC风格攻击特定残差、提示忽略图像指令、随机控制。每个方向与同范数随机控制配对，评估拒绝恢复与效用保持。

Result: 没有单一候选在拒绝恢复和效用保持上同时占优。图像条件偏移在LLaVA 1.5和Pixtral 12B上领先，且是唯一在所有家族中效用损失保持在测量噪声底的方法；提示指令在Qwen2.5 VL上领先；攻击特定残差在Qwen2 VL 2B上领先。CMRM方向与图像条件偏移在全部15个单元中余弦对齐为正（均值0.35），表明两种方法恢复部分重叠的几何结构。

Conclusion: 方向减除防御具有强架构特异性且不可跨家族迁移，应针对每个语言解码器家族单独校准；同时文本与多模态拒绝几何存在部分重叠，为理解越狱防御机制提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 论文研究视觉语言模型越狱的推理时防御方法（direction-based inference-time defences），属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_safety_attack。

Importance reason: Focuses on jailbreak defence auditing, which is outside your configured priority directions and subtopics. Signals: cross-architecture audit, inference-time defences, vision-language model jailbreaks, direction subtraction, refusal geometry

Abstract: Inference time defences against vision language model jailbreaks often subtract a calibrated direction from the residual stream at a chosen decoder layer. We compare five defence candidates across 15 model and layer cells from four architectural families under a magnitude controlled protocol that matches the intervention size for each prompt and pairs every direction with a random control of the same norm. The candidates are the mean image conditioning shift, a CMRM style refusal direction, a ShiftDC style attack specific residual, a prompt instruction to ignore the image, and a random control. No single candidate dominates on both refusal recovery and utility preservation. The image conditioning shift leads on LLaVA 1.5 and Pixtral 12B and is the only candidate whose utility loss remains at the measurement noise floor in every family. The prompt instruction leads on Qwen2.5 VL, while the attack specific residual leads on Qwen2 VL 2B. The image conditioning direction is direction specific in 13 of 15 cells, but strongly architecture specific and nontransferable across the only dimension compatible pair, LLaVA 1.5 13B and Pixtral 12B. We also connect text only and multimodal refusal geometry. The CMRM direction has positive cosine alignment with the image conditioning shift in all 15 cells, with mean 0.35, range 0.17 to 0.65, 15 to 25 times the random vector null, and a sign test p value of about 3e-5. These results show that the two recipes recover partially overlapping geometry and that direction based defences should be calibrated separately for each language decoder family.

</details>


### [50] [One Anchor for All: Unified Multilingual and Multimodal Safety Alignment for LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27917)
*Enyi Shi, Fei Shen, Chuancheng Shi, Linxia Zhu, Shuyi Miao, Jinhui Tang, Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经元级跨维度安全对齐框架 MLS-Neurons，通过识别模态与语言共享的安全神经元（仅更新约0.03%参数），将英文安全监督迁移到多语言多模态场景，显著提升 LVLM 对复合攻击的防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 安全防御方法将语言与模态防御割裂，且面临安全数据稀缺与微调成本高的问题，难以应对多语言指令与视觉信息结合的复合攻击。

Method: 通过对比有害与良性样本的响应识别单语单模态安全神经元，量化功能显著性（激活强度与下游影响）；在同一语言内取交集得到模态共享安全神经元（MS-Neurons）；以英语为语义锚点跨语言取交集得到模态与语言共享安全神经元（MLS-Neurons）；仅更新这一最小共享神经元子集（约0.03%参数）完成安全对齐。

Result: 在多种多语言与多模态安全基准上显著优于现有 SOTA 方法，同时保持模型通用能力。

Conclusion: 通过定位并仅更新模态与语言共享的安全神经元，可以高效地将英文安全监督迁移到多语言多模态场景，实现低成本的跨维度安全对齐。

Classification reason: 论文提出面向 LVLM 的多语言与多模态安全对齐框架，属于多模态安全防御方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_safety_attack（多模态安全与攻防）。

Importance reason: Safety alignment for LVLMs is peripheral to your core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: safety alignment, multilingual multimodal, neuron-level tuning, LVLM defense, parameter-efficient

Abstract: As large vision-language models (LVLMs) are deployed globally, the combination of multilingual instructions and visual information makes malicious attacks more covert and sophisticated than ever before. However, existing methods isolate language and modality defenses, which, coupled with the scarcity of safety data and high fine-tuning costs, makes it difficult for models to defend against compound attacks. To address this severe challenge, we propose a neuron-level cross-dimensional safety alignment framework driven by modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons). First, we identify monolingual and unimodal safety neurons by comparing responses to harmful and benign samples, quantifying functional saliency through activation strength and downstream impact. Then, by intersecting these unimodal neurons within each language, we extract modality-shared safety neurons (MS-Neurons) responsive to both visual and textual risks, bridging the safety representation gap between modalities. Furthermore, using English as a semantic anchor, we intersect MS-Neurons across languages to identify modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons), serving as key defenses against compound attacks. Finally, we update only this minimal subset of shared neurons (~0.03% of parameters), transferring English-only safety supervision to multilingual and multimodal scenarios. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across diverse multilingual and multimodal safety benchmarks while preserving general utility.

</details>

### [51] [Witness Evidence Portfolios: Single-Prefill Risk Detection for Closed Multimodal Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27667)
*Fexiang Liu, Shiye Wang, Qiang Qiu, Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (76.3) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: WEP 提出一种白盒推理时风险检测方法，通过逐层估计视觉贡献对预测候选的支持/反对程度，构建问题相关证据来源与符号证据集中度两类可解释路由族，结合候选置信度判断闭集视觉答案是否可信、需复核或转交更强系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 的可靠部署需要判断一个自信的视觉答案是否应被信任、复核或转交更强系统。现有置信度分数只捕捉候选的边际，却无法反映这些边际所对应的视觉读数的来源与分布，缺乏可解释的风险检测手段。

Method: WEP 利用产生答案的同一白盒 prefill 路径，逐层估计哪些视觉贡献支持或反对预测候选，通过两个可解释路由族（问题相关证据来源与符号证据集中度）进行总结；采用嵌套分组验证选择更可靠的路由族与稀疏 top-k 路由组合，并与候选置信度融合。无需图像扰动、解码改动、反向传播或外部验证器。

Result: 在三个 MLLM 和四个二值答案基准上，WEP 将平均错误 AP 提升 0.134；全部 12 个模型-数据集组合增益为正，10 对组合的图像聚类 bootstrap 区间严格为正。

Conclusion: WEP 提供了一种无需外部验证器、无需扰动或解码改动的白盒推理时风险检测方案，能有效提升闭集视觉答案的可信度判断，适用于白盒闭集答案系统并需使用带标签的校准切片。

Classification reason: 论文研究 MLLM 闭集视觉答案的推理时风险检测与可信度判断，属于多模态模型的可靠性/忠实性范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态幻觉与可靠性。

Importance reason: Solid white-box reliability method for MLLMs, but it targets hallucination/risk detection rather than the user's core generative or continuous-model directions. Signals: inference-time risk detection, white-box prefill, no perturbation or external verifier, interpretable routing, closed-set visual answers

Abstract: Reliable deployment of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) requires deciding whether a confident visual answer should be trusted, reviewed, or routed to a stronger system. Confidence scores capture candidate margins, but not where the estimated signed visual readouts associated with those margins come from or how they are distributed. We study inference-time risk detection for closed visual answers using the same white-box prefill path that produces the answer. Witness Evidence Portfolios (WEP) first estimates, layer by layer, which visual contributions support or contradict the predicted candidate. It summarizes these contributions through two interpretable route families: question-related evidence provenance and signed evidence concentration. Nested grouped validation chooses the more reliable family and a sparse top-k route portfolio, which is fused with candidate confidence. WEP needs no image perturbation, decoding change, backward pass, or external verifier. Across three MLLMs and four binary-answer benchmarks, WEP improves mean error AP by 0.134. All 12 model--dataset gains are positive, and image-cluster bootstrap intervals are strictly positive on 10 pairs. WEP targets white-box closed-answer systems and uses a labeled calibration slice.

</details>


### [52] [Hallucinations Leave a Grounding Signature:Verifier-Guided Decoding for Selective Object Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27823)
*Lei Yang, Xinze Liu, Dayan Wu, Ding Wang, Hengjie Zhu, Zihao Zhang, Tianzhu Hu, Hanqi Wu, Peng Fu, Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (61.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Intrinsic Grounding Signature (IGS) 与 Verifier-Guided Decoding (VGD) 框架，通过轻量验证器检查生成过程中每个对象提及的视觉证据支持，对高风险幻觉对象进行选择性 KV cache 回滚与抑制，实现对象级幻觉的精准缓解，同时保留模型原有的视觉理解与真实对象覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 幻觉缓解方法缺乏可靠的对象级 grounding 诊断，往往采用粗粒度干预，会损害视觉理解、缩短回答并降低真实对象覆盖率。输出置信度反映的是 next-token 合理性而非视觉支持，语言先验会让不存在的对象显得确定。因此需要一种能在生成过程中检测每个对象提及是否被可靠视觉证据支持的方法。

Method: 作者发现缺失的诊断证据编码在 Intrinsic Grounding Signature (IGS) 中——一种分布式的带符号注意力模式，即使对高置信幻觉也保持信息量。基于 IGS 提出 Verifier-Guided Decoding (VGD)：一个轻量验证器检查每个出现的对象提及，当识别为高风险时回滚 KV cache，抑制该对象及其同义词，并重新生成受影响的续写。VGD 仅对高风险对象提及进行干预。

Result: 在 CHAIR 和 AMBER-G 上达到 SOTA 对象幻觉缓解效果：在 @rec90 下，AMBER-G CHAIR 降低 43.6%，同时保留 99.6% 的真实对象覆盖率；CHAIR-MSCOCO 的 CHAIR_i/CHAIR_s 分别降低 37.0%/30.4%，且不缩短描述长度。

Conclusion: VGD 通过选择性干预高风险对象提及，在显著降低对象幻觉的同时保持模型的视觉理解能力和真实对象覆盖，验证了 IGS 作为对象级 grounding 诊断信号的有效性，为幻觉缓解提供了更精细、更可靠的解码策略。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的对象级幻觉检测与缓解，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性这一经典方向，直接映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_hallucination 子主题。

Importance reason: Solid hallucination-mitigation work for LVLMs, but it is not aligned with the user's core continuous-model, generation, or world-model priorities. Signals: object hallucination, verifier-guided decoding, KV cache rollback, LVLM reliability, attention-based grounding signature

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate objects that are absent from an image. Despite recent progress, existing mitigation methods still lack reliable object-level grounding diagnostics and therefore tend to apply coarse-grained interventions, which can impair visual understanding, shorten responses, and reduce coverage of genuinely grounded objects. The key challenge is thus to detect, during generation, whether each emerging object mention is supported by reliable visual evidence, so that hallucination can be mitigated selectively. Yet output confidence reflects next-token plausibility rather than visual support, allowing language priors to make absent objects appear certain. We show that the missing diagnostic evidence is encoded in an Intrinsic Grounding Signature (IGS), a distributed signed attention pattern that remains informative for such confident hallucinations. Based on IGS, we propose Verifier-Guided Decoding (VGD), a decoding framework in which a lightweight verifier examines each emerging object mention, rolls back the KV cache when the mention is identified as high risk, suppresses the object and its synonyms, and regenerates the affected continuation. Because VGD intervenes only on object mentions identified as high risk, it reduces object hallucination while preserving the model's original visual understanding and grounded object coverage. Experiments on CHAIR and AMBER-G show that VGD achieves state-of-the-art object hallucination reduction: at @rec90, it cuts AMBER-G CHAIR by 43.6\% while retaining 99.6\% of grounded-object coverage, and reduces CHAIR-MSCOCO CHAIR$_i$/CHAIR$_s$ by 37.0\%/30.4\% without shortening captions.

</details>

### [53] [Shared Semantic Codebook Distillation for Unpaired Cross-Modal Medical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27357)
*Dillan Imans, Phuoc-Nguyen Bui, Duc-Tai Le, Hyunseung Choo*

Main category: cs.CV, eess.IV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (50.86) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出共享语义码本蒸馏（SSCD），通过共享离散码本将教师与学生模态的表示对齐为分布，实现无需配对样本的跨模态医学分类知识蒸馏，在OCT-to-fundus和CT-to-chest-X-ray两个非配对设置上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态知识蒸馏可将诊断知识从昂贵教师模态迁移到廉价学生模态，但医学图像中两种模态往往非配对（来自不同患者队列、特征空间几何不兼容），导致实例级蒸馏失效、直接特征匹配不可靠。

Method: 提出共享语义码本蒸馏（SSCD）：通过共享的离散码本将每张图像表示为模态无关词汇上的分布，通过全局和类别条件两种方式对齐跨模态分布，无需配对样本或直接可比的原始特征。码本通过指数移动平均在线演化，并用熵正则化和死码重启保持多样性。推理时丢弃教师侧和码本模块，仅保留学生编码器和分类器。

Result: 在两个异构非配对设置上：OCT-to-fundus视网膜疾病分类从64.5提升到70.2 macro-F1，CT-to-chest-X-ray肺炎分类从73.8提升到76.3 macro-F1，均优于所有评估的蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: SSCD通过共享离散码本实现模态无关的分布对齐，有效解决了非配对跨模态医学分类中的知识蒸馏难题，且推理时无需额外模块，具有实际部署价值。

Classification reason: 论文提出跨模态知识蒸馏方法，通过共享离散码本对齐不同模态的表示分布，属于多模态对齐范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 子主题。

Importance reason: Medical cross-modal distillation with discrete codebooks is methodologically interesting but falls outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: medical classification, unpaired cross-modal distillation, discrete codebook, knowledge distillation, negative keyword: medical diagnosis

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation can transfer diagnostic knowledge from a strong but costly teacher modality to a cheaper and more deployable student modality. In medical image analysis, however, the two modalities are often unpaired: they are collected from different patient cohorts and occupy geometrically incompatible feature spaces. This makes instance-level distillation invalid and direct feature matching unreliable. To address these challenges, we propose Shared Semantic Codebook Distillation (SSCD), which compares teacher and student representations through a shared discrete codebook. Each image is represented as a distribution over a common, modality-agnostic vocabulary, and knowledge is transferred by aligning these distributions across modalities, both globally and class-conditionally, without requiring paired samples or directly comparable raw features. The codebook is evolved online by exponential moving average and kept diverse through entropy regularization and dead-code restart. At inference, all teacher-side and codebook modules are discarded, leaving only the student encoder and classifier. On two heterogeneous unpaired settings, OCT-to-fundus retinal disease classification and CT-to-chest-X-ray pneumonia classification, SSCD improves the student from 64.5 to 70.2 macro-F1 and from 73.8 to 76.3 macro-F1, respectively, outperforming all evaluated distillation baselines on both settings. Code and pretrained models are available at

</details>

*Xingjian Wu, Junlin Liu, Xingchen Liu, Xuhang Zhu, Jianing Wang, Linsen Guo, Xiaoyu Li, Xuezhi Cao, Xunliang Cai*

TL;DR: 提出 CRPO（对比强化策略优化），从对比学习视角重构智能体场景下的在线自蒸馏（OPSD），利用预测熵区分正位置（反思性探索）与负位置（暴露偏差），进行组内对比以保留可靠的细粒度优化信号，在 13 个推理与深度搜索基准上优于现有 RL 与自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自蒸馏（OPSD）虽提供稠密的 logit 级监督，但因自教师特权信息存在暴露偏差；在多轮智能体场景中会导致推理路径收敛并失去清晰优化方向。作者希望解决这一训练稳定性与泛化问题。

Method: CRPO 将智能体 OPSD 从对比学习视角重构：利用预测熵区分正位置（反思性探索）与负位置（暴露偏差），并执行组内对比（group-wise contrast）以保留可靠、细粒度的优化信号。

Result: 在 13 个具有挑战性的推理与深度搜索基准上，CRPO 一致优于现有强化学习与自蒸馏基线，显著提升长时程交互中的训练稳定性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 对比学习视角下的自蒸馏重构能有效缓解暴露偏差与推理路径收敛问题，为智能体场景的 LLM 后训练提供了更稳定、更泛化的优化范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出对比强化策略优化（CRPO），将智能体场景下的在线自蒸馏（OPSD）从对比学习视角重构，属于强化学习与策略优化方法，映射到多模态强化学习与对齐这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL/self-distillation for agentic reasoning, which is outside the user's core continuous multimodal and video generation priorities. Signals: reinforcement learning, self-distillation, agentic reasoning, contrastive learning, LLM post-training

Abstract: Recent advances in post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly rely on Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) or On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD). While OPSD provides dense, logit-level supervision, it inherently suffers from exposure bias due to the privileged information of the self-teacher. In multi-turn agentic settings, this leads to reasoning route convergence and the loss of clear optimization directions. To tackle these challenges, we introduce Contrastive Reinforced Policy Optimization (CRPO), which reformulates agentic OPSD from a contrastive learning perspective. By leveraging predictive entropy to distinguish between positive positions (reflective exploration) and negative positions (exposure bias), CRPO conducts group-wise contrast to preserve reliable, fine-grained optimization signals. Extensive evaluations across 13 challenging reasoning and deep-search benchmarks demonstrate that CRPO consistently outperforms existing reinforcement learning and self-distillation baselines, significantly enhancing training stability and generalization in long-horizon interactions.

</details>


### [55] [FaithEyes: Towards Faithful Tool Use via Multi-Agent Process-Image Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28225)
*Haoqing Wang, Xingrun Xing, Wei Xia, Ziheng Li, Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

TL;DR: FaithEyes 是一个多智能体自评判框架，用于提升智能体 VLM 工具调用的忠实性。它用 VLM 判断每个过程图像是否有助于回答问题，将判断注入推理上下文并用于缩放工具奖励以抑制奖励黑客，同时通过多智能体设计在推理时无需外部模型即可获得判断，保证训练与评测一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体 VLM 常不忠实地使用工具（如裁剪错误区域或遗漏目标），这些装饰性或错位的工具调用浪费计算，且模型依赖先验知识而非检索到的证据。现有方法的工具奖励无法区分有用与无用的调用，工具反馈也不携带有用性信号。

Method: FaithEyes 采用多智能体自评判框架：用 VLM 判断每个过程图像是否有助于回答问题，将判断注入推理上下文作为工具观察的一部分以辅助后续推理，同时用 helpful-tool ratio 缩放工具奖励以抑制奖励黑客。设计多智能体框架使模型自身作为子智能体评判主智能体的工具调用，推理时无需外部模型。训练采用两阶段 SFT+RL 流程，在改编的开源数据上进行。

Result: 在视觉感知与推理基准上取得具有竞争力或更优的准确率，同时显著提升工具调用的忠实性。

Conclusion: FaithEyes 通过过程图像验证与奖励缩放有效提升了工具调用的忠实性，同时保持或提升了下游任务准确率，验证了多智能体自评判框架在训练与评测一致性上的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 FaithEyes 多智能体自评判框架，通过工具奖励缩放（helpful-tool ratio）抑制 reward hacking，并采用两阶段 SFT+RL 训练，属于多模态强化学习与对齐范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_rl_alignment。

Importance reason: Focuses on tool-use faithfulness in agentic VLMs, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: multi-agent self-judging, tool reward scaling, process-image verification, agentic VLM, RL training

Abstract: Agentic vision-language models (VLMs), which interleave textual reasoning with explicit tool calls such as cropping and code-based image manipulation, have emerged as a compelling paradigm for reliable and interpretable multimodal reasoning. However, recent studies have revealed that such models often use tools unfaithfully. Many process images are irrelevant to the question (e.g., the tool crops the wrong region or misses the queried target), yet the call still receives full credit and the model still answers correctly. Such decorative or misaligned tool calls waste computation and reveal that the model leans on prior knowledge or the original image rather than the evidence it retrieves. This may stem from two limitations of prevailing methods: the tool reward fails to distinguish useful from useless calls, and tool feedback carries no signal of usefulness. To this end, we introduce FaithEyes, a multi-agent self-judging framework. Concretely, we use a VLM to judge whether each process image helps answer the question. The judgement is injected into the reasoning context as part of the tool observation to help subsequent reasoning, and meanwhile is used to scale the tool reward by the helpful-tool ratio to suppress reward hacking. To keep judgement available at evaluation and thus ensure train-test consistency, we further design a multi-agent framework where the model itself serves as a subagent to judge the tool calls from main agent, eliminating any dependence on an external model at inference. Training via a two-stage SFT + RL pipeline on adapted open-source data, FaithEyes attains competitive or superior accuracy across visual perception and reasoning benchmarks, while markedly improving tool faithfulness. The homepage is at .

</details>


### [56] [Group-Reflective Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28076)
*Binbin Zheng, Zijun Xie, Guanqun Zhao, Enlei Gong, Xing Ma, Xiaoliang Fu, Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 提出 GRSD（组反思自蒸馏）方法，利用策略自身验证过的 rollout 组，通过 stop-gradient 快照对比成功与失败轨迹的反思，构造组级特权引导，再由自教师进行回合级信用分配，从而增强 agentic 强化学习中的稀疏监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 对 LLM agent 训练有效，但终端奖励只提供粗粒度的轨迹级监督，成功行为、重复错误与偶然选择纠缠在同一结果信号中。现有 agentic 自蒸馏方法依赖外部检索或更强模型提取的技能，可能与当前经验不匹配、超出策略能力或过于路径特定。

Method: GRSD 从策略自身的已验证 rollout 中派生能力对齐且结果可区分的引导。对每个 prompt，策略对 on-policy 组中的每条已验证轨迹进行反思，stop-gradient 快照对比成功与失败 rollout 的反思以构造组级特权引导。基于该引导，自教师通过调节基于结果的优势来细化回合级信用分配，同时保持验证器确定的学习方向。

Result: 在多个 agentic 环境和多种模型规模下，GRSD 一致优于竞争基线，并对未见任务具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GRSD 通过利用策略自身验证 rollout 的组级反思对比，有效增强稀疏奖励监督，提升 agentic RL 的性能与泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Group-Reflective Self-Distillation (GRSD) 方法，用于 agentic 强化学习中的稀疏奖励增强与自蒸馏，属于强化学习与对齐范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Solid RLVR/agentic RL method paper, but it is outside the user's configured priority directions and subtopics (no continuous/multimodal/video/world-model focus). Signals: agentic reinforcement learning, self-distillation, sparse reward credit assignment, RLVR, LLM agents

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is effective for training large language model agents. However, terminal rewards provide only coarse trajectory-level supervision, leaving successful behaviors, recurring mistakes, and incidental choices entangled in the same outcome signal. Existing agentic self-distillation methods enrich sparse supervision with natural-language skills, but skills retrieved externally or extracted from a single trajectory by stronger models may mismatch current experience, exceed the policy's capability, or remain path-specific. We propose Group-Reflective Self-Distillation (GRSD), which derives capability-aligned and outcome-discriminative guidance from the policy's own verified rollouts. For each prompt, the policy reflects on each verified trajectory in an on-policy group, and a stop-gradient snapshot contrasts the resulting reflections from successful and failed rollouts to construct group-level privileged guidance. Conditioned on this guidance, a self-teacher refines turn-level credit assignment by modulating outcome-based advantages while preserving the verifier-determined learning direction. Experiments across multiple agentic environments and model scales demonstrate that GRSD consistently outperforms competitive baselines and generalizes more effectively to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [57] [VAD: Attributing Visual Evidence for Target Reconstruction in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28590)
*Kangning Zhang, Yixing Li, Shuai Shao, Qingyao Li, Zhengxi Lu, Zhiyuan Yao, Jianghao Lin, Wenxiang Jiao, Yuan Lu, Weiwen Liu, Weinan Zhang, Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (55.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VAD 提出一种反事实目标重建算法，通过评估教师模型在有/无视觉证据时的对数概率变化，估计教师修正中由视觉证据支持的部分，并据此重建学生锚定的训练目标，在多模态 on-policy 蒸馏中优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态 on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）通过特权视图教师监督学生生成轨迹来迁移细粒度视觉知识，但其 next-token 修正是"源混合"的，同时包含视觉信号、语言先验和教师特定效应。关键挑战在于估计哪些修正是由视觉证据支持的，而不仅仅是"在哪里"或"以多强"进行蒸馏。

Method: VAD 是一种反事实目标重建算法。在每个学生生成前缀处，VAD 用同一固定教师分别在有证据和无证据的情况下评估，两者中心化对数概率的变化定义 ut（有符号的视觉证据方向代理）。VAD 将原始修正投影到该代理上，得到干预对齐分量和代理无法解释的残差，再从前者重建学生锚定目标。训练时重建目标提供主要监督信号，特权教师仅作为弱正则化器。

Result: 在 4B 和 9B 规模的六个细粒度视觉基准上，VAD 优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权。Token 级和受控目标分析表明，代理对齐分量富含任务相关的视觉修正，并产生更强的目标偏移，尤其在证据反驳错误答案时效果显著。

Conclusion: 反事实目标重建是源混合监督的有效替代方案，能够更精准地提取视觉证据支持的修正信号，提升多模态 on-policy 蒸馏的效果。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态 on-policy 蒸馏中的视觉证据归因与目标重建，属于多模态对齐/蒸馏范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal distillation for RL/alignment, which is adjacent to but not central to your priority on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: multimodal distillation, on-policy distillation, counterfactual target reconstruction, visual evidence attribution, multimodal understanding

Abstract: Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) transfers fine-grained visual knowledge by supervising student-generated trajectories with a privileged-view teacher. Yet its next-token corrections are source-mixed, combining visual signals with linguistic priors and teacher-specific effects. The key challenge is to estimate which corrections are supported by visual evidence, not merely where or how strongly to distill. We introduce Visual Attribution Distillation (VAD), a counterfactual target-reconstruction algorithm that estimates the visually attributable part of a teacher correction. At each student-generated prefix, VAD evaluates the same fixed teacher with the relevant evidence present and removed. The corresponding change in centered log-probabilities defines ut, a signed proxy for the visual evidence direction that estimates how revealing the evidence supports or refutes candidate tokens. VAD projects the original correction onto this proxy to obtain an intervention-aligned component and a proxy-unexplained residual, then reconstructs a student-anchored target from the former. During training, this reconstructed target supplies the primary supervision signal, while the privileged teacher contributes a weak regularizer. Across six fine-grained visual benchmarks at 4B and 9B scales, VAD outperforms direct privileged-view distillation and visual-advantage weighting. Token- level and controlled-target analyses show that the proxy-aligned component is enriched in task-relevant visual corrections and yields stronger target shifts, especially when evidence refutes a mistaken answer. These results support counterfactual target reconstruction as an effective alternative to source-mixed supervision.

</details>


### [58] [Can Vision-Language Models Reason about AI Edits in Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28464)
*Darsha Udayanga, Pin-Yu Chen, Payel Das, Qiang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

TL;DR: 本文研究能否用强化学习（GRPO）而非显式推理监督来训练 VLM 对 AI 编辑图像进行推理式检测与定位。模型生成结构化推理轨迹并预测图像是否被篡改，再由轻量分割模型生成像素级定位掩码，并提出 eff-IoU 统一评测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 生成图像篡改日益难以识别，传统二分类检测器缺乏可解释性与泛化能力。VLM 虽有强视觉理解与推理能力，但现有方法依赖带人工解释的监督微调，未充分利用其内在推理能力。作者希望探索用强化学习而非显式推理监督来训练 VLM 推理 AI 编辑内容。

Method: 提出基于 GRPO 的训练框架，利用简单的准确率与格式奖励。给定输入图像，模型生成结构化推理轨迹并预测是否被篡改；随后用轻量分割模型根据推理输出生成像素级定位掩码。同时提出 eff-IoU 统一指标联合评估检测与定位性能。

Result: 在多个图像篡改数据集上，该方法在检测与定位性能上与最先进的图像伪造检测器相当，但所需监督显著更弱。

Conclusion: 强化学习为训练 VLM 推理 AI 生成内容提供了有效且可扩展的机制，无需显式推理监督即可获得具有可解释性的检测与定位能力。

Classification reason: 论文使用 GRPO 强化学习训练 VLM 进行图像篡改检测与定位推理，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: Uses RL (GRPO) for VLM reasoning on image tampering, but the topic is peripheral to your core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: GRPO reasoning training, VLM tampering detection, eff-IoU metric, weak supervision, no continuous tokens or video/world-model focus

Abstract: Detection and localization of AI-tampered images are critical for trustworthy AI, yet modern generative models have made such manipulations increasingly difficult to identify. While traditional binary classifiers can detect image tampering, they lack interpretability and generalization. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a promising alternative due to their strong visual understanding and reasoning capabilities; however, existing approaches typically rely on supervised finetuning with curated explanations rather than exploiting their inherent reasoning capabilities. In this work, we investigate whether VLMs can be trained to reason about AI-generated image edits using reinforcement learning (RL) rather than explicit reasoning supervision. Motivated by the success in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an RL technique that incentivizes the model to reason by generating thinking traces prior to giving the final answer, we propose a GRPO-based training framework that utilizes simple accuracy and format rewards. Given an input image, the model produces a structured reasoning trace and predicts whether the image has been tampered with. A lightweight segmentation model is then guided by the reasoning output to generate pixel-level localization masks. Experiments across multiple image manipulation datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive detection and localization performance compared to state-of-the-art image forgery detectors, despite requiring substantially weaker supervision. We introduce effective intersection over union (eff-IoU), a unified metric to jointly evaluate detection and localization. These results suggest that reinforcement learning provides an effective and scalable mechanism for teaching VLMs to reason about AI-generated content.

</details>


### [59] [Correcting What You Cannot See: Credit Assignment for Perception Distillation in Multimodal Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28336)
*Feng Xiong, Leyan Xue, Hongyu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (58.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Perception-Correction Distillation (PCD)，一种无标签方法，通过下游失败与教师-学生分歧作为互补证据，识别可纠正的感知失败，从而改进多模态推理器的 on-policy 蒸馏。PCD 使用分离的感知-推理 rollout 和均值保持权重，在八个基准上将 8B→2B 宏平均从 44.50 提升到 47.28，32B→8B 从 56.94 提升到 61.22。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy 蒸馏为多模态推理器提供密集监督，但其轨迹级奖励无法判断失败答案源于感知错误还是后续推理错误。感知成功率 (PSR) 因低成功率混淆了感知不足与推理难度而存在歧义，需要一种能识别可纠正感知失败的方法。

Method: 提出 Perception-Correction Distillation (PCD)，利用下游失败与教师-学生分歧作为互补证据，其乘积构成软 AND 门，仅在两个证据同时存在时增强蒸馏。通过贝叶斯证据组合进行动机推导，证明乘法是唯一在任一证据缺失时消失的归一化双线性门。PCD 采用分离的感知-推理 rollout 和均值保持权重，保持推理目标不变。

Result: 在八个基准上，PCD 将 8B→2B 宏平均从 OPD 的 44.50 提升到 47.28，32B→8B 从 56.94 提升到 61.22。在匹配的 2B 消融中，移除 PCD 和分离 rollout 分别使留出平均下降 2.22 和 0.88 分。

Conclusion: 有效的多模态蒸馏不仅取决于教师预测什么，还取决于识别何时感知是合适的纠正目标。PCD 通过分离感知与推理并利用互补证据，显著提升了多模态推理器的蒸馏效果。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Perception-Correction Distillation (PCD)，一种用于多模态推理器的蒸馏/信用分配方法，通过感知与推理分离的 rollout 和教师-学生分歧来改进 on-policy 蒸馏。这属于多模态对齐与蒸馏范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐 (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Solid distillation method for multimodal reasoners, but tangential to the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: multimodal distillation, perception failure credit assignment, on-policy distillation, 8B→2B and 32B→8B gains, label-free method

Abstract: On-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multimodal reasoners, but its trajectory-level reward cannot determine whether a failed answer arose from perception or subsequent reasoning. Perception Success Rate (PSR), estimated from multiple reasonings sharing one perception, remains ambiguous because low success conflates perceptual insufficiency with reasoning difficulty. We introduce \textbf{Perception-Correction Distillation (PCD)}, a label-free method that identifies correctable perception failures using downstream failure and teacher--student disagreement as complementary witnesses. Their product, , forms a soft AND gate that strengthens distillation only when both witnesses are present. We motivate this rule through Bayesian evidence combination and show that multiplication is the unique normalized bilinear gate that vanishes when either witness is absent. PCD uses separated perception--reasoning rollouts and mean-preserving weights, leaving the reasoning objective unchanged. Across eight benchmarks, PCD improves the 8B 2B macro average from 44.50 with OPD to 47.28 and the 32B 8B result from 56.94 to 61.22. In matched 2B ablations, removing PCD and separated rollout reduces held-out average by 2.22 and 0.88 points, respectively. Effective multimodal distillation therefore depends not only on what the teacher predicts, but also on identifying when perception is the appropriate target of correction.

</details>
