### [60] [Position, Not Provenance: Separating Reasoning Mediation from Sycophancy in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27304)
*Supratik Bhowal, Subhrajyoti Basu, Aritra Gir Mahanta, Anik Pal Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (44.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoT-Mediate 行为框架，通过扰动模型自身生成的推理中单个临床属性，并测量预测是否跟随编辑后的推理，来分离医学 VLM 中推理中介与谄媚（sycophancy）效应。在 LLaVA-Med 和 MedGemma 上各 1000 个 VQA-RAD 样本上评估，发现前缀强制续写比重新提示产生更高的中介忠实度，且上下文位置而非来源归属是决定模型是否使用生成推理的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在回答临床问题前会生成思维链推理，但推理是否真正因果影响预测尚不清楚。现有研究未区分推理中介（模型真正依据推理作答）与谄媚效应（模型仅因推理存在而附和），需要一种行为框架来解耦这两种机制。

Method: 提出 CoT-Mediate 框架，包含双臂协议（重新提示证据 vs 前缀强制续写）和来源受控干预（仅改变相同推理的归属来源），以分离推理中介与谄媚。在 LLaVA-Med 和 MedGemma 上各 1000 个 VQA-RAD 样本上评估。

Result: 前缀强制续写始终比重新提示产生更高的中介忠实度；来源分析揭示模型特定的顺从行为差异。移除视觉证据会增加对注入推理的依赖，而侧别（laterality）是最不被忠实跟踪的临床属性。

Conclusion: 注入推理的机制显著影响测量的忠实度，上下文位置而非声明的来源归属是决定医学 VLM 是否使用其生成推理的主要因素。

Classification reason: 论文研究医学视觉语言模型（VLM）中思维链（CoT）推理对预测的因果影响，属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM reasoning mediation, which is outside the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world model priorities. Signals: medical VLM, reasoning mediation, sycophancy, chain-of-thought, VQA-RAD

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before answering clinical questions, but whether this reasoning causally influences predictions remains unclear. We present CoT-Mediate, a behavioral framework that perturbs a single clinically meaningful attribute within a model's own generated reasoning and measures whether the resulting prediction follows the edited reasoning. Our framework combines a dual-arm protocol comparing re-prompted evidence with prefix-forced continuation, together with a provenance-controlled intervention that varies only the attributed source of identical reasoning to disentangle reasoning mediation from sycophancy. We evaluate LLaVA-Med and MedGemma on 1,000 VQA-RAD samples each. Prefix-forced continuation consistently yields higher mediation faithfulness than re-prompting, while the provenance analysis reveals distinct model-specific deference behaviors. Across both models, removing visual evidence increases reliance on injected reasoning, whereas laterality is the least faithfully tracked clinical attribute. These results show that the mechanism used to inject reasoning substantially affects measured faithfulness and that contextual position, rather than stated provenance, is the primary determinant of whether medical VLMs use their generated reasoning.

</details>


### [61] [RRM: Experience-Driven Reflective Retrieval Memory for Long-Horizon Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28156)
*Jingxiang Fan, Junbao Zhuo, Bochao Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RRM 是一个面向长时程多模态推理的反思式检索记忆框架，通过从历史任务轨迹中蒸馏可迁移的程序性检索知识，构建反思经验记忆，并配合生命周期管理机制减少冗余噪声，在 M3-Bench-Robot、M3-Bench-Web 和 Video-MME-Long 上超越现有 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态长期记忆智能体依赖外部记忆来克服长视频上下文限制，但多数方法只关注存储什么，而忽略了如何检索存储的记忆。当检索不准确或反复无法获得有用证据时，现有智能体缺乏从历史任务轨迹中诊断失败并调整未来搜索策略的机制。

Method: RRM 在实体为中心的多模态记忆图上增强反思经验记忆，从历史任务轨迹中蒸馏可迁移的程序性检索知识。反思经验记忆捕获跨任务可复用的搜索策略，与保留当前视频事实证据的情境记忆和语义记忆不同。RRM 将检索到的经验转化为查询级引导，而答案生成仅以当前视频新检索到的事实证据为条件。生命周期管理机制通过使用频率、复用反馈和时间衰减来调节经验记忆，减少冗余与噪声。

Result: RRM 在 M3-Bench-Robot、M3-Bench-Web 和 Video-MME-Long 三个基准上一致超越此前的最先进方法，验证了反思式检索记忆对长时程多模态推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 反思式检索记忆能够有效提升长时程多模态推理性能，通过将可迁移的检索策略与事实证据分离，并配合生命周期管理，可显著改善记忆检索的准确性与效率。

Classification reason: 论文提出 RRM 反思式检索记忆框架，用于长时程多模态推理（long-horizon multimodal reasoning），属于多模态推理方向，映射到 CANONICAL 子主题"多模态推理与思维链"。

Importance reason: Focuses on retrieval memory for long-horizon multimodal reasoning, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: multimodal reasoning, long-horizon video, retrieval memory, M3-Bench, Video-MME-Long

Abstract: Existing multimodal long-term memory agents use external memory to overcome the limited context available for long videos. However, most methods emphasize what to store rather than how stored memory should be retrieved. When retrieval becomes inaccurate or repeatedly fails to obtain useful evidence, existing agents lack mechanisms to diagnose failures from previous task trajectories and adapt future search introduce Reflective Retrieval Memory (RRM), a reflective memory framework for long-horizon multimodal reasoning. RRM augments an entity-centric multimodal memory graph with reflective experience memory, which distills transferable procedural retrieval knowledge from historical task trajectories. Unlike episodic and semantic memories that preserve factual evidence from the current video, reflective experience memory captures reusable search strategies across tasks. RRM converts retrieved experiences into query-level guidance, while answer generation remains conditioned only on factual evidence newly retrieved from the current video. A lifecycle management mechanism further regulates experience memory through usage frequency, reuse feedback, and temporal decay, thereby reducing redundancy and noise. RRM consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches on M3-Bench-Robot, M3-Bench-Web, and Video-MME-Long, demonstrating the effectiveness of reflective retrieval memory for long-horizon multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [62] [Thinking Once Is Enough: Intermediate-Layer Evidence Routing for High-Resolution VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27830)
*Zhongkuan Mao, Xianjie Liu, Tianyu Meng, Yidong Wang, Wenzhuo Zhao, Ronghao Xian, Yao Jiang, Fei Shen, Junfeng Fang, Yong Dai, Yi Zhang, Keren Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (77.55) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Thinking-Once，一种免训练、单次视觉编码的证据路由方法，通过重建中间层的问题条件注意力，将已编码的细粒度证据路由到后续层，无需额外视觉编码即可提升高分辨率 VQA 性能并降低内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 VQA 常被视为证据获取不足的问题，现有方法通过裁剪、重新编码或多轮搜索来反复获取视觉输入。作者指出该观点不完整：许多情况下细粒度证据已在视觉编码后存活于中间层路由窗口，但在答案生成前被稀释。因此无需重复获取视觉输入，只需路由已编码的证据。

Method: Thinking-Once 是一种免训练、单次视觉通路的证据路由方法。它在中间层路由窗口重建问题条件注意力，保留核心实体 token 与紧凑背景上下文，并将这些证据路由到后续层，无需额外视觉编码。

Result: 在五个基础模型上，Thinking-Once 一致提升或持平基线，V*Bench、HRBench-4K、HRBench-8K 平均分分别提升 +3.1、+3.0、+2.7，同时平均峰值内存降低约 4GB。在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上三项基准分别提升 +9.9、+4.6、+5.5，跨基准均值从 72.5 提升到 79.1。在 ZwZ-8B 上达到 82.7 均值。对比 11 个开源 HR-VQA 基线取得最佳或并列最佳成绩，相比 DeepScan 推理时间降低 97.2%。

Conclusion: 高分辨率 VQA 可以通过路由已编码的证据而非反复获取新的视觉输入来改进，这为 HR-VQA 提供了一种更高效、免训练的新范式。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦高分辨率视觉问答（HR-VQA）中的证据路由与推理优化，属于多模态视觉推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）。

Importance reason: Solid efficiency-focused VQA method, but only tangential to the user's core continuous-model and video-generation priorities. Signals: training-free, single-visual-pass, high-resolution VQA, evidence routing, memory reduction

Abstract: High-resolution visual question answering (HR-VQA) is often treated as a problem of insufficient evidence acquisition, where failing multimodal large language models must inspect images again through cropping, re-encoding, or multi-round search. We show that this view is incomplete: in many cases, fine-grained evidence has already survived visual encoding and become identifiable and influential within an intermediate-layer routing window, but is later diluted before answer generation. We propose Thinking-Once, a \textbf{training-free, single-visual-pass} evidence-routing method that reconstructs question-conditioned attention at this window, preserves core entity tokens and compact background context, and routes this evidence to later layers without extra visual encoding. Across five base models, Thinking-Once consistently improves or matches the corresponding base setting, increasing the average scores on V$^*$Bench, HRBench-4K, and HRBench-8K by \textit{+3.1}, \textit{+3.0}, and \textit{+2.7} points while reducing the average peak memory by about 4,GB. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, it improves the three benchmarks by \textit{+9.9}, \textit{+4.6}, and \textit{+5.5} points, raising the cross-benchmark mean from 72.5 to 79.1. With the ZwZ-8B base model, Thinking-Once reaches a mean score of 82.7. Against 11 open-source HR-VQA baselines, it obtains the best or tied-best score on all three benchmark averages and the best overall mean; for example, compared with DeepScan, it reduces V$^*$Bench inference time by \textbf{97.2\%} while improving the cross-benchmark mean from 77.8 to 79.1. These results show that HR-VQA can be improved by routing already encoded evidence rather than repeatedly acquiring new visual inputs. Code is available in the appendix.

</details>


### [63] [SpatialCLI: Learning to Reason With Spatial Tools, Then Without Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27703)
*Yang Zhou, Zixuan Huang, Sunzhu Li, Zhuo Yang, Chen Zhang, Shunian Chen, Caijun Yan, Jianyao Xu, Shunyu Liu, Weijie Fu, Peiliang Li, Xiaozhi Chen, Yuxiang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (61.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpatialCLI 是一个三阶段框架，教 VLM 先借助空间工具（定位、分割、深度、位姿）进行空间推理，再通过冷启动 SFT 与智能体 RL 学习工具使用，最后将成功的工具使用轨迹内化为模型自身能力。在 MindCube 上，Qwen3-VL-8B 从 29.3% 提升到 84.6%（带工具），内化后无工具仍达 73.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 VLM 能理解整体任务但常遗漏决定成败的视觉细节，而专用视觉模型能捕捉细节却无法转化为任务级决策。两者之间存在能力鸿沟，需要一种方法让 VLM 既能利用专家感知能力又能将其内化为自身能力。

Method: SpatialCLI 采用三阶段流程：(1) Call 阶段将专用视觉模型作为空间工具暴露给 VLM 以增强感知；(2) Learn 阶段使用冷启动 SFT 和智能体 RL 提升工具使用能力；(3) Internalize 阶段将成功的工具使用轨迹语言化，使模型内化专家感知能力。同时引入 SpatialCLI-Bench，一个包含 516 个样本的组合感知基准，覆盖定位、分割、深度和位姿。

Result: 在 MindCube 上，SpatialCLI 将 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 从 29.3% 提升到 84.6%（带工具），超过带工具的 GPT-5.6 Sol（72.1%）；内化后无工具仍保持 73.8% 的性能。

Conclusion: 通过"先借助工具、再内化能力"的渐进式训练范式，VLM 可以有效弥合通用推理与专家感知之间的鸿沟，在保留工具增强优势的同时实现无工具时的能力内化，为具身智能体的空间推理提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 论文提出 SpatialCLI，训练 VLM 使用空间工具进行空间推理并逐步内化专家感知能力，属于多模态空间推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链。

Importance reason: Strong spatial reasoning gains via tool internalization, but focus on embodied spatial tools is only partially aligned with your continuous multimodal/world-model priorities. Signals: spatial tool use, VLM reasoning, tool internalization, agentic RL, MindCube benchmark

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in embodied agents to interpret visual inputs, reason about spatial relationships, and make task-level decisions based on that reasoning. However, a fundamental capability mismatch remains: general VLMs can reason about the overall task but often miss the visual details that determine success, while specialist vision models can capture those details but cannot translate them into task-level decisions. In this work, we propose SpatialCLI, a framework that teaches VLMs to reason with spatial tools and progressively internalize the specialist perceptual capabilities they provide. SpatialCLI proceeds in three stages: (1) Call exposes specialist vision models as spatial tools to augment the VLM's perception; (2) Learn uses Cold-Start SFT and agentic RL to improve tool use; and (3) Internalize verbalizes successful tool-use trajectories to internalize specialist perceptual capabilities. We further introduce SpatialCLI-Bench, a 516-example benchmark for compositional perception across localization, segmentation, depth, and pose. On MindCube, SpatialCLI raises Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct from 29.3% to 84.6% with tools, surpassing GPT-5.6 Sol with tools (72.1%), while retaining 73.8% without tools after internalization.

</details>


### [64] [Sample More, Reflect Less: Self-Refine and Reflexion Lose to Repeated Sampling at Equal Token Cost, from 1.5B to 7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28576)
*Iliya Mirzaei*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 该论文通过设计实验系统比较了七种推理方法（自反思、Reflexion、Best-of-N 等）与重复采样基线在等 token 预算下的表现，使用 1.5B/3B/7B 模型和两个数学基准，发现没有任何方法在等成本下可靠优于重复采样，且所有自检类方法均显著更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理增强方法（如自反思、Reflexion、自我辩论）都会生成远多于单条思维链的文本，而生成更多文本本身就能提升准确率，因此无法证明方法的核心思想真正有效。Wang et al. (2024) 曾报告重复采样基线常能胜出，但缺乏置信区间和显著性检验，需要更严谨的实验验证。

Method: 设计受控实验：在 1.5B、3B、7B 三个开源模型上，对七个方法（自反思、Reflexion、Best-of-N、自我辩论等）在两个数学基准上各 150 题进行对比。统计所有生成的 token（包括批评、反思、辩论轮次和检查），将每个方法与其自身实测成本下的重复采样基线进行配对比较，使用 bootstrap 区间和多重性校正。

Result: 全部 36 组配对比较中，没有任何方法在等成本下可靠优于重复采样；10 组显著更差，全部是模型检查自身输出的方法；18 组自检类比较全部为负。Best-of-N 的多数投票在 1.5B 上比模型自选高 8.0 和 11.3 分，但在 7B 上仅高 2.0 和 1.3 分。Self-Refine 和强制 Reflexion 在 7B 上仍低于基线 3.6 到 10.1 分。Reflexion 在最小模型上从未触发重试，静默退化为单条思维链。

Conclusion: 在等 token 成本下，重复采样（多数投票）是比自反思、Reflexion 等自检方法更稳健的推理增强策略。模型越大，自检方法的劣势越小但并未消失。研究强调推理方法评测必须控制 token 预算并做显著性检验，否则会得出误导性结论。

Classification reason: 该论文研究语言模型推理方法（自反思、Reflexion、Best-of-N 采样等）在数学基准上的效果对比，属于推理时计算扩展与思维链相关研究，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链子主题。

Importance reason: Rigorous negative result on self-refinement methods, but only tangential to the user's core continuous/multimodal generation focus. Signals: repeated sampling beats self-refine, equal token budget, 1.5B-7B models, math benchmarks, self-inspection fails

Abstract: Methods that make a language model plan, criticise and rewrite its own answer, reflect on mistakes, pick the best of several attempts, or debate with copies of itself nearly all make it generate far more text than a single chain of thought. Because generating more text raises accuracy by itself, a gain over one chain of thought does not show the method's idea is what helped. Wang et al. (2024) reported that a simple baseline, sampling the same question repeatedly and keeping the most common answer, often wins once budgets are comparable, but gave point estimates with no confidence intervals or significance tests. We rerun that comparison as a designed experiment: seven methods, open models of 1.5B, 3B and 7B parameters, two mathematics benchmarks, 150 questions each. We count every generated token, including those spent on critiques, reflections, debate turns and checking, and compare each method against repeated sampling at its own measured cost. All 36 comparisons are paired by question, with bootstrap intervals and multiplicity correction. No method is reliably better than repeated sampling at equal cost anywhere. Ten are reliably worse, all of them methods where the model inspects its own output, and all 18 self-inspection comparisons are negative. The two kinds of self-inspection part company as models grow. Choosing stops hurting: taking Best-of-N's eight samples and just counting the most common answer beats letting the model pick by 8.0 and 11.3 points at 1.5B, but only 2.0 and 1.3 at 7B, no longer distinguishable from zero. Rewriting does not recover: Self-Refine and a forced Reflexion stay 3.6 to 10.1 points below baseline at 7B. Reflexion as published never triggered its own retry on the smallest model. It judged itself correct every time and silently became a single chain of thought. We release code, prompts, all generations, and our verification scripts.

</details>


### [65] [Not All Tokens Deserve Equal Credit: Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation for Long-CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27888)
*Qiangqiang He, Zhongheng Wu, ZiJian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (81.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究长思维链推理中 RLVR 的 token 级信用分配问题。通过反事实敏感性分析发现，privileged self-teacher 产生的似然偏移方向不可靠，幅度主要反映反事实敏感性而非学习价值。据此提出 CSCR 方法，对高敏感 token 降低信用并重新归一化 token 级优势，在长 CoT 数学推理基准上稳定优于 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 等无评论家方法将响应级奖励均匀广播到所有 token，忽略了 token 对最终结果的不等贡献；而 OPSD 假设 privileged self-teacher 的似然偏移编码了可靠的答案对齐信息。作者希望检验这一假设是否成立，并据此改进 token 级信用分配。

Method: 作者先通过反事实实验诊断：固定每条采样轨迹，在"正确"与"错误"两种相反结果条件下重新打分，观察 token 偏移方向。发现多数受影响 token 在两种条件下同向偏移、符号反转少、优化信号重叠大，且大偏移集中在可替换的表面形式 token 上。基于此提出 CSCR：对高敏感 token 降低信用，并重新归一化 token 级优势以保持原始信用预算与验证器决定的方向。

Result: 在长 CoT 数学推理基准上，CSCR 在相同策略更新次数下一致优于 GRPO 基线。消融实验证实：privilege 诱导的方向不可靠、适度降权最有效、过强调制会破坏优化稳定性。

Conclusion: privileged shifts 无法提供可靠的答案对齐方向，其幅度主要反映反事实敏感性而非 token 学习价值。基于反事实敏感性的信用重分配（CSCR）是一种简单有效的 GRPO 扩展，可提升长 CoT 推理的强化学习效果。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦长思维链（long-CoT）推理的强化学习优化，属于推理与思维链方向，映射到 CANONICAL 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL credit assignment for long-CoT reasoning, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: long-CoT reasoning, RLVR, GRPO, token-level credit, counterfactual sensitivity

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is central to improving long-CoT reasoning in large language models. Critic-free methods such as GRPO convert response-level rewards into advantages and uniformly broadcast them across tokens, overlooking their unequal contributions to the final outcome. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) instead provides dense distributional supervision by minimizing the forward KL divergence between an unprivileged policy and a privileged self-teacher, implicitly assuming that the resulting likelihood shifts encode reliable answer-aligned information. We test this premise by fixing each sampled trajectory and re-scoring it under two opposing outcome conditions, one asserting correctness and the other incorrectness. Most affected tokens shift in the same direction under both conditions, with few sign reversals and substantial overlap in the induced optimization signals. Large shifts also concentrate on highly substitutable surface-form tokens, whereas tokens carrying problem-specific reasoning content are less sensitive. These findings show that privileged shifts fail to provide reliable answer-aligned directions, while their magnitudes primarily reflect counterfactual sensitivity rather than token-level learning value. Based on these observations, we propose Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation (CSCR), a simple extension of GRPO that reduces credit for highly sensitive tokens and renormalizes token-level advantages to preserve both the original credit budget and verifier-determined direction. On long-CoT mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CSCR consistently outperforms GRPO baseline with the same number of policy updates. Targeted ablations further corroborate our diagnosis: privilege-induced directions are unreliable, moderate downweighting is most effective, and stronger modulation destabilizes optimization.

</details>


### [66] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen, Liang Lin, Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (78.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SVR 提出一种无外部验证器的多轮强化学习框架，让模型通过自验证（正确性判定+置信度）作为计算控制策略，在推理时自适应决定是否保留答案或继续细化，在七个数学推理基准上以平均 2.99 轮推理超越固定预算基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时计算扩展能提升语言模型推理能力，但统一预算在简单输入上浪费计算，而基于验证器的细化依赖外部反馈信号。作者希望让模型学会用自身自验证作为内部计算控制信号，实现自适应测试时计算分配。

Method: SVR 是无 oracle 的多轮强化学习框架。每轮模型同时生成解决方案、离散正确性判定和置信度分数；仅当判定为 Correct 且置信度超过阈值时保留当前答案，否则基于自身自验证继续细化。真实正确性仅用于构造训练奖励，不暴露给策略。使用 GRPO 在固定时域轨迹上训练，奖励促进解正确性、校准感知的自验证和可停止的正确状态；自适应停止仅在推理时激活。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准上使用 Qwen3.5-2B，SVR 达到宏平均准确率 0.563，平均仅需 2.99 个推理轮次。在完整系统对比中超过标准 GRPO、强多轮基线和固定预算的 oracle 引导分数反馈参考，同时比固定十轮推理使用更少的轮次。

Conclusion: 学习到的自验证可以作为有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配，无需外部验证器即可实现高效且准确的推理。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于语言模型推理时的自适应测试时计算分配（test-time compute），通过自验证强化学习提升数学推理准确率，属于推理时计算扩展与推理增强范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM math reasoning with self-verification, not on the user's priority continuous/multimodal generation or world-model directions. Signals: test-time compute, self-verification, reinforcement learning, math reasoning, adaptive inference

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [67] [Beacon: Knowing When and How to Perform Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28595)
*Qixun Wang, Yang Shi, Letian Cheng, Zhuoran Zhang, Yan He, Yuqi Tang, Qi Zhang, Xinlei Yu, Ruizhe Chen, Tianrun Xu, Yuanxing Zhang, Pengfei Wan, Haotian Wang, Xianghua Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (80.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Beacon 是一种 agentic 视觉推理模型，通过 Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward 和 Hint-Guided Capability Expansion 两个机制，在强化学习阶段鼓励 MLLM 根据任务必要性自适应调用工具，并强化其在最困难问题上的工具使用能力，从而提升整体性能、模式自适应性和工具真实增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic 视觉推理模型存在两个问题：模式自适应性（MA）有限，即模型难以判断何时真正需要工具；工具效应（TE）不佳，即工具在难题上的增益被简单问题上引入的错误所抵消。作者希望让工具使用真正提升成功率，而非仅仅配备一个复杂低效的推理范式。

Method: 提出 Beacon 模型，核心包含两个机制：(1) Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward，根据任务必要性鼓励自适应工具调用，避免不必要的计算开销；(2) Hint-Guided Capability Expansion，在强化学习阶段通过提示引导强化模型在最具挑战性问题上的工具使用能力。作者还提出量化 MA 与 TE 两个维度的分析方法。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，Beacon 在整体性能、模式自适应性（MA）和工具真实增益（TE）方面均取得显著提升，相比现有 agentic 视觉推理模型表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模工具使用的必要性与效果两个维度，并在强化学习阶段引入自适应奖励与能力扩展机制，可以构建更高效且真正受益于工具调用的 agentic 视觉推理模型。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Beacon，一种 agentic 视觉推理模型，通过强化学习实现自适应工具调用以提升 MLLM 在复杂任务上的成功率。这属于多模态推理与工具增强推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链。

Importance reason: Solid RL-based agentic MLLM improvement, but it is not centered on the user's priority continuous/multimodal generation or world-model directions. Signals: agentic visual reasoning, MLLM, reinforcement learning, tool use, mode adaptiveness

Abstract: The fundamental goal of agentic visual reasoning is to improve the success rate of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on complex tasks, rather than merely equipping them with a sophisticated yet inefficient reasoning paradigm. In this work, we rethink agentic visual reasoning through two key dimensions of tool use: Mode Adaptiveness (MA) and Tool Effect (TE). Mode Adaptiveness characterizes whether an MLLM can recognize when tools are truly necessary and invoke them accordingly, thereby avoiding unnecessary computational overhead while improving performance on challenging problems that require tool assistance. Tool Effect characterizes the actual impact of tool use: tools should extend the model's capabilities on problems unsolvable through text-only reasoning, while avoiding additional errors on problems that the model can already solve without tools. We conduct a comprehensive analysis to quantify these two properties and empirically reveal that existing agentic visual reasoning models exhibit limited Mode Adaptiveness, while the gains produced by tool use on hard examples are largely offset by the harm introduced on easy examples that the models can already solve. Motivated by these observations, we propose Beacon, a novel agentic visual reasoning model that achieves stronger overall performance, improved Mode Adaptiveness, and genuine tool-induced performance gains. At the core of Beacon are the Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward and the Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism in the reinforcement learning stage, which respectively encourage adaptive tool invocation based on task necessity and strengthen the model's tool-use capability on the most challenging problems. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the strong overall performance of Beacon and its substantial improvements in both Mode Adaptiveness and Tool Effect.

</details>

### [68] [When Does Explicit View Routing Work? A Controlled Study of Multi-View Graph-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27530)
*Xiao Yue, Guangzhi Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (42.15) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究多视图图-文本对齐（MV-GTA）中显式视图路由何时真正有效。作者使用确定性可验证文本段、隔离文本编码器、视图特定图头以及外部标签/RDKit描述符驱动的相关性，区分了架构通道化与语义路由。在BBBP和BACE数据集上，正确路由相比错乱训练在标签和属性nDCG上提升0.305-0.685；联合模型在拓扑/标签/属性nDCG上达到0.720/1.000/0.877，优于三个独立训练的Single专家（0.633/0.976/0.859）。但证据仅限于显式、外部锚定的标签和属性路由，未建立自由形式路由、一致三视图专业化或统计等价于专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图-文本检索通常将图和描述映射到单一嵌入，即使查询只关注某一语义方面（如类别标签或分子属性）。多视图头可以分离这些方面，但查询头的变化可能改变检索结果，即使错误的文本被发送到该头——这反映的是架构通道化而非语义路由。作者希望厘清在何种条件下这种区分可以被解决，即显式视图路由何时真正有效。

Method: 作者构建了受控版MV-GTA：使用确定性可验证文本段、隔离的文本编码器、视图特定图头，以及由外部标签或RDKit描述符驱动的相关性。通过正确路由与逐样本错乱（derangement）形成因果测试，判断检索是否真正依赖内容。在BBBP和BACE数据集上进行多组对照实验，包括联合模型与三个独立Single专家的三种子匹配比较、属性释义增强以及一致性和硬模板扩展。

Result: 在BBBP和BACE上，正确路由相比错乱训练在标签和属性nDCG上提升0.305-0.685；期望图头超过最佳错误头0.303-0.453。拓扑未在两个数据集上一致专业化。三种子匹配比较中，联合模型获得平均拓扑/标签/属性nDCG为0.720/1.000/0.877，三个独立Single专家为0.633/0.976/0.859。属性释义增强在未见模板nDCG上提升0.140和0.147。但一致性和硬模板扩展在某些设置下降低了规范检索性能。

Conclusion: 证据仅限于显式、外部锚定的标签和属性路由，以及观察到的多接口整合。该研究并未建立自由形式路由、一致的三视图专业化、与专家模型的统计等价性，也未证明下游预测的优越性。显式视图路由的有效性取决于外部可验证的语义锚定，而非单纯的架构设计。

Classification reason: 论文研究图-文本检索（graph-text retrieval）中的多视图对齐与路由问题，属于跨模态检索范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on molecular graph-text retrieval with negative keywords (molecule, graph neural network) and no overlap with priority subtopics. Signals: molecular retrieval, graph-text alignment, negative keyword molecule, negative keyword graph neural network, no continuous/multimodal/video/world-model signals

Abstract: Graph-text retrieval typically maps a graph and its description to a single embedding, even when a query concerns only one semantic aspect, such as a class label or molecular property. Multiple heads can separate these aspects, but a change in the query head may alter retrieval even when the wrong text is sent to that head. Such behavior demonstrates architectural channelization, not necessarily semantic routing. We examine the conditions under which this distinction can be resolved. Our controlled version of MV-GTA uses deterministic, verifiable text segments; isolated text encoders; view-specific graph heads; and relevance derived from external labels or RDKit descriptors. Correct routing and per-sample derangements form a causal test of whether retrieval depends on content. On BBBP and BACE, correct routing improves label and property nDCG by 0.305 to 0.685 over deranged training. The expected graph head exceeds the best wrong head by 0.303 to 0.453. Topology does not specialize consistently across the two datasets. In a matched three-seed comparison, one joint model obtains mean topology, label, and property nDCG of 0.720/1.000/0.877; three separately trained Single specialists obtain 0.633/0.976/0.859. Property paraphrase augmentation also improves unseen-template nDCG by 0.140 and 0.147 over a matched-exposure canonical control. Consistency and hard-template extensions, however, reduce canonical retrieval in some settings. The evidence is therefore limited to explicit, externally grounded label and property routing and observed multi-interface consolidation. It does not establish free-form routing, consistent three-view specialization, statistical equivalence to specialists, or superior downstream prediction.

</details>


### [69] [Gradient-free Task-Conditioned Retrieval for On-Device In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27766)
*Xinyu Luo, Hui Liu, Yihua Shao, Junyi Yang, Arindam Basu, Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.IR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (64.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CoRA 是一个无梯度的任务条件检索框架，通过闭式岭回归将冻结编码器转换为任务条件检索器，用于端侧设备上的上下文学习示例选择，并支持多模态示例检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧设备上的上下文学习（ICL）需要在推理前从本地记忆中检索示例，但受限于有限的计算、内存和数据暴露预算。现有检索方法难以在无微调、无反向传播的条件下利用任务特定信息进行有效检索。

Method: CoRA 框架：选择互补的编码器层，从候选记忆构建输出派生的条件空间，通过闭式岭回归将候选输入表示对齐到该空间；低秩分解产生紧凑的检索基，输出仅在离线索引构建时使用，查询时只需查询输入和预计算索引。还推导了精确的两遍流式构建方法，并扩展到多模态示例检索。

Result: 在十个文本数据集和四个多模态基准上，使用 Llama-3.2-1B、MobileLLM-Pro、OpenFlamingo-3B 和 Qwen3.5-2B 验证，并在 Raspberry Pi 5 上端到端部署，证明 CoRA 无需检索器微调、反向传播或目标模型调用即可实现有效的任务条件检索。

Conclusion: CoRA 展示了在资源受限的端侧设备上实现任务条件检索的可行性，其秩约束基是最优的低秩压缩，且流式构建避免了完整拟合矩阵的物化，为端侧 ICL 提供了高效且可扩展的检索方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出 CoRA 框架，将冻结编码器转换为任务条件检索器，用于端侧 ICL 的示例检索，并扩展到多模态示例检索。核心是多模态检索与示例选择，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on efficient on-device retrieval for ICL, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: gradient-free retrieval, on-device ICL, multimodal exemplar retrieval, closed-form ridge regression, low-rank factorization

Abstract: On-device in-context learning (ICL) relies on pre-inference retrieval to select demonstrations for useful context before downstream model inference. This retrieval must exploit task-specific information while operating over local memories under limited computation, memory, and data-exposure budgets. We propose Conditional Retrieval Alignment (CoRA), a gradient-free framework that converts a frozen encoder into a task-conditioned retriever using paired candidate inputs and outputs. CoRA selects complementary encoder layers, constructs an output-derived conditioning space from candidate memory, and aligns candidate input representations to this space through closed-form ridge regression. Low-rank factorization then produces a compact retrieval basis where candidate outputs are used only during offline index construction, whereas query-time retrieval requires only the query input and precomputed index. We show that CoRA's rank-constrained basis is the optimal low-rank compression of the output-conditioned fitted representation, and derive an exact two-pass streaming construction that avoids materializing the full fitted matrix. We further extend the framework to multimodal exemplar retrieval by incorporating visual representations into the conditioning and retrieval spaces. Experiments across ten textual datasets and four multimodal benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, MobileLLM-Pro, OpenFlamingo-3B, and Qwen3.5-2B, as well as end-to-end Raspberry Pi~5 deployment demonstrate that CoRA supports effective task-conditioned retrieval without retriever fine-tuning, backpropagation, or target-model calls.

</details>


### [70] [CXR-Retrieve: Compositional Text-to-Image Retrieval in Chest Radiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27779)
*Tomer Erez, Moshe Kimhi, Chaim Baskin, Ehud Rivlin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (60.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CXR-Retrieve 是一个面向胸部 X 光片的组合式文本到图像检索基准，包含 5,159 张 MIMIC-CXR-JPG 测试图像和 145 条涵盖单/多病理发现、正/负断言的文本查询。论文还提出标签感知对比微调目标，在 CXR-CLIP 基础上对两病理合取查询提升 Precision@5 8.5 个百分点，对否定查询提升 22.0 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型胸部 X 光档案难以检索，因为多数研究仅配有自由文本报告而非结构化临床标注。现有生物医学视觉语言模型主要针对报告到图像匹配优化，而非满足简短临床查询，导致目标不匹配：模型可能检索到与查询词语相关的图像，却无法满足完整临床约束，尤其是合取与否定（如"肺不张且无肺炎"）。

Method: 提出 CXR-Retrieve 基准，基于 MIMIC-CXR-JPG 官方测试集构建 5,159 张图像和 145 条组合式查询，相关性定义为检索图像是否满足所有断言的病理约束。同时提出标签感知对比微调目标，吸引具有兼容断言病理约束（包括共享确认缺失）的图像-文本对，并显式排斥矛盾对。

Result: 从域内 CXR-CLIP 检查点出发，该方法在双病理合取查询上比 CXR-CLIP 提升 Precision@5 8.5 个百分点，在否定查询上提升 22.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 可靠的胸部 X 光检索需要训练目标不仅建模查询中提及了哪些发现，还要建模这些发现是如何被临床断言的（肯定/否定、合取关系），这为医学影像组合式检索提供了新的基准与方法。

Classification reason: 论文提出 CXR-Retrieve，一个面向胸部 X 光片的组合式文本到图像检索基准，并引入标签感知对比微调目标。这属于跨模态/组合检索范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）子主题。

Importance reason: 医学影像检索与用户关注的多模态生成/世界模型方向偏离，且负向关键词命中。 Signals: 医学影像, 多模态检索, 负向关键词(medical diagnosis), 非生成任务, 非连续语言模型

Abstract: Large chest radiography archives are difficult to search because most studies are paired only with free-text reports rather than structured clinical annotations. Vision-language models offer a natural interface for text-to-image retrieval, but current biomedical models are primarily optimized for report-to-image matching rather than for satisfying short clinical search queries. This creates an objective mismatch: a model may retrieve images related to words in the query while failing to satisfy the full clinical constraint, especially for conjunctions and negations such as ``atelectasis and no pneumonia.'' We introduce CXR-Retrieve, a structured benchmark for compositional chest X-ray text-to-image retrieval. The benchmark contains 5,159 test images from the official test-split of MIMIC-CXR-JPG and 145 textual queries spanning single and conjunction findings, both positive and negative. Relevance is defined by whether a retrieved image satisfies all asserted pathology constraints, rather than by whether it matches a paired report. We further propose a label-aware contrastive fine-tuning objective for clinical retrieval. Our method attracts image-text pairs with compatible asserted pathology constraints, including shared confirmed absences, while explicitly repelling contradictory pairs. Starting from the in-domain CXR-CLIP checkpoint, our method improves Precision@5 over CXR-CLIP by 8.5 percentage points on two-pathology conjunctions and by 22.0 percentage points on negation queries. These results show that reliable chest X-ray retrieval requires training objectives that model not only which findings are mentioned, but also how they are clinically asserted.

</details>

*Bohan Hou, Haoqiang Lin, Xuemeng Song, Haokun Wen, Meng Liu, Yupeng Hu, Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 FiRE 方法，通过自动化的细粒度多模态五元组数据集构建流水线和两阶段细粒度多模态微调策略，增强 MLLM 在复杂图像检索任务（长文本到图像检索、视觉对话检索、组合图像检索）中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将 MLLM 作为通用图像检索器的工作忽略了细粒度上下文建模和分离式微调目标对检索性能的潜在提升，尤其在长文本到图像检索、视觉对话检索和组合图像检索等复杂任务上表现不足。

Method: 提出自动化细粒度多模态五元组数据集构建流水线，生成带细粒度图像描述和修改文本的 CIR 数据集；并设计两阶段细粒度多模态微调策略：(1) 细粒度上下文推理导向微调，增强模型上下文理解能力；(2) 细粒度检索导向微调，增强查询-目标对齐能力。

Result: 在五个涵盖多种复杂图像检索任务的数据集上进行零样本检索实验，FiRE 显著优于现有方法，且使用的 MLLM 骨干网络比对比方法更轻量。

Conclusion: 分离式两阶段微调策略和细粒度上下文建模能有效提升 MLLM 在复杂图像检索任务上的零样本性能，验证了细粒度上下文学习对通用图像检索的价值。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于利用 MLLM 作为通用图像检索器，涉及组合图像检索（CIR）、长文本到图像检索等复杂检索任务，属于多模态检索方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索 subtopic。

Importance reason: Focuses on image retrieval fine-tuning rather than core continuous/multimodal generation or world-model directions, with limited direct overlap with priority subtopics. Signals: MLLM fine-tuning, composed image retrieval, zero-shot retrieval, fine-grained context, two-stage tuning

Abstract: Due to their strong generalizable multimodal processing and reasoning capabilities, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated significant potential as universal image retrievers, effectively addressing diverse real-world image retrieval tasks. Nevertheless, pioneering studies, while promising, overlook the potential of fine-grained context modeling and disentangled fine-tuning objectives in enhancing MLLMs' retrieval performance, particularly for complex tasks such as long-text-to-image retrieval, visual dialog retrieval, and composed image retrieval (CIR). Therefore, in this work, we propose an automated fine-grained multimodal quintuple dataset construction pipeline and a novel two-stage fine-grained multimodal fine-tuning strategy. The dataset generation pipeline produces a comprehensive CIR dataset with fine-grained image captions and modification text, facilitating fine-grained context modeling. Beyond the previously entangled fine-tuning paradigm, our approach separates the fine-tuning process into two distinct stages: (1) fine-grained context reasoning-oriented fine-tuning and (2) fine-grained retrieval-oriented fine-tuning. These stages aim to sequentially enhance the model's context understanding and query-target alignment capabilities, thereby improving retrieval performance. Extensive experiments across five datasets encompassing diverse and complex image retrieval tasks demonstrate the remarkable superiority of our method over existing approaches in zero-shot retrieval settings, even with a more lightweight MLLM backbone compared to those methods.

</details>

*Yao Xiao, Reuben Tan, Zhen Zhu, Yuqun Wu, Jianfeng Gao, Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.AI

TL;DR: ReToken 是一个可学习的单 token 检索目标，从预填充的视觉 KV cache 中稀疏选择与查询相关的视觉 token，以缓解长视觉上下文中 VLM 性能退化与显存瓶颈问题，在图像和视频检索基准上取得显著提升。

Method: ReToken 训练一个单一的可学习 embedding 作为显式检索目标，从预填充的视觉 KV cache 中稀疏选择与查询相关的视觉 token。该设计轻量，仅需在小规模图像 QA 数据集上训练。

Conclusion: ReToken 通过轻量级可学习检索 token 有效解决了长视觉上下文中 VLM 的检索与显存问题，且具备良好的跨模态（图像到视频）零样本迁移能力。

Classification reason: 论文提出 ReToken，通过可学习检索目标从视觉 KV cache 中选择查询相关的稀疏视觉 token，用于视觉检索任务（Visual Haystacks、LVBench），属于多模态检索方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索 subtopic。

Abstract: Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at:

### [73] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao, Qingyun Sun, Haonan Yuan, Ziwei Zhang, Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (52.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DualG-MRAG 提出一种双层解耦架构，将多模态 RAG 的宏观推理（Macro Graph）与微观匹配（Micro Graph）分离，通过 GNN 检索器进行查询驱动的消息传递，并用动态规划解码从 GNN 前向传播中提取显式推理路径，替代孤立文档块输入，提升复杂多跳 QA 的证据召回与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MM-RAG 方法主要依赖独立的实例级匹配，难以捕捉跨模态和跨文档的显式关系；图增强方法虽引入结构建模，但在多模态场景中面临两难：细粒度视觉特征导致图快速膨胀和检索噪声，粗粒度表征则丢失关键局部证据。

Method: 提出 DualG-MRAG 双层框架：构建 Macro Graph 用于全局拓扑路由（隔离全局结构推理），Micro Graph 用于精确局部验证（细粒度证据匹配）；将检索建模为查询驱动的 GNN 消息传递过程（GNN Retriever），实现跨异构证据源的动态相关性传播；并引入动态规划解码机制，从 GNN 前向传播中直接提取显式推理路径，替代标准孤立文档块输入。

Result: 大量实验表明 DualG-MRAG 在证据召回和复杂 QA 准确率上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦宏观推理与微观匹配，DualG-MRAG 有效缓解了多模态图检索中细粒度特征带来的噪声与粗粒度表征丢失局部证据的矛盾，为多模态 RAG 的复杂多跳推理提供了更优的结构化检索方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出 DualG-MRAG，一种面向多模态检索增强生成（MM-RAG）的双层图框架，核心是跨模态检索与证据匹配，属于多模态检索方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal RAG with GNNs, which is outside your core priorities on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: multimodal RAG, GNN retriever, multi-hop QA, graph reasoning, no generation focus

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在牙科全景放射影像上的评估缺乏细粒度、临床可靠的基准，无法反映专家解读水平，难以评测模型在牙齿级诊断与推理上的真实能力。

Method: 构建 PanDent 基准：收集 9,524 张 OPG 影像，由经验丰富的牙医生成细粒度牙齿级结构化标注，并经口腔颌面放射科医生验证；基于临床定义报告逻辑构建与结构化证据对应的放射学报告，评测 MLLM 生成报告的语言连贯性与临床一致性。

Conclusion: PanDent 可作为评测 MLLM 牙齿级临床推理的严格基准，也是临床落地牙科 AI 的宝贵资源，表明当前 MLLM 在细粒度医学影像临床推理上仍有明显不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PanDent，一个面向牙科全景X光影像（OPG）的细粒度牙齿级多模态大语言模型评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: Domain-specific dental benchmark with no overlap with the user's priority topics on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: dental radiology benchmark, MLLM evaluation, negative keyword medical diagnosis, no autoregressive/video/world model focus, no continuous token/flow matching signal

Abstract: Accurate evaluation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in dental panoramic radiography (orthopantomogram, OPG) is limited by the lack of fine-grained, clinically reliable benchmarks that reflect expert interpretation. This work introduces PanDent, a large-scale, clinically grounded OPG benchmark built upon fine-grained, expert-validated tooth-level annotations. The dataset comprises 9,524 high-quality OPGs, each associated with comprehensive structured annotations produced by experienced dentists and further validated by an oral and maxillofacial radiologist, providing clinically reliable supervision for tooth-level diagnosis and reasoning. Clinically consistent radiology reports are constructed from expert-validated findings using clinician-defined reporting logic, establishing explicit correspondence between structured clinical evidence and free-text descriptions. This design enables evaluation of whether MLLMs generate reports that are not only linguistically coherent but also clinically consistent with expert-validated tooth-level findings. Experiments are conducted on diverse MLLMs, including state-of-the-art (SOTA) proprietary models, general-domain open-source models, and medical-specific models. Results show that current MLLMs can generate fluent reports, yet fail to produce clinically consistent descriptions, exhibiting substantial errors in fine-grained localization and tooth-level diagnosis. Fine-tuning on PanDent significantly improves structure-language consistency, substantially enhancing visual localization accuracy and diagnostic correctness, and bringing model outputs closer to expert dental interpretation. These results establish PanDent as a rigorous benchmark for evaluating tooth-level clinical reasoning in MLLMs and a valuable resource for clinically grounded dental AI.

</details>

*Simone Giano, Lorenzo Severini, Alessandro Galdelli, Adriano Mancini*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构建并自动验证大规模多模态灾难响应数据集的方法：从纯视觉的 Incidents1M 中恢复 10 万图像，用 Qwen3.5-4B 稠密模型和 Qwen3.5-35B MoE 模型生成高质量文本描述，并引入图像盲的 LLM-as-a-Judge（Qwen3.5-9B）验证管线，模拟学生模型在无数据蒸馏中的模态差距，评估显示两架构语义一致性高（78.65/100），且揭示保守标注行为（高精度 77.6%、低召回 46.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难管理等关键领域部署 VLM 需要高质量多模态数据集，尤其用于无数据知识蒸馏（DFKD）的知识迁移。然而现有数据集要么完全缺乏描述文本（如 Incidents1M），要么存在严重的图文语义错位（如 CrisisMMD），阻碍了跨模态知识蒸馏的可靠性。

Method: 从纯视觉 Incidents1M 中恢复 10 万图像，使用两种 Qwen3.5 架构（4B 稠密模型与 35B MoE 模型）生成高保真文本描述；引入图像盲的 LLM-as-a-Judge 验证管线（Qwen3.5-9B），通过故意向评判者隐藏原始图像来模拟学生模型在无数据蒸馏中的模态差距，从而验证生成描述能否为 DFKD 提供可靠的语义锚定。

Result: 在 173,179 个标签对上评估，两种架构间语义一致性高（78.65/100）；自动评估揭示保守标注行为，精度高（77.6%）而召回低（46.0%），最小化假阳性噪声，同时暴露了原始标注中潜在的人工标注不一致问题。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可扩展、经 LLM 验证的多模态数据集以及可复现的框架，用于推进跨模态知识蒸馏，为灾难响应领域的 DFKD 提供可靠的语义锚定。

Classification reason: 论文构建并自动验证大规模多模态灾难响应数据集（Incidents1M 的 10 万图像配文），并引入 LLM-as-a-Judge 自动评估管线验证图文语义对齐，属于多模态数据集构建与自动评测范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准。

Importance reason: Dataset construction and caption validation for disaster response is off-topic for your core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: multimodal captioning, LLM-as-a-Judge, data-free distillation, disaster response, Qwen3.5

Abstract: The deployment of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in critical domains like disaster management requires high-quality multimodal datasets, especially for transferring knowledge via Data-Free Knowledge Distillation (DFKD). However, existing datasets in this domain either entirely lack descriptive text, such as Incidents1M, or suffer from severe text-image semantic misalignment, such as CrisisMMD. In this work, we present a novel methodology to construct and automatically validate a large-scale multimodal dataset for disaster response. Starting from the vision-only Incidents1M, we successfully recovered 100,000 images and generated high-fidelity textual descriptions using two distinct Qwen3.5 architectures: a 4B dense model and a 35B Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model. To ensure the generated captions provide reliable semantic anchoring for DFKD, we introduce an image-blind LLM-as-a-Judge validation pipeline leveraging Qwen3.5-9B. By intentionally obscuring the original image from the judge, this evaluator accurately simulates the modality gap of the student model during data-free distillation. Our evaluation across 173,179 label pairs demonstrates a high semantic agreement (78.65/100) between the two architectures. Furthermore, the automated evaluation reveals a conservative captioning behaviour, characterized by a high Precision (77.6%) and low Recall (46.0%). This minimizes the false positive noise, while simultaneously exposing underlying human annotation inconsistencies in the original ground truth. This work provides a scalable, LLM-validated multimodal dataset and a reproducible framework to advance cross-modal knowledge distillation.

</details>

TL;DR: LayerRAG-Bench 是一个面向 Agentic 检索增强生成系统的跨层可靠性评测基准，覆盖 8 个企业领域、240 个任务、9 种故障场景、2 种契约模式，对 9 个模型进行 38,880 条任务级记录评测，提出分层评估原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic RAG 系统可能在证据、工具契约、授权或会话状态等不同层面产生看似有依据实则错误的回答，现有评测缺乏对这些跨层故障的系统性刻画与分层归因。

Method: 构建受控的跨层可靠性基准 LayerRAG-Bench，覆盖 8 个企业领域、240 个任务、9 种故障场景与 2 种契约模式，对来自 OpenAI、Anthropic、Gemini 的 9 个模型进行大规模任务级评测，并对比 schema 归一化等干预手段在不同层的修复效果。

Result: Schema 归一化将 schema-drift 成功率从 0.000 提升到 0.913，但无法修复陈旧证据、缺失工具输出、权限拒绝和错误会话上下文；仅基于 groundedness 的评估在陈旧证据和错误会话场景下产生大量假阳性。

Conclusion: 可靠性干预应被归因于其修复的目标层，而不应被误认为通用修复方案，支持分层评估原则。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LayerRAG-Bench，一个面向 Agentic RAG 系统的跨层可靠性评测基准，属于评测基准类工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on RAG reliability benchmarking, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: RAG benchmark, enterprise domains, reliability evaluation, no continuous language model, no video/image generation

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation systems can produce answers that appear grounded while failing at the evidence, tool-contract, authorization, or session-state layer. We introduce LayerRAG-Bench, a controlled cross-layer reliability benchmark with 8 enterprise domains, 240 tasks, 9 fault scenarios, 2 contract modes, and 38,880 live task-level records across nine models from OpenAI, Anthropic, and Gemini. Schema normalization raises schema-drift success from 0.000 to 0.913, but stale evidence, missing tool output, denied permissions, and wrong-session context are not recovered by schema normalization. Groundedness-only evaluation also produces substantial false positives under stale and wrong-session evidence. These results support a layer-specific evaluation principle: a reliability intervention should be credited for repairing its target layer without being mistaken for a universal fix.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨表情包是日益增长的多模态在线危害形式，其敌意常通过图像、文本、文化引用与隐含目标的联合解读传达。虽然高资源语言的仇恨表情包检测已有进展，但阿拉伯语仍未被充分探索，现有资源主要聚焦于宣传或粗粒度有害内容标签。

Method: 构建 AHA-Memes 数据集：5K 人工标注样本，采用捕捉仇恨类型（攻击策略）的细粒度多标签分类体系；另提供约 66K 银标签样本。基准测试涵盖文本-only、图像-only、晚期融合多模态模型，以及少样本 ICL 和开放/闭源 VLM 的零样本与微调设置。

Result: 建立了强基线结果，并揭示了文化背景相关的阿拉伯语仇恨表情包检测中的关键挑战。

Conclusion: AHA-Memes 填补了阿拉伯语仇恨表情包细粒度基准的空白，为后续研究提供数据集、标注指南与评测脚本，并指出文化语境在仇恨检测中的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AHA-Memes，一个大规模阿拉伯语仇恨表情包细粒度多标签基准数据集，并评测了多种 VLM 与多模态模型。这属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: A solid benchmark paper for Arabic hateful memes, but it falls outside the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: Arabic hateful memes, multimodal benchmark, fine-grained hate taxonomy, VLM evaluation, low overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Hateful memes are a growing form of multimodal online harm, where hostile intent is often conveyed through the joint interpretation of images, text, cultural references, and implicit targets. While hateful meme detection has advanced in high-resource languages, Arabic remains underexplored, with existing meme resources focusing mainly on propaganda or coarse harmful-content labels. We introduce AHA-Memes (Arabic HAteful Memes), which is, to our knowledge, the first large-scale Arabic hateful meme benchmark with fine-grained, multi-label annotations. The dataset includes 5K manually annotated memes using a taxonomy that captures hate types, i.e., attack strategies. We further provide ~66K silver-labeled memes to support future studies. We benchmark text-only, image-only, and late-fusion multimodal models, as well as few-shot in-context learning (ICL) and open- and closed-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under zero-shot and fine-tuning settings. Our results establish strong baselines and highlight key challenges in culturally grounded Arabic hateful meme detection. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and evaluation scripts to support future research. WARNING: This paper contains examples that may be disturbing to readers.

</details>

*Kaiyu Li, Zepeng Xin, Zixuan Jiang, Jing Fu, Lanxuan Xue, Lingyu Zhang, Xiangyong Cao*

TL;DR: OVEarth-Bench 是一个面向开放词汇地球观测（EO）的评测基准，通过扩展类别广度（层级类别覆盖与正负表达）和查询多样性（词汇、指代、推理查询），在统一零样本协议下支持掩码与框定位，系统评估了通用与 EO 专用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇地球观测基准通常只覆盖狭窄的类别词汇或有限的查询形式，难以真实反映模型在多样化地理空间概念定位上的能力，需要更全面、更真实的评测基准。

Method: 构建 OVEarth-Bench 基准，从类别广度（层级类别覆盖、正负表达）与查询多样性（词汇、指代、推理查询）两个维度扩展评测，并在统一零样本协议下支持掩码与框定位，评估多种通用与 EO 专用方法。

Result: 评测发现：当前方法性能仍有限，更广的类别覆盖带来更稳定的模型排名；MLLM 方法整体表现最强；EO 专用方法普遍不如通用模型，很少能匹配最强方法。

Conclusion: 研究为未来开放词汇 EO 方法设计提供指导，强调开发更真实、多样、高质量和大规模基准对可靠评测的重要性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出开放词汇地球观测评测基准 OVEarth-Bench，属于多模态/视觉语言评测基准类工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Benchmark paper on remote sensing, which is a negative keyword, with limited direct relevance to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: remote sensing, open-vocabulary benchmark, zero-shot evaluation, MLLM evaluation, negative keyword match

Abstract: Open-vocabulary Earth observation (EO) aims to localize geospatial concepts specified in natural language rather than a fixed label set. Existing benchmarks, however, usually cover narrow category vocabularies or limited query forms. To fill this gap, we introduce OVEarth-Bench, which extends existing evaluation in two directions: category breadth, through broad hierarchical category coverage with positive and negative expressions, and query diversity, through vocabulary, referring, and reasoning queries. The benchmark supports mask and box localization under a unified zero-shot protocol. We evaluate a broad set of general and EO-specific methods. The evaluation reveals that: (1) the performance of current methods remains limited, while broader category coverage yields more stable model rankings; (2) MLLM-based methods achieve the strongest overall performance; and (3) EO-specific methods generally underperform general models and rarely match the strongest methods. These findings provide guidance for future open-vocabulary EO method design and highlight the importance of developing more realistic, diverse, high-quality, and large-scale benchmarks for reliable evaluation. Our data and evaluation package are released at .

</details>


### [79] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen, Xirui Shi, Yuyao Wang, Jerry Chen, Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PAUSE 是一个用户中心的基准，用于在状态化、服务集成的环境中评估个人 AI 助手。它要求智能体跨异构用户资源协调动作，同时保持与环境状态一致并遵守授权约束，采用多机制评估框架，并引入用户模拟实现显式交互评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准往往碎片化服务上下文或抽象掉用户状态，难以在真实服务场景中评估用户中心的个人助手行为。需要一个新的基准来捕捉真实部署的核心挑战，包括持久用户状态推理、用户特定配置与权限、跨多服务的长时间约束感知交互。

Method: PAUSE 基准要求智能体在状态化、服务集成的环境中协调跨异构用户资源的动作，保持与环境状态一致并遵守多轮交互的授权约束。采用多机制评估框架：开放式的服务管理任务用语义和轨迹级行为指标评估，约束密集型任务用确定性的基于状态的验证。同时引入用户模拟实现显式用户-智能体交互，并提供用户中心合成管线以规模化生成连贯的服务环境、用户配置和带标注任务。

Result: 即使最先进的专有模型在需要状态化推理和配置感知的场景中任务完成率也未能达到 70%，揭示了一致且可解释的失败模式。

Conclusion: PAUSE 揭示了现有 SOTA 模型在状态化推理和配置感知方面的显著不足，其多机制评估框架和用户中心合成管线为可扩展、可复现的助手评测提供了基础，支持基准扩展与未来研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PAUSE，一个用于评估个人 AI 助手在统一服务环境中行为的用户中心基准，属于多模态/智能体评测基准范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: A benchmark for personal AI assistants, but it is tangential to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: benchmark, personal AI assistant, user simulation, stateful environments, multi-regime evaluation

Abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [80] [Can LVLMs Uncover the Truth Behind Visual Illusions? An Analysis of Perceptual and Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27747)
*Liangjie Zhao, Jiaqing Lyu, Kexin Tang, Zecheng Fang, Rong Yin, Yulan Hu, Da Li, Jianing Li*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成
