TL;DR: 本文提出利用视觉错觉作为诊断工具来评测大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的感知与推理联合能力，构建了 IllusionReasoning 基准，包含从真实世界收集的错觉图像及多样化的问答对，实验表明多种 LVLMs 的推理能力并未如宣称的那样先进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 评测要么只关注感知，要么依赖数学或编程等特定领域，缺乏与开放世界环境对齐、同时考虑感知与推理的评测。作者希望利用视觉错觉这一人类视觉系统会误解客观信号的现象，作为诊断工具来联合评测 LVLMs 的感知与推理能力。

Method: 构建 IllusionReasoning 基准，从真实世界收集视觉错觉图像，并标注多样化的问答对。基于该基准对多种 LVLMs 进行评测，考察其在视觉错觉场景下的感知与推理能力。

Result: 实验表明，多种 LVLMs 的推理能力并未如宣称的那样先进，在视觉错觉场景下表现有限。

Conclusion: 视觉错觉可作为评测 LVLMs 感知与推理联合能力的有效诊断工具，为理解 LVLMs 的局限和未来优化方向提供了新见解。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了 IllusionReasoning 基准，利用视觉错觉图像评测 LVLMs 的感知与推理能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: 视觉错觉评测虽新颖，但偏离用户核心的连续多模态与生成方向，且未涉及模型效率或世界模型。 Signals: 视觉错觉基准, LVLM感知与推理评测, 多模态评测, 非生成任务, 无连续语言模型或视频生成关联

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models have integrated reasoning capabilities, elevating cognitive performance to new levels. However, existing evaluations either focus solely on perception or rely on specific domains such as maths or coding. Evaluation for reasoning capabilities that align with an open-world environment is still required, especially one that considers perception and reasoning jointly. To bridge this gap, we propose to evaluate LVLMs by exploiting visual illusions as a diagnostic tool. Visual illusions are phenomena in which the human visual system misinterprets objective signals, resulting in an understanding that deviates from reality. We constructed IllusionReasoning, a benchmark of illusion images collected from the real world, incorporating diverse annotated question-answer pairs. Based on IllusionReasoning, we show that the reasoning capabilities of a wide range of LVLMs are not as advanced as claimed. Our work provides new insights into LVLMs and offers future direction for optimisation.

</details>


### [81] [MMOOC: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Out-of-Context Evaluation in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27637)
*Wenjie Zhu, Yabin Zhang, Wenjun Zeng, Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MMOOC 是一个大规模基准，包含超过 41K 图像-问题对，用于评测多模态大语言模型在上下文偏移场景下的拒绝能力与稳健回答能力，涵盖可回答的 Shifted IC 与不可回答的 OOC 问题，跨越三种问题格式、八种偏移类型和六种视觉场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在上下文偏移或非理想场景下表现不佳，而现有基准主要关注 OOC 或视觉上不可回答的问题，忽略了可回答的 Shifted IC 情况，且覆盖的 OOC 偏移类型有限。需要一个更全面的基准来评测模型在偏移上下文下的拒绝与稳健回答能力。

Method: 构建 MMOOC 大规模基准，包含 41K+ 图像-问题对，涵盖可回答的 Shifted IC 与不可回答的 OOC 两类问题，跨越三种问题格式、八种偏移类型和六种视觉场景。通过 MLLM 过滤与人工验证保证数据质量，使用 Accuracy 和 Refusal Rate 评估模型响应，并引入 LLM-as-a-Judge 指标评估推理正确性。

Result: 在多种 MLLM 上的实验表明，当前模型在偏移上下文下仍难以在可回答性与拒绝之间取得平衡。进一步分析了关键失败模式，并证明后训练（post-training）可以提升模型的稳健性。

Conclusion: MMOOC 揭示了现有 MLLM 在上下文偏移场景下的不足，为评测和提升模型的拒绝能力与稳健回答能力提供了全面的基准，并表明后训练是改善模型鲁棒性的有效途径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MMOOC 基准，用于评测 MLLM 在上下文偏移（out-of-context）场景下的拒绝与稳健回答能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Benchmark paper on refusal/robustness in MLLMs, but tangential to the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: benchmark, MLLM evaluation, out-of-context, refusal rate, robustness

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks, but often fail under imperfect or shifted contexts. A reliable MLLM should refuse truly out-of-context (OOC) questions with subject-level context shifts while still answering shifted in-context (Shifted IC) questions with non-subject context shifts. Existing benchmarks mainly target OOC or visually unanswerable questions, but overlook answerable Shifted IC cases and cover limited OOC shifts. To fill this gap, we present MMOOC, a large-scale benchmark for evaluating refusal and robust answering abilities of MLLMs. MMOOC contains over 41K image-question pairs, including answerable Shifted IC cases and unanswerable OOC cases, spanning three question formats, eight shift types and six visual scenarios, with data quality ensured through MLLM-based filtering and human verification. We evaluate model responses using Accuracy and Refusal Rate, and further introduce an LLM-as-a-Judge metric to assess the correctness of model reasoning. Experiments on diverse MLLMs show that current models still struggle to balance answer-ability and refusal under shifted contexts. We further analyze key failure patterns and show that post-training can improve robustness. MMOOC will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [82] [JigShape: Evaluating Visual-Geometric Reasoning in VLMs through Jigsaw Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27670)
*Shawn Li, Wei Yang, Jike Zhong, Jiate Li, Jiawei Yang, You Qin, Ryan Rossi, Franck Dernoncourt, Roger Zimmermann, Yue Wang, Zhengzhong Tu, Vicente Ordonez, Mohit Bansal, Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (58.1) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: JigShape 是一个基于拼图游戏的视觉-几何推理评测基准，使用榫卯互锁拼图块消除纹理重复区域的歧义，在 95K 实例上评测发现零样本 VLM 大多缺乏几何推理能力，且所有模型在更大网格上出现"缩放悬崖"式性能崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拼图类基准使用矩形切割，在纹理重复区域会产生歧义的真实标注，无法可靠评测 VLM 的几何推理能力。作者希望设计一个具有明确几何约束、无歧义真实标注的基准，以揭示当前 VLM 在视觉-几何联合推理上的真实水平。

Method: 设计榫卯互锁（tab-and-blank interlocking）拼图块，使几何约束提供强局部兼容性要求，结合视觉内容得到无歧义真实标注。构建 95K 实例，覆盖 4×4 到 16×16 四种网格密度，对五个前沿 VLM 进行零样本评测，并对部分模型进行监督微调。

Result: 零样本下仅 GPT-5.5 在 4×4 拼图上超过随机基线，其余模型均处于随机水平；监督微调在 4×4 上可达 97% 以上，但所有模型在更大网格上崩溃：GPT-5.5 从 70% 跌至 8×8 上的近随机水平，微调模型在 12×12 上低于 5%。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 架构无法在拼图块数量增加时保持一致的约束满足能力，存在明显的"缩放悬崖"现象。可扩展的几何推理被确立为视觉语言模型面临的开放性挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 JigShape 基准，用于评测 VLM 的视觉-几何推理能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: A solid VLM benchmark for geometric reasoning, but it is a diagnostic/eval work rather than a method advancing the user's core continuous/multimodal generation or world-model directions. Signals: VLM geometric reasoning benchmark, scaling cliff on larger grids, zero-shot VLMs near chance, supervised fine-tuning fails at scale, multimodal understanding evaluation

Abstract: Jigsaw puzzle solving requires jointly reasoning about visual content and geometric constraints, yet existing benchmarks use rectangular cuts that create ambiguous ground truth in texture-repeated regions. We introduce \textit{\ours{}}, a benchmark with tab-and-blank interlocking pieces where geometric constraints provide strong local compatibility requirements that, combined with visual content, yield unambiguous ground truth. Across 95K instances at four grid densities (4$\times$4 to 16$\times$16), we find that \textbf{zero-shot VLMs largely lack geometric reasoning}: only one of five frontier models (GPT-5.5) exceeds random baseline on 4$\times$4 puzzles, while all others perform at chance level. While supervised fine-tuning achieves $>$97\% on 4$\times$4, \textbf{all models collapse on larger grids}: GPT-5.5 drops from 70\% to near-random on 8$\times$8, and even fine-tuned models fall below 5\% on 12$\times$12. This ``scaling cliff'' suggests current architectures cannot maintain consistent constraint satisfaction as the number of pieces increases. \ours{} establishes scalable geometric reasoning as an open challenge for vision-language models.

</details>


### [83] [Automated Transcript Analysis for Detecting Flaws in Agentic Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27518)
*Jeff Mohl, Nelson Gardner-Challis, Magda Dubois, Harry Coppock, Benjamin Allan-Rahill, Kaelan Yim, Damian Sójka, James Mann, Justin Olive*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出用 AI 扫描器自动检测 agentic benchmark 中的四类有效性缺陷（ground truth 访问、工具失败、猜测漏洞、答案格式歧义），并在 Inspect Evals 基准上对照人工标注进行验证，在五个广泛使用的基准中发现了若干质量问题，作为自动化审计基准质量的可行性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型能力常通过 agentic benchmark 评估，但基准必须准确测量其声称的能力且无失效缺陷。此前对 SWE-Bench-Verified 等基准的人工审计发现了若干有效性问题，但人工审查难以规模化，且不清楚自动化方法能否可靠地发现损害基准有效性的缺陷。

Method: 开发了四类 AI 扫描器分别检测 ground truth 访问、工具失败、猜测漏洞和答案格式歧义四类有效性问题；为每类问题制定评分标准以指导人工标注，并在 Inspect Evals 基准的留出测试集上对照人工标签评估扫描器性能。

Result: 扫描器在五个广泛使用的基准中识别出若干经验证的质量问题，包括随机人工检查难以发现的案例。但并非所有案例都被识别，扫描器性能在不同基准、标准和模型间差异显著。

Conclusion: 该工作作为使用自动化 transcript 分析审计基准质量的可行性证明。同时指出若干开放挑战，包括评测领域更广泛的标准缺失会降低扫描器性能，需进一步改进以支撑更强的质量保证声明。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于 agentic benchmark 的自动化质量审计，检测基准中的有效性缺陷（ground truth 访问、工具失败、猜测漏洞、答案格式歧义），属于评测基准与评测协议范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Focuses on benchmark auditing methodology rather than core model capabilities, with limited direct relevance to your priority topics. Signals: benchmark quality, agentic evaluation, automated auditing, validity flaws, cs.AI

Abstract: Capabilities of frontier models are often assessed using agentic benchmarks. To trust these results, benchmarks must accurately measure what they claim to and be free from invalidating flaws. Previous manual audits of benchmarks such as SWE-Bench-Verified have uncovered several validity issues in transcripts. However, manual review is difficult to scale, and it is unclear whether automated methods can reliably surface flaws that compromise benchmark validity. In this paper, we developed AI scanners to detect four types of validity issues: ground truth access, tool failure, guessing vulnerability, and answer format ambiguity. We produced grading rubrics for each to instruct human labeling, and evaluated the scanners against human labels on a held-out test set of Inspect Evals benchmarks. Our scanners identified several verified quality issues in five widely used benchmarks, including cases unlikely to be caught by random manual inspection. Not all cases were identified, and scanner performance varied substantially across benchmarks, criteria and models. We highlight several open challenges to be addressed to improve scanners for stronger quality assurance claims, including broader standardization gaps in the evaluation field that degrade scanner performance. Together, these results serve as a proof of concept for using automated transcript analysis to audit benchmark quality more broadly.

</details>


### [84] [LoMeVQA: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Longitudinal Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27806)
*Zhilin Wu, Zhangkai Ni, Chengmei Yang, Longzhen Yang, Yihang Liu, Ying Wen, Lianghua He*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (59.78) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LoMeVQA 是一个包含 206K 纵向医学视觉问答对的综合基准，覆盖进展分类、进展描述、进展报告生成、差异区域定位与差异区域描述五个任务，并引入 MedLong-8B 模型，在全部任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中患者常多次影像检查产生纵向数据，建模时序信息对疾病进展评估与治疗反应判断至关重要。然而尽管 MLLM 快速发展，纵向医学视觉推理仍鲜有探索，缺乏相应基准。

Method: 构建自动化流水线：(1) 按时间顺序组织患者记录；(2) 通过医学知识图谱提取临床相关实体；(3) 建模其时间演化以引导大语言模型生成高质量纵向 VQA 对。同时提出 MedLong-8B 模型以提升纵向医学视觉推理能力。

Result: 广泛评测表明通用与医学领域 MLLM 在 LoMeVQA 上表现不佳，暴露时序推理的显著局限；MedLong-8B 在全部任务上达到 SOTA。详细分析揭示了关键失败模式。

Conclusion: 纵向医学视觉推理仍是 MLLM 的重大挑战，LoMeVQA 提供了系统评测基准，MedLong-8B 展示了改进方向，并揭示了如何提升纵向医学视觉推理的洞见。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 LoMeVQA，一个面向纵向医学视觉问答的 206K 规模综合评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Medical longitudinal VQA benchmark is outside the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models, and hits a negative keyword (medical). Signals: medical VQA benchmark, longitudinal temporal reasoning, MLLM evaluation, MedLong-8B model, negative keyword: medical

Abstract: In clinical practice, patients often undergo multiple imaging examinations over successive visits, yielding longitudinal data. Modeling such temporal information is crucial for reliable assessment of disease progression and treatment response. However, despite the rapid advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), longitudinal medical visual reasoning remains largely underexplored. To fill this gap, we propose LoMeVQA, a comprehensive benchmark consisting of 206K longitudinal visual question answering (VQA) pairs for temporal medical image analysis. LoMeVQA covers five tasks: progress classification, progress description, progress report generation, differential region grounding, and differential region description. To construct the dataset, we develop an automated pipeline that (1) organizes patient records chronologically, (2) extracts clinically meaningful entities via a medical knowledge graph, and (3) models their temporal evolution to guide large language models in generating high-quality longitudinal VQA pairs. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that both general-purpose and medical-domain MLLMs perform poorly on LoMeVQA, revealing substantial limitations in temporal reasoning. To address these limitations, we introduce MedLong-8B, which achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks. Beyond benchmarking, we conduct detailed analyses that uncover key failure modes and shed light on how to improve longitudinal medical visual reasoning. Our data is available at:

</details>


### [85] [Benchmarking Foundation and Large Language Models for Few-Shot Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27856)
*Jinghong Liu, Yuchuan Deng, Fanping Liu, Meng Huang, Xirong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (72.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 FAME，一个统一评测基准，用于评估少样本医学图像分割（FS-MIS）方法，涵盖专家模型、SAM、CLIP 和 MLLM 四类范式，包含 14,958 个测试样本、7 个解剖部位、9 种成像模态和 14 个 ROI 类别，并在零样本与十样本设置下评估目标缺失识别及协变量/语义偏移泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本医学图像分割方法虽发展迅速，但现有方案跨越多种范式，且在不同且不一致的设置下被评估，导致其相对有效性不清晰，缺乏统一、全面的评测基准。

Method: 构建 FAME 统一基准，覆盖专家模型、SAM 类、CLIP 类和 MLLM 类四类 FS-MIS 方法，在零样本与十样本设置下进行评测，并额外评估目标缺失识别能力以及协变量偏移和语义偏移下的泛化性能。

Result: 评测发现：直接视觉适应通常优于基于提示的策略；只有当模型能有效利用支持样本时，增加支持样本数量才会提升性能；语义迁移比成像域适应更具挑战性，且强定位能力并不必然意味着可靠的目标缺失识别。

Conclusion: FAME 为 FS-MIS 提供了全面理解，揭示了不同范式方法的相对优劣，并指出当前方法在语义迁移与目标缺失识别方面的不足，为开发更有效、更可靠的少样本医学分割方法提供指导。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FAME 统一基准，用于评测少样本医学图像分割方法（涵盖 SAM、CLIP、MLLM 等范式），属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: Benchmarking paper on medical image segmentation, which falls outside your core research directions and is flagged as a negative keyword area. Signals: few-shot medical image segmentation, benchmark, SAM/CLIP/MLLM evaluation, negative keyword: medical, no continuous/multimodal generation focus

Abstract: Few-shot medical image segmentation (FS-MIS) aims to segment novel regions of interest (ROIs) from a few annotated support examples. Despite rapid progress, existing FS-MIS solutions span diverse paradigms but are evaluated under inconsistent settings, leaving their relative effectiveness unclear. We introduce FAME, a unified benchmark for evaluating FS-MIS solutions, covering specialists, SAM-based methods, CLIP-based methods, and MLLM-based methods. FAME contains 14,958 test samples across 7 anatomical sites, 9 imaging modalities, and 14 ROI categories, and evaluates models under zero-shot and ten-shot settings with additional assessment of target-absence recognition and generalization under covariate and semantic shifts. Our evaluation reveals several findings. First, effective few-shot segmentation depends on how models exploit support examples: direct visual adaptation generally outperforms prompt-based strategies. Second, increasing support examples improves performance only when models can effectively utilize them. Third, semantic transfer remains substantially more challenging than imaging-domain adaptation, and strong localization ability does not necessarily imply reliable target-absence recognition. We hope FAME provides a comprehensive understanding of current FS-MIS solutions and facilitates the development of more effective and reliable few-shot medical segmentation methods.

</details>


### [86] [EEG-EditBench: Probing Visual Information in EEG-Image Retrieval Models with Controlled Image Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27857)
*Kaifan Zhang, Lihuo He, Yuqi Ji, Junjie Ke, Lukun Wu, Tianhao You, Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EEG-EditBench 是一个诊断性评测基准，通过对 THINGS-EEG2 测试图像进行受控编辑（物体身份、属性、背景、物体存在性），评估八个 EEG 视觉解码模型在编辑后图像上的检索能力，揭示强标准检索性能并不总能迁移到编辑评测，细粒度属性变化最具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG-to-image 检索模型在语义多样候选上表现优异，但无法揭示模型究竟依赖何种视觉信息完成匹配。作者希望探究模型能否在受控编辑下仍区分目标图像，从而暴露聚合检索准确率掩盖的模型行为。

Method: 基于 THINGS-EEG2 的 200 张测试图像构建 EEG-EditBench，包含 2,137 个质量控制编辑，涵盖物体身份、属性、背景和物体存在性四类受控编辑，并评估八个代表性 EEG 视觉解码模型。

Result: 强标准检索性能不能一致迁移到基于编辑的评测，细粒度属性变化对模型构成最大挑战；EEG-EditBench 揭示了聚合检索准确率所隐藏的模型行为。

Conclusion: EEG-EditBench 为研究 EEG 图像模型保留何种视觉信息提供了受控基础，代码与数据集已公开。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EEG-EditBench，一个用于诊断 EEG-to-image 检索模型视觉信息保持能力的评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: EEG-image retrieval benchmark is far outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: EEG-image retrieval, diagnostic benchmark, controlled image edits, multimodal understanding, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent EEG-to-image retrieval models have achieved strong performance in identifying viewed images from semantically diverse candidates. Yet such success does not reveal what visual information supports the match. A model may readily identify a cheetah among tools, plants, and vehicles, but can it still distinguish the viewed cheetah from the same scene with the cheetah replaced by a dog? Motivated by this question, we introduce EEG-EditBench, a diagnostic benchmark that examines this question through controlled edits of object identity, attributes, background, and object presence. Built from the 200 THINGS-EEG2 test images, EEG-EditBench contains 2,137 quality-controlled edits and evaluates eight representative EEG visual decoding models. Our results show that strong standard retrieval does not consistently transfer to edit-based evaluation, with fine-grained attribute changes presenting the greatest challenge. EEG-EditBench reveals model behavior hidden by aggregate retrieval accuracy and provides a controlled basis for studying what visual information EEG-image models preserve. The code and complete dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [87] [MemeBench: What LVLMs Miss When Interpreting Culture-Dependent Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27798)
*Weihang Wang, Kainan Tu, Jielei Zhang, Run Yang, Boheng Sheng, Yuchen He, Yu Xie, Pengyu Chen, Peiyi Li, Huyang Sun, Longwen Gao, Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MemeBench 是一个针对文化依赖梗图解释的诊断性评测基准，包含 1,253 个中英文梗图，采用 VIKR 模式将解释分解为视觉线索、身份链接、知识单元和推理机制四个维度。评测 26 个 LVLM 后发现所有模型在覆盖可见内容方面都比解释所需的知识更可靠，最强模型仍存在 22.6% 的视觉-知识差距。论文还提出基于 CultureBase 的实体引导检索基线 KAR，可将 VIKR 成功率提升 3.6-7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梗图基准通常将解释简化为标签或整体评分，无法定位解释在何处失效。梗图的含义依赖文化实体、背景知识和社区惯例，而准确描述视觉内容并不等于正确解读。需要一个能诊断解释失败具体环节的评测基准。

Method: 构建 MemeBench 基准，包含 1,253 个中英文梗图，配有人工撰写的参考解释和质量控制的 VIKR 标注。VIKR 模式将解释分解为 Visual clues（视觉线索）、Identity links（身份链接）、Knowledge units（知识单元）和 Reasoning mechanisms（推理机制）四个维度。同时提出 KAR（entity-guided retrieval）基线，基于 CultureBase 知识库进行实体引导检索以辅助模型解释。

Result: 在 26 个 LVLM 上评测发现，所有模型对可见内容的覆盖都比解释所需的知识更可靠，最强模型仍保留 22.6% 的视觉-知识差距。KAR 在四个受控模型上将 VIKR 成功率提升 3.6-7.4%，相比通用检索修复更多答案且破坏更少。但两种检索条件都提升了身份和知识维度，同时降低了视觉覆盖。

Conclusion: MemeBench 能够揭示解释是否成功、缺失了什么，以及针对性证据能否填补诊断出的差距。梗图解释的核心瓶颈在于文化知识而非视觉感知，检索增强可以部分弥补知识缺口，但可能以牺牲视觉覆盖为代价。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MemeBench，一个包含 1,253 个中英文梗图的诊断性评测基准，用于评估 LVLM 对文化依赖梗图的解释能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: Benchmark paper on culture-dependent meme interpretation, only tangentially related to your core continuous multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: LVLM evaluation, VIKR diagnostic schema, cultural knowledge gap, retrieval-augmented baseline, multimodal understanding benchmark

Abstract: Large vision-language models have improved at describing visual content, but accurate descriptions do not ensure interpretation when meaning depends on knowledge beyond the pixels. Memes expose this gap because they rely on cultural entities, background knowledge, and community conventions. Most meme benchmarks reduce interpretation to labels or holistic scores, obscuring where an explanation breaks down. We introduce MemeBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,253 Chinese and English memes with human-written references and quality-controlled VIKR annotations, centered on anime, comics, games, and adjacent online subcultures. Its VIKR schema decomposes explanations into Visual clues, Identity links, Knowledge units, and Reasoning mechanisms. Across 26 LVLMs, every model covers visible content more reliably than the knowledge needed to interpret it, and even the strongest retains a 22.6% Visual-Knowledge gap. To test whether this diagnosis can guide improvement, we introduce KAR, an entity-guided retrieval baseline built on CultureBase. Across four controlled models, KAR raises VIKR Success by 3.6-7.4% and, compared with generic retrieval, repairs more answers and breaks fewer. Yet both retrieval conditions improve Identity and Knowledge while reducing Visual coverage in every comparison. MemeBench reveals whether an interpretation succeeds, what is missing, and whether targeted evidence fills the diagnosed gap.

</details>


### [88] [MMHBench: A Multi-Perspective Benchmark for Mental Health Understanding in Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27895)
*Jinpeng Hu, Erqiang Wang, Shan Wang, Zhuo Li, Peipei Song, Xun Yang, Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MMHBench 是一个面向长视频心理健康理解的多视角多模态评测基准，包含 268 个长视频和 2184 个问题，分为第三人称评估与第一人称视角推理两种设置，并采用多智能体问题生成框架合成多视角问题，评测了 22 个 MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理健康理解基准大多将任务简化为粗粒度分类，无法判断模型是否真正理解心理现象还是依赖表面相关性。作者希望构建一个能深入评估模型对心理状态多视角理解能力的基准。

Method: 提出 MMHBench 基准，包含 268 个长视频和 2184 个问题，分为第三人称评估（605 题，关注可观察行为与多模态证据解读）和第一人称视角推理（1579 题，需要视角条件推理）。提出 Multi-Agent Question Generation (MAQG) 框架，模拟多种社会角色合成多视角问题，通过多角色反馈与迭代优化精炼，并经专家引导验证质量。

Result: 对 22 个代表性 MLLM（涵盖开源与领先闭源模型）进行广泛评测，结果表明长视频心理健康理解仍极具挑战性。

Conclusion: 长视频心理健康理解对现有 MLLM 仍是高度困难的任务，MMHBench 提供了更细粒度的多视角评测框架，有助于推动模型对心理现象真实理解能力的研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MMHBench，一个面向长视频心理健康理解的多视角多模态评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: Benchmark for mental health in long videos is methodologically solid but far outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: long-form video benchmark, mental health, multi-agent question generation, MLLM evaluation, third/first-person reasoning

Abstract: Mental health understanding in long-form videos requires nuanced reasoning over observable behavior, interpersonal context, and latent psychological states. Existing benchmarks largely reduce this task to coarse-grained classification, providing limited insight into whether models truly understand psychological phenomena or rely on superficial correlations. To address this limitation, we introduce MMHBench, a comprehensive multimodal benchmark for multi-perspective mental health understanding, comprising 268 long-form videos and 2,184 carefully curated questions. MMHBench organizes the evaluation into two complementary settings: (1) third-person assessment, consisting of 605 questions that focus on the interpretation of observable behaviors and multimodal evidence, and (2) first-person perspective-taking, comprising 1,579 questions that require perspective-conditioned reasoning to identify the interpretation of the mental state supported by the available multimodal evidence. We propose a Multi-Agent Question Generation (MAQG) framework that simulates diverse social roles to synthesize questions from multiple perspectives. The generated questions are refined through multi-role feedback and iterative optimization, followed by expert-guided verification to ensure quality and validity. Extensive evaluation of 22 representative multimodal large language models (MLLMs), spanning both open-source and leading closed-source models, demonstrates that long-form video mental health understanding remains highly challenging.

</details>


### [89] [Scaling Vision-Language Models Is Not Enough to Mitigate Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28211)
*Ioannis Sarridis, Ioannis Kompatsiaris, Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 对194个公开VLM（16个模型家族、24个训练数据集）进行首次大规模实证研究，发现模型规模与性能的相关性在从ImageNet到单属性再到多属性偏差基准上逐渐减弱（ρ从0.68降至0.05），而训练数据属性与最差组准确率关系更一致，精选数据集在相近规模下可带来高达25%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（如CLIP）已成为多模态系统的基础，但其对虚假相关性的鲁棒性在大规模下仍缺乏系统理解。现有研究通常局限于单一模型或小规模对比，无法揭示模型规模、训练数据与架构选择对偏差鲁棒性的真实影响。

Method: 构建包含194个公开VLM的大规模评测体系，覆盖16个模型家族、多种模型规模、24个训练数据集，在ImageNet（整体性能）、CelebA（单属性偏差）和UrbanCars（多属性复杂偏差）三个基准上进行系统评测，并计算Spearman相关系数分析模型规模、数据属性与架构选择对性能的影响。

Result: 模型规模与性能的Spearman相关性随评测从ImageNet（ρ=0.68）到单属性偏差（ρ=0.48）再到多属性偏差（ρ=0.05）逐渐减弱；训练数据属性（规模与质量）与最差组准确率的关系更一致；精选数据集在相近规模下比非精选数据集提升高达25%；架构选择（patch size、图像分辨率）的影响高度依赖基准性质。

Conclusion: 仅扩大模型规模不足以缓解VLM的偏差问题，训练数据的规模与质量对偏差鲁棒性更为关键，架构选择的影响具有上下文依赖性。这为构建更公平、更鲁棒的多模态系统提供了实证指导。

Classification reason: 该论文对194个公开VLM进行大规模实证研究，评估模型规模、训练数据与架构选择对偏差鲁棒性的影响，属于多模态模型评测范畴，映射到[CANONICAL]多模态评测与基准。

Importance reason: 该论文聚焦VLM偏见评测，与用户优先关注的连续多模态、视频生成及世界模型方向关联较弱。 Signals: 大规模VLM实证研究, 模型规模与偏见缓解相关性弱, 训练数据质量影响显著, 多属性偏见基准, 非用户优先方向

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP are now foundational to multimodal systems, yet their robustness to spurious correlations remains poorly understood at scale. We present the first large-scale empirical study of 194 publicly available VLMs, including 16 model families, covering a wide range of model sizes, 24 training datasets, and three evaluation benchmarks, namely ImageNet (overall performance), CelebA (typical single-attribute bias), and UrbanCars (complex multi-attribute biases). Across these settings, the Spearman correlation between model scale and performance weakens as evaluation shifts from ImageNet ($\rho{=}0.68$) to single-attribute ($\rho{=}0.48$) and further to multi-attribute ($\rho{=}0.05$) bias benchmarks. In contrast, properties of the training data (size and quality) show more consistent relationships with worst-group accuracy across both bias benchmarks. Notably, curated datasets yield improvements of up to 25% over uncurated alternatives at a comparable scale. Finally, the effect of architectural choices (e.g., patch size, image resolution) is highly context-dependent, varying with the nature of the benchmark, including the type of bias and its spatial distribution within images.

</details>


### [90] [OSReward: Instituting Standardized Evaluation for Cross-Platform Computer-Use Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28609)
*Qiushi Sun, Kanzhi Cheng, Yian Wang, Bowen Yang, Hang Yan, Liheng Chen, Fangzhi Xu, Zichen Ding, Nuo Chen, Jialin Cao, Xingdong Gong, Zehao Li, Kaiming Jin, Xinfeng Yuan, Zhoumianze Liu, Jingyang Gong, Zhangyue Yin, Jiahui Gao, Zhiyong Wu, Tianbao Xie, Jianbing Zhang, Ben Kao, Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL, cs.AI

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: OSReward 是一个用于评测视觉语言模型（VLM）作为跨平台计算机使用智能体（CUA）轨迹评判者可靠性的基准，包含 OSReward、OSReward-Hard 和 OSReward-Multi 三个子集，并训练了开源奖励模型 OS-Shepherd（9B/35B），以低成本提供稳定可靠的奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）快速发展，但其轨迹验证（判断是否完成任务指令）是评估、数据整理和强化学习的核心。人工验证无法规模化，因此领域转向用 VLM 作为 CUA 轨迹的评判者。然而这些 VLM 评判者是否足够可靠这一根本问题长期未被系统研究。

Method: 构建 OSReward 基准：从多种智能体骨干在跨平台执行人工验证指令的轨迹中，通过多阶段人工标注获得 ground-truth 判定。衍生出 OSReward-Hard（聚焦困难案例）和 OSReward-Multi（细粒度效率与对齐评分）。基于此训练 OS-Shepherd-100K 语料并训练 OS-Shepherd（9B/35B）开源奖励模型。

Result: 对 VLM 评判者最全面的评测发现，即使最先进的模型也达不到理想评判者标准，存在系统性宽松偏差（将失败运行误判为成功）。少数可靠模型成本过高难以规模化，而廉价开源模型表现落后。OS-Shepherd 以比前沿模型低 30-60% 的成本匹配商业评判者性能。

Conclusion: VLM 评判者普遍存在可靠性问题，尤其是宽松偏差。开源奖励模型 OS-Shepherd 提供了低成本、稳定、可靠的奖励信号，为大规模 CUA 奖励设计提供了参考。

Classification reason: 论文提出 OSReward 基准，用于评测 VLM 作为计算机使用智能体（CUA）轨迹评判者的可靠性，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准。

Importance reason: Focuses on computer-use agent reward models, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: VLM judge reliability, CUA trajectory evaluation, reward model training, OS-Shepherd, benchmark construction

Abstract: Computer-using agents (CUAs) are advancing rapidly across the digital world. A CUA trajectory records the agent's actions, states, and reasoning. Verifying whether it fulfilled the task instruction is central to CUA evaluation, data curation, and reinforcement learning. Neither human-written verifiers nor human annotators can provide such verification at scale, so the field increasingly turns to vision-language models (VLMs) as judges of CUA trajectories. But a fundamental question has long gone unexamined: are these VLM judges reliable enough? To study it systematically, we introduce OSReward, a realistic, high-quality benchmark that evaluates VLM judges on CUA trajectories. The trajectories come from diverse agent backbones executing human-verified instructions across platforms, then rigorously labeled with ground-truth verdicts through multi-stage human annotation. Building on it, we derive OSReward-Hard, a challenge set concentrating genuinely hard cases, and OSReward-Multi for fine-grained efficiency and alignment scoring. The most comprehensive evaluation of VLM judges to date finds even state-of-the-art models fall short of an ideal judge, sharing a systematic leniency bias that mislabels failed runs as successes. The few reliable enough to trust are too expensive to run at scale, while affordable open models trail far behind. To close this gap, we construct and release OS-Shepherd-100K, an open corpus of reasoning-annotated trajectory judgments for the CUA community. On it, we train OS-Shepherd (9B and 35B), open reward models that supply low-cost, stable, and reliable reward signals, matching commercial judges at 30-60% lower cost than the frontier. Extensive analyses further inform the design of reliable CUA reward at scale. Our code, benchmark, dataset, and model checkpoints are available at .

</details>


### [91] [PathView-Bench: Can Multimodal Large Language Models Achieve Fine-grained Multiscale Understanding of Pathology Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28318)
*Zongyi Chen, Yu Liang, Jie Lin, Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathView-Bench 提出 PathVU，一个面向计算病理学的视觉锚定基准，用于细粒度、多尺度视觉理解评测。基于 23 个公开病理数据集构建，包含 14 个 VQA 任务、61,673 张图像、308,070 个样本，覆盖 28 个器官。评测 18 个 MLLM 后发现先进模型在细粒度多尺度病理视觉任务上仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理多模态基准主要评测最终诊断答案、描述或报告，无法揭示模型是否真正理解病理推理和决策所需的多尺度视觉内容。需要一个新的基准来显式评测 MLLM 在病理图像上的细粒度多尺度视觉理解能力。

Method: PathVU 从 23 个公开病理数据集构建，利用人工监督标签和空间标注，在 Region FOV（高分辨率局部区域）和 Slide FOV（宏观全切片视图）两个视野下评测 MLLM 理解。将原始标注转换为确定性任务目标，实现区域定位、视觉识别、数量估计、空间推理和上下文不足判断的程序化评分。包含 14 个 VQA 风格任务、61,673 张图像、308,070 个样本、28 个器官和 7,253,526 个标注。

Result: 评测了 18 个代表性通用、医学领域和病理专用 MLLM，观察到即使先进模型在跨多尺度病理图像的细粒度视觉任务上也存在显著局限。

Conclusion: PathVU 为开发和评测具有显式多尺度视觉理解的病理 MLLM 提供了可复现的基础，揭示了当前模型在病理细粒度视觉理解上的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PathVU 病理图像多尺度视觉理解评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: Pathology-specific benchmark with limited overlap with the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: pathology benchmark, MLLM evaluation, multiscale understanding, negative keyword: medical diagnosis, no generative or continuous modeling focus

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to analyze pathology images. However, dominant multimodal benchmarks in pathology mainly score final diagnostic answers, captions, or reports. These evaluations provide limited insight into whether a model understands the multiscale visual content needed for pathology reasoning and decision-making. We introduce PathVU, a vision-anchored benchmark for fine-grained and multiscale visual understanding in computational pathology. Built from 23 public pathology imaging datasets with human-supervised labels and spatial annotations, PathVU evaluates MLLM understanding in two fields of view: Region FOV for high-resolution local regions and Slide FOV for macro whole-slide views. By converting raw annotations into deterministic task targets, PathVU enables programmatic scoring of region localization, visual recognition, quantity estimation, spatial reasoning, and insufficient-context judgment. The benchmark contains 14 VQA-style tasks, 61,673 images, and 308,070 samples across 28 organs and 7,253,526 annotations. Evaluating 18 representative general-purpose, medical-domain, and pathology-oriented MLLMs, we observe substantial limitations even in advanced models on fine-grained visual tasks across multiscale pathology images. PathVU provides a reproducible basis for developing and evaluating pathology MLLMs with explicit multiscale visual understanding.

</details>


### [92] [How Benchmarks Mis-Score Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28367)
*Zihan Dong, Zhiyuan Ma, Zekun Wang, Yunqing Li, Zirou Liu, Ruixuan Deng, Qishi Zhan, Rui Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了计算机使用智能体（CUA）基准评测中评分不可靠的问题，提出一个涵盖任务构建、轨迹观测、评分与报告的可靠性框架，并审计了五个基准中的150条失败轨迹，发现15.3%的FAIL判定是错误的（10.7%为评估器假阴性，4.7%为损坏任务）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）正被部署用于浏览网页和操作桌面软件，但其基准分数仍常由脆弱的脚本化oracle产生，导致评测结果不可靠。作者希望系统化地识别和量化这些评测缺陷。

Method: 作者构建了一个涵盖任务构建、轨迹观测、评分和报告四个阶段的可靠性框架，并据此审计了来自五个web、企业工作流和桌面控制基准的150条公开失败评分轨迹，同时提出一个三层诊断分类法来区分真实失败的原因。

Result: 审计发现15.3%的FAIL判定是错误的：10.7%为评估器假阴性，4.7%为损坏任务。对于真实失败，验证/反馈和规划类失败占主导，而非执行/接地错误，单一标量成功率无法解释这些差异。

Conclusion: 作者将这些发现与更新的长时序CUA基准联系起来，并推导出针对CUA评测的分阶段设计规则，以提升基准评测的可靠性。

Classification reason: 论文研究计算机使用智能体（CUA）基准评测的可靠性问题，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on benchmark reliability for computer-use agents, which is tangential to your core interests in continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: benchmark evaluation, computer-use agents, scoring reliability, trajectory analysis, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUA) are being deployed to browse the web and operate desktop software, yet their benchmark scores are still commonly produced by brittle scripted oracles. A score is the output of a pipeline in which tasks can be stale, trajectories can omit decisive visual evidence, evaluators can reject valid alternatives, and aggregate reports can hide the cause of failure. We organize these problems into a reliability framework spanning task construction, trajectory observation, scoring, and reporting. We then audit 150 public failure-scored trajectories from five web, enterprise-workflow, and desktop-control benchmarks, find that 15.3\% of FAIL verdicts are wrong: 10.7\% are evaluator false negatives and 4.7\% are broken tasks. For genuine failures, a three-tier diagnostic taxonomy shows that verification/feedback and planning failures dominate execution/grounding errors, while a single scalar success rate can not explain. We connect these findings to newer long-horizon CUA benchmarks and derive stage-specific design rules for CUA evaluation.

</details>

### [93] [Calibrate Before Reason: Robust Visual Token Reduction against Semantic Drift in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27700)
*Jiasheng Li, Zhong Ji, Yan Zhang, Huihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (69.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 CaRe，一种无需训练的视觉 token 压缩框架，在压缩前先校准紧凑视觉表征以保持语义保真，避免语义漂移。剪掉 94.4% 视觉 token 仍保留 96.4% 全 token 性能，端到端推理加速最高 2.30 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 因长视觉 token 序列导致推理开销巨大。现有 token 压缩方法只追求效率，未检查压缩后表征是否与原始表征语义一致，可能丢弃、稀释或错配关键视觉线索，引发语义漂移，损害模型理解。

Method: 提出"先校准再推理"原则，CaRe 由两个互补模块组成：1) 扰动鲁棒校准锚定（Perturbation-Robust Calibration Anchoring），在多方向扰动下识别具有稳定模型侧影响的校准锚点；2) 置信门控 token 校准（Confidence-Gated Token Calibration），从未选 token 中提取可靠校准信号并注入锚点。整体为无需训练的框架。

Result: 在多种 VLM 架构和基准上验证，CaRe 优于 SOTA token 压缩基线。剪掉 94.4% 视觉 token 时保留 96.4% 原始全 token 性能，相对未剪枝 vanilla 模型端到端推理速度提升最高 2.30 倍。

Conclusion: 在视觉 token 压缩前进行语义校准能有效缓解语义漂移，在保持语义保真的同时实现大幅效率提升，为 VLM 高效推理提供了新的无需训练的思路。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于 VLM 视觉 token 压缩/剪枝以降低推理开销，属于多模态高效化与轻量化方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] efficient_multimodal 子主题。

Importance reason: Directly targets efficient inference for multimodal LLMs with a training-free visual token compression method, aligning strongly with your model efficiency and unified multimodal priorities. Signals: visual token reduction, training-free, efficient inference, VLM acceleration, semantic drift

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) suffer from prohibitive inference overhead due to long sequences of visual tokens. However, existing visual token reduction methods mainly improve efficiency by pruning or compressing redundant tokens without examining whether the resulting representation remains semantically consistent with the original representation. Mapping the original N-token visual sequence to K tokens may discard, dilute, or misassign critical visual cues, triggering severe semantic drift that deviates the VLM's understanding. In this paper, we first introduce the principle of 'Calibrate Before Reason' to visual token reduction and propose CaRe, a training-free robust framework that calibrates compact visual representations before reasoning to preserve semantic fidelity in VLMs. CaRe consists of two mutually complementary modules: 1) Perturbation-Robust Calibration Anchoring, which identifies calibration anchors with stable model-side influence under multi-directional perturbations; 2) Confidence-Gated Token Calibration, which extracts reliable calibration signals from unselected tokens and injects them into anchors. Extensive evaluations across diverse VLM architectures and benchmarks verify that CaRe outperforms state-of-the-art token reduction baselines. While pruning 94.4% of visual tokens, our method retains 96.4% of the original full-token performance, delivering up to 2.30 times faster end-to-end inference speed relative to unpruned vanilla models.

</details>


### [94] [LAST: The Last Query Token Guides Visual Token Pruning for Edge-Cloud Collaborative MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27952)
*Feng Yang, Xinrui Ju, Keyang Zhang, Xiandong Meng, Rongqun Lin, Howard Leung, Shiqi Wang, Haoliang Li, Chris Xing Tian*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (83.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LAST 是一种免训练的查询相关视觉 token 剪枝方法，用于边缘-云端协作 MLLM 推理。它利用边缘侧紧凑 VLM 作为引导代理，从最后一个查询 token 对视觉 token 的注意力中提取轻量重要性信号，在固定 token 预算下保留多样且查询相关的视觉 token，无需访问云端模型或自回归生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型将边缘-云端视觉智能转变为基于 token 的接口，但密集的视觉 token 序列增加了云端推理成本。现有剪枝方法要么是查询无关的（无法在传输前确定相关性），要么依赖目标 MLLM 内部状态（无法在传输前执行），而紧凑引导模型可能需要昂贵的注意力聚合或辅助生成。

Method: LAST 使用边缘侧紧凑 VLM 作为引导代理，利用因果注意力下最后一个查询 token 能关注完整视觉序列和全部查询上下文的特性，从该 token 对视觉 token 的注意力中提取轻量重要性信号，实现查询感知的剪枝，无需云端模型访问、自回归生成或多查询位置聚合。在固定 token 预算下保留一组多样且查询相关的视觉 token。

Result: 在 11 个多模态基准上、多种 token 预算下，LAST 一致取得最强性能，仅保留 12.5% 的视觉 token 即可保持全 token 精度的 95.4%，且边缘侧选择开销低、云端计算量减少。

Conclusion: LAST 证明利用最后一个查询 token 的注意力可以高效实现查询感知的视觉 token 剪枝，在边缘-云端协作推理场景中兼顾精度与效率，为分布式 MLLM 推理的 token 压缩提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出 LAST 方法，通过边缘-云端协作的视觉 token 剪枝来降低 MLLM 云端推理成本，属于多模态高效化与 token 压缩范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] efficient_multimodal 子主题。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with efficient MLLM inference and edge-cloud collaboration, though it is not directly on the core continuous-model or generation priorities. Signals: efficient inference, multimodal large language model, token pruning, edge-cloud collaboration, training-free

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models are reshaping edge-cloud visual intelligence from task-specific feature pipelines into token-based interfaces, where edge devices encode visual inputs into tokens for a general-purpose cloud MLLM. However, dense visual-token sequences increase cloud-side inference costs. Existing pruning methods mainly target centralized inference: vision-driven methods can operate before cloud execution but are typically query-agnostic, whereas query-guided methods often rely on internal states of the target MLLM and cannot determine token relevance before transmission. Compact guidance models offer an alternative, but existing designs may require costly attention aggregation or auxiliary generation. We propose LAST, a training-free framework for query-dependent visual token pruning in edge-cloud collaborative MLLM inference. LAST uses a compact edge-side VLM as a guidance proxy and derives a lightweight importance signal from the last query token's attention to visual tokens. Under causal attention, the last query token can attend to the full visual sequence and the entire query context, enabling query-aware pruning without cloud-model access, autoregressive generation, or costly aggregation over multiple query positions. LAST then retains a diverse set of query-relevant visual tokens under a fixed token budget. We evaluate LAST on 11 multimodal benchmarks under multiple token budgets against pruning methods with different guidance strategies. Experiments show that LAST consistently achieves the strongest performance, preserving 95.4% of the full-token accuracy while retaining only 12.5% of the visual tokens, with low edge-side selection overhead and reduced cloud-side computation.

</details>


### [95] [Capturing Token Tendencies for Training-Free Token Pruning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28341)
*Jie Ma, Zhike Qiu, Jie Gao, Jiayi Ji, Qian Chen, Xiaoshuai Sun, Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (86.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Trend-aware Pruning 框架，将 MLLM 视觉 token 剪枝从静态局部快照决策提升为时序轨迹建模，通过捕捉注意力流动的动量实现动态纠正，可选择性重新激活"晚开花"token，在减少 77.8% 视觉 token 的同时保持竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的视觉 token 剪枝方法依赖静态、瞬时启发式进行不可逆过滤，忽略了 MLLM 的分层特性——token 重要性在层间动态演化。浅层估计会过早丢弃对深层推理至关重要的 token，导致关键视觉信息丢失。

Method: 提出 Trend-aware Pruning 框架，将剪枝从局部快照决策提升为时序轨迹建模问题。通过捕捉注意力流动的动量（momentum of attention flow），实现动态纠正机制，选择性重新激活那些初始被低估但语义重要性上升的"晚开花"token，从而防止关键视觉线索丢失。

Result: 在多种多模态任务上取得更优的效率-性能权衡。视觉 token 减少超过 77.8%，最终层仅保留约 23 个 token，同时保持竞争性能。

Conclusion: 通过将 token 剪枝建模为时序轨迹问题并引入动态纠正机制，实现了鲁棒且可逆的高效多模态推理方案，验证了捕捉 token 重要性动态演化对高效 MLLM 推理的价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 中的视觉 token 剪枝以提升推理效率，属于多模态高效化与轻量化方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] efficient_multimodal 子主题。

Importance reason: Training-free token pruning with dynamic trajectory modeling directly addresses efficient MLLM inference, a high-priority subtopic, though it is not a core continuous-model or generation method. Signals: efficient inference, token pruning, multimodal large language model, training-free, attention flow

Abstract: While visual token pruning is essential for efficient Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), existing training-free methods suffer from a critical limitation: they rely on static, instantaneous heuristics to perform irreversible filtering. This approach ignores the hierarchical nature of MLLMs, where token importance often evolves dynamically rather than remaining fixed across layers. Consequently, tokens essential for deep-layer reasoning are often prematurely discarded by shallow-layer estimates. To address this, we propose Trend-aware Pruning, a novel framework that elevates pruning from a local snapshot decision to a temporal trajectory modeling problem. Instead of relying on isolated scores, our method captures the momentum of attention flow. This enables a dynamic rectification mechanism that selectively reactivates "late-blooming" tokens, those initially undervalued but exhibiting rising semantic importance, thereby preventing the loss of critical visual cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves a superior efficiency-performance trade-off across diverse multimodal tasks. Notably, it reduces visual tokens by over 77.8%, retaining only approximately 23 tokens in the final layer while maintaining competitive performance, offering a robust and reversible solution for high-efficiency multimodal inference.

</details>

## 空间与 3D 多模态推理 [[Back]](#toc)

空间推理、3D 视觉定位、具身推理、几何推理。

### [96] [MonoVoc: Decoupling Geometry and Semantics for Lightweight Monocular Open-Vocabulary 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28300)
*Pouya Ardekhani, Zahra Dehghanian, Morteza Abolghasemi, Hamid R. Rabiee*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MonoVoc 提出一种免训练、轻量级的单目开放词汇 3D 高斯场景理解方法，将 3D 几何重建与语义集成显式解耦，通过模块化后处理在物体级语义高斯地图上实现语义接地，相比 SOTA 基线在内存占用上降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 高斯开放词汇场景理解框架受限于多视角采集要求、昂贵的场景级优化以及存储稠密语言特征带来的巨大内存开销，难以从日常单目视频中高效实现开放词汇 3D 场景理解。

Method: 提出免训练流水线，将 3D 几何重建与语义集成显式解耦：从单目视频序列独立提取几何，通过轻量级模块化后处理框架接地语义，用物体级语义嵌入替代稠密的逐高斯语言特征存储，输出紧凑、可解释、可检索的物体级语义高斯地图。

Result: 在 Replica 数据集上的广泛评估表明，该解耦架构保持了较强的渲染保真度和有竞争力的分割精度，同时相比 SOTA 基线在内存占用上实现一个数量级的降低。

Conclusion: 该工作为直接从日常单目视频进行开放词汇 3D 检索与问答提供了高效、可扩展且实用的解决方案，验证了解耦几何与语义这一范式的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出基于单目视频的开放词汇 3D 场景理解方法，将几何重建与语义解耦，构建可检索的物体级语义高斯地图，属于空间与 3D 多模态推理方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] spatial_3d_reasoning。

Importance reason: Focuses on monocular 3D open-vocabulary scene understanding, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: 3D Gaussian, open-vocabulary, monocular video, memory-efficient, semantic grounding

Abstract: Open vocabulary 3D scene understanding is essential for next-generation interactive systems, empowering users to intuitively query and navigate reconstructed environments using natural language. However, current 3D Gaussian frameworks are often bottlenecked by restrictive multiview capture requirements, costly scene-specific optimization, and the massive memory overhead of storing dense language features. We present a novel, training-free pipeline that fundamentally reimagines this paradigm by explicitly decoupling 3D geometric reconstruction from semantic integration. Given a standard monocular video sequence as input, our method efficiently outputs a compact, highly interpretable, and fully searchable object-level semantic Gaussian map. Rather than entangling heavy language embeddings within the mapping loop, we extract geometry independently and ground semantics through a lightweight, modular post-processing framework. Extensive evaluations on the Replica dataset demonstrate that this decoupled architecture preserves strong rendering fidelity and competitive segmentation accuracy. Crucially, by replacing dense per-Gaussian storage with modular, object-level semantic embeddings, our approach delivers an order-of-magnitude reduction in memory usage compared to SOTA baselines. This provides a highly efficient, scalable, and practical solution for open-vocabulary 3D retrieval and question answering directly from everyday monocular video.

</details>


### [97] [ViewMind3D: Modular View-Aware Inference for Training-Free 3D-QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28442)
*Ping-Kun Chiang, Kun-Ru Wu, Po-han Li, Sandeep Chinchali, Ufuk Topcu, Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ViewMind3D 是一个完全无需训练的模块化框架，通过将 3D-QA 任务分解为多视角选择、视觉定位、BEV 空间编码和角色推理四个可解释组件，利用通用 LLM/VLM 实现 3D 空间推理，在 ScanQA 和 SQA3D 上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D-QA 方法大多依赖 3D 特定训练或微调，需要昂贵的标注，限制了可扩展性和实际应用。作者希望利用通用 LLM/VLM 的模块化编排实现无需训练的 3D 空间推理，无需完整 3D 重建。

Method: 将 3D-QA 分解为四个可解释组件：(1) 问题驱动的多视角选择；(2) 基于语言条件物体线索的引导式视觉定位；(3) 通过鸟瞰图（BEV）视角指示符进行空间上下文编码；(4) 基于角色推理的结构化答案生成。整个流程无需模型微调。

Result: 在 ScanQA 和 SQA3D 上取得与先前无需训练和微调的 3D-LLM 相当的竞争性能。在 SQA3D 的空间接地问题类型（如"What"问题）上表现提升，整体准确率 50.8%，ScanQA 上 CIDEr 达 73.4。

Conclusion: 通过通用 LLM 和 VLM 的模块化编排即可实现有效的 3D 推理，无需 3D 特定训练，为真实世界环境中的机器人感知提供了可扩展、可解释的 3D-QA 方案。

Classification reason: 论文提出 ViewMind3D，一种无需训练的模块化 3D 空间推理框架，用于 3D 问答（3D-QA），属于空间与 3D 多模态推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] spatial_3d_reasoning。同时涉及具身智能与机器人感知，与 world_model 方向的 embodied_world_model 相关。

Importance reason: Training-free 3D-QA via modular VLM orchestration is useful but tangential to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive/diffusion video generation. Signals: training-free 3D-QA, modular VLM reasoning, BEV spatial encoding, no 3D reconstruction, ScanQA/SQA3D benchmarks

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) have enabled new possibilities for 3D question answering (3D-QA), a key capability for embodied AI and robotic perception. However, most existing methods rely on 3D-specific training or fine-tuning with costly annotations, limiting their scalability and real-world applicability. We present \textbf{ViewMind3D}, a fully training-free and modular framework for 3D spatial reasoning over multi-view observations of a scene without requiring complete 3D reconstruction. The framework decomposes the 3D-QA task into four interpretable components: (1) question-driven multi-view selection, (2) guided visual grounding with language-conditioned object cues, (3) spatial context encoding via a bird's-eye-view (BEV) viewpoint indicator, and (4) structured answer generation through role-based reasoning. This design enables structured, robust, and interpretable reasoning without requiring model tuning. Experimental results on ScanQA and SQA3D show that ViewMind3D achieves competitive performance compared to prior training-free and fine-tuned 3D-LLMs. In particular, our method improves performance on spatially grounded question types, such as ``What'' questions in SQA3D, while maintaining strong overall accuracy (50.8\%) and achieving 73.4 CIDEr on ScanQA. These results demonstrate that effective 3D reasoning can be achieved through modular orchestration of general-purpose LLMs and VLMs for robotic perception in real-world environments.

</details>

### [98] [One Patch Is Enough: Reinforcement-Optimized Visual Token Grounding for MLLM-Based Scene Text Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27902)
*Rui Tang, Wentao Yang, Peirong Zhang, Yongxin Shi, Shun Zhang, Huiguo He, Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (76.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 SPaTS，一种基于 MLLM 的视觉中心场景文本识别框架，通过单一锚点视觉 token 路由每个文本实例，并结合强化学习优化 token 选择、方向嵌入对齐与补丁增强解码，实现高精度文本识别与空间定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景文本识别需要文本识别与空间定位的高精度对齐。现有基于 MLLM 的视觉 token 接地采用多补丁范式，在密集或小文本实例上会引入冗余噪声和定位歧义。

Method: 提出 SPaTS 框架：(1) 通过单一锚点视觉 token 路由每个文本实例，再用全图精化恢复几何；(2) SPaSO 强化学习框架，用补丁级奖励优化离散视觉 token 选择；(3) DEA 方向嵌入对齐，解耦特征幅度与方向以抑制范数偏差；(4) PED 补丁增强解码，融合锚点与语言语义并对全图特征图交叉注意力进行几何感知边界回归。

Result: 大量实验表明 SPaTS 在场景文本识别上持续且显著优于前沿闭源 MLLM 和 OCR MLLM。

Conclusion: 单一锚点视觉 token 范式配合强化学习优化与几何感知解码，能有效提升 MLLM 场景文本识别的精度与鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 论文提出基于 MLLM 的场景文本识别框架 SPaTS，通过视觉 token 接地实现文本识别与空间定位，属于通用视觉语言模型/MLLM 架构与指令微调范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Solid MLLM-based OCR refinement work, but it is a niche application with limited direct impact on the user's core continuous-model, world-model, or generation priorities. Signals: MLLM, scene text spotting, reinforcement learning, visual token grounding, OCR

Abstract: Scene text spotting requires high-precision alignment between textual recognition and spatial localization. While visual-token grounding has emerged as a promising formulation for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), the previous multi-patch paradigm often introduces redundant noise and localization ambiguity, particularly for dense or small text instances. To address this, we propose Single-Patch Text Spotting (SPaTS), a vision-centric framework that routes each text instance through a single anchor visual token and then recovers geometry via full-image refinement. To accurately identify this anchor without oracle labels, we introduce Single-Patch Selective Optimization (SPaSO), a reinforcement learning framework that optimizes discrete visual-token selection using patch-level rewards. To further improve representation robustness and localization precision, we introduce Directional Embedding Alignment (DEA) to suppress unstable norm bias by decoupling feature magnitude and direction, and Patch-Enhanced Decoding (PED) to fuse the routed anchor with language semantics and cross-attend over the full-image feature map for geometry-aware boundary regression beyond coordinate-space surrogates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPaTS consistently and significantly outperforms both frontier closed-source MLLMs and OCR MLLMs. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [99] [IndustryForge-27B: A Domain-Enhanced Multimodal Foundation Model for Industrial CAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28050)
*Nianchen Deng, Jiaxin Ai, Tao Hu, Shu Zou, Yurui Dong, Siqi Li, Xinyu Cai, Xuemeng Yang, Licheng Wen, Hongbin Zhou, Hairong Zhang, Pinlong Cai, Botian Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: IndustryForge-27B 是基于 Qwen3.5-VL-27B 构建的工业 CAD 领域增强多模态基础模型，通过整合约 5.2 万条多模态样本（CAD 视觉问答、参数化 CAD 代码、装配级 CAD 代码及 Inventor/SolidWorks COM 子语料）进行统一多任务 SFT 训练，在四个 CAD 领域基准上平均提升 33.65pp 并超越 GPT-5.4，同时在十一个通用能力基准上保持并小幅提升基础模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化工业 CAD 设计与制造对多模态基础模型提出了独特要求：模型需要理解工程图纸和 3D 几何截图、编写正确的参数化建模脚本和 Windows COM API 代码，并覆盖从单个零件到装配体的完整范围。通用多模态模型在这些任务上表现不足，而单任务微调又过于狭窄，无法支撑上层智能体发出的多样化调用需求。

Method: 在 Qwen3.5-VL-27B 基础上，整合六个工业 CAD 子语料库（约 5.2 万条多模态样本）：CAD 视觉问答（CAD-VQA）、参数化 CAD 代码（text2cadquery）、装配级 CAD 代码（text2cadquery-assembly），以及面向 Inventor/SolidWorks 的三个 COM 子语料（com_2d / com_3d / com_assembly），采用统一的多任务 SFT 训练配方进行领域增强。

Result: 在四个 CAD 领域基准上，IndustryForge-27B 相比基础模型平均提升 33.65pp，并在全部四个基准上超越强闭源模型 GPT-5.4；在十一个通用能力基准上保持并小幅提升基础模型性能（平均 +1.56pp），未出现灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: IndustryForge-27B 可作为下游工业智能体项目的通用基础底座，为覆盖从 CAD 设计到工业软件操作、从零件到装配体、从单次生成到闭环自改进的全栈工业智能体提供统一起点。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个面向工业 CAD 领域的多模态基础模型（基于 Qwen3.5-VL 微调），属于视觉语言模型/MLLM 的领域增强与多任务指令微调工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general 子主题。

Importance reason: Domain-specific CAD fine-tuning with limited novelty for the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and video/world-model generation. Signals: industrial CAD domain, multimodal SFT, Qwen3.5-VL-27B, CAD VQA and code generation, benchmark gains

Abstract: Automating industrial CAD design and manufacturing places distinctive demands on multimodal foundation models: the model must see engineering drawings and 3D geometry screenshots, write correct parametric-modelling scripts and Windows COM API code, and cover the full range from single parts to assemblies. General-purpose multimodal models fall short on these tasks, while single-task fine-tuning is too narrow to support the diverse calls that upper-layer agents issue. We build IndustryForge-27B on top of Qwen3.5-VL-27B by curating and integrating six industrial-CAD sub-corpora totalling $\sim$52k multimodal samples---CAD Visual QA (CAD-VQA), parametric CAD code (text2cadquery), assembly-level CAD code (text2cadquery-assembly), and three COM sub-corpora for Inventor / SolidWorks (com_2d / com_3d / com_assembly)---and training with a unified multi-task SFT recipe. Across four CAD-domain benchmarks IndustryForge-27B lifts the base model by $+33.65$~pp on average and outperforms the strong closed-source model GPT-5.4 on all four; across eleven general-capability benchmarks it retains, and slightly improves upon, the base model ($+1.56$~pp mean, no catastrophic forgetting). IndustryForge-27B will serve as the common substrate for downstream industrial-agent projects, providing a unified starting point for a full-stack industrial agent that spans from CAD design to industrial-software operation, from parts to assemblies, and from single-shot generation to closed-loop self-improvement.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Visual Ambiguity: Guiding Robust Monocular Depth Estimation in Challenging Scenarios via Detailed Long Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28285)
*Junrui Zhang, Jiaqi Li, Yiran Wang, Liao Shen, Zhiguo Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CapDepth 是一个利用详细长文本描述引导单目深度估计的框架，通过长描述输入模板、动态文本编码器和文本自适应解码器，缓解非朗伯表面和恶劣天气下的视觉歧义，深度误差分别降低 25.0% 和 22.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计在非朗伯表面和恶劣天气等挑战场景下因单图信息有限而存在视觉歧义。现有方法通过图像修复或增强孤立处理这些问题，鲁棒性提升有限。虽然语言作为视觉的互补模态已被证明能增强 VLM 的感知能力，但先前的语言集成 MDE 方法因短文本信息有限、粗粒度全局文本特征学习以及深度解码阶段语言引导不足而未能充分利用这一潜力。

Method: CapDepth 包含三个核心组件：1) 详细长描述输入模板，用多个原子句显式传达丰富的空间关系；2) 动态文本编码器，通过渐进式掩码注意力提取细粒度深度相关文本特征；3) 文本自适应解码器，通过稳定的自适应层归一化用文本特征引导增强的深度解码。

Result: 大量实验验证了 CapDepth 的有效性，在非朗伯表面上深度误差降低 25.0%，在恶劣天气条件下降低 22.0%，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: CapDepth 证明详细长文本描述能够有效缓解单目深度估计中的视觉歧义，通过细粒度文本特征提取和文本引导解码，显著提升了挑战场景下的深度估计鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CapDepth，利用详细长文本描述（语言模态）引导单目深度估计，属于视觉语言模型在视觉感知任务中的应用，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）子主题。

Importance reason: Solid but niche MDE robustness work; only tangential to your core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model priorities. Signals: monocular depth estimation, long captions, vision-language guidance, non-Lambertian surfaces, adverse weather

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation (MDE) faces challenges with non-Lambertian surfaces and adverse weather conditions due to the visual ambiguities inherent in single-image limited information. Existing works address them in isolation via image inpainting or augmentation, yielding limited robustness gains. Language, as a powerful complementary modality to vision, is demonstrated to enhance the visual perception capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) via detailed long captions. However, prior language-integrated MDE methods fail to fully harness this potential due to short text input with limited information, coarse global text feature learning, and limited language guidance during depth decoding. To address these limitations, we propose CapDepth, a novel framework for robust MDE that leverages guidance from detailed long captions to alleviate visual ambiguities in both challenging scenarios. First, we design a detailed long caption input template that explicitly conveys rich spatial relationships among multiple atom sentences. Second, a dynamic caption encoder is introduced to extract fine-grained depth-relevant text features via progressive masked attention. Finally, we propose a text-adaptive decoder that guides enhanced depth decoding with text features via stable adaptive layer normalization. Extensive experiments validate the efficacy of CapDepth, which outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving depth error reductions of 25.0% on non-Lambertian surfaces and 22.0% under adverse weather conditions.

</details>


### [101] [Hand-Object Interaction in the Age of Large Foundation Models:Reconstruction, Generation, and Embodied Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28394)
*Weiquan Lin, Yu Deng, Shiyang Liu, Luping Xiao, Xu Tang, Junzhi Yu, Jiaolong Yang, Lei Zhang, Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文是首篇系统综述基础模型先验在手-物交互（HOI）建模中的应用，将文献组织为六个 HOI 任务（重建与生成），并提出八类基础模型子先验的分类体系（几何、语义、视觉三大族），同时探讨 HOI 知识在机器人学习中的迁移，并汇总数据集与评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HOI 建模需要联合推理手部姿态、物体几何、接触、语义与动力学，在严重视觉不确定性下极具挑战。基础模型引入跨域大规模先验知识，但相关文献碎片化，现有研究仅笼统描述为"使用大模型"，缺乏对引入何种知识、注入到 HOI 流程何处、减少何种不确定性的系统刻画。

Method: 作者提出首个基础模型先验用于 HOI 的系统综述：将文献组织为六个 HOI 任务（覆盖重建与生成），建立八类基础模型子先验的分类体系，分为几何族（形状检索、形状重建、空间重建）、语义族（语义定位、语言推理）和视觉族（视觉表征、图像生成、视频生成）。基于该分类体系，系统分析不同先验在 HOI 流程中的表示、注入与适配方式，并考察 HOI 知识在机器人学习中的迁移（人类数据预训练、人-机器人技能迁移、HOI 到机器人数据生成）。

Result: 论文提供了 HOI 基础模型先验的系统分类框架，梳理了各先验在重建与生成任务中的注入方式，总结了 HOI 到机器人学习的迁移路径，并汇总了数据集与评测协议，同时维护一个持续更新的在线仓库。

Conclusion: 基础模型先验为 HOI 建模提供了超越任务特定数据与模型的迁移知识，但当前研究仍碎片化。作者呼吁建立更系统的先验分类与注入框架，并指出向更通用、可泛化的 HOI 系统发展的未来方向。

Classification reason: 该论文是一篇关于手-物交互（HOI）建模的综述，系统梳理了基础模型（包括视觉语言模型、生成模型等）如何为 HOI 重建与生成提供先验知识。论文核心关注基础模型先验在 HOI 任务中的应用，属于视觉语言模型与多模态理解/生成范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general 子主题。

Importance reason: A solid survey on hand-object interaction, but it is tangential to your core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: survey, hand-object interaction, foundation model priors, reconstruction and generation, robot learning

Abstract: Hand-object interaction (HOI) modeling remains challenging because it requires joint reasoning about hand articulation, object geometry, contact, semantics, and dynamics under severe visual uncertainty. Foundation models introduce transferable prior knowledge learned from large-scale cross-domain data, offering new ways to address these challenges beyond task-specific data and models. However, the rapidly growing literature remains fragmented, and existing studies typically describe these methods simply as ``using large models'' without systematically characterizing what knowledge is introduced, where it enters the HOI pipeline, or which HOI uncertainty it helps reduce. This survey presents the first systematic review of foundation-model priors for HOI. We organize the literature into six HOI tasks spanning reconstruction and generation. More importantly, we establish a taxonomy of eight foundation-model sub-priors grouped into geometric, semantic, and visual families. Geometric priors encompass shape retrieval, shape reconstruction, and spatial reconstruction; semantic priors include semantic grounding and language reasoning; and visual priors cover visual representation, image generation, and video generation. Based on this taxonomy, we systematically analyze how different priors are represented, injected, and adapted across HOI pipelines and tasks. Beyond how foundation models empower HOI, we further examine how HOI-derived knowledge is used in robot learning, including human-data pretraining, human-to-robot skill transfer, and HOI-to-robot data generation. Finally, we summarize datasets and evaluation protocols, and discuss limitations and future directions toward more generalizable HOI systems. To support long-term progress, we curate a live repository that continuously aggregates emerging methods and benchmarks.

</details>


### [102] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu, Yan Zhu, Yili He, Zilong Wang, Xinyang Jiang, Peiyao Fu, Ruijie Yang, Tianyi Chen, Siyuan Li, Zhihua Wang, Fei Wu, Quanlin Li, Xian Yang, Pinghong Zhou, Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (60.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EndoCLIP 是一个基于 28 万份常规结肠镜报告训练的视觉语言基础模型，通过从整份报告中逐步恢复病灶级图像-文本对（12.5 万对），在病灶级图文检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务上超越通用与生物医学视觉语言编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜领域视觉语言模型应用不足，尽管常规报告中包含丰富的专家描述（病灶外观、大小、位置），但这些报告总结的是整个检查过程而非逐帧标注，导致临床发现与对应图像之间仅存在弱关联，难以作为监督信号。

Method: 从 280,476 份常规结肠镜记录中逐步恢复出 125,756 个病灶级图像-文本对，训练 EndoCLIP 视觉语言基础模型，实现病灶级图文检索、结构化报告生成与多中心临床分类。

Result: 在病灶级图文检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务上，EndoCLIP 在零样本和线性探测设置下均优于通用与生物医学视觉语言编码器；在良恶性分类上，其线性探测在包含 12 名内镜医师的盲测中接近专家水平。

Conclusion: 恢复发现-帧对应关系可将常规文档转化为可扩展的监督信号，使临床目标能够以语言形式指定，而无需为每个任务单独标注，展示了将常规临床记录转化为视觉语言模型训练数据的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EndoCLIP，一个面向结肠镜的视觉语言基础模型，属于通用视觉语言模型（VLM）的构建与应用，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general 子主题。

Importance reason: Specialized medical vision-language model with limited relevance to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: medical domain, colonoscopy VLM, report-to-frame recovery, zero-shot classification, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>
