### [103] [Drawing-Recode: Annotation Grounding for Parametric CAD Code Generation from Raster 2D CAD Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27558)
*Mingi Kim, Yongjun Kim, Hyungki Kim*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Drawing-Recode 框架，从光栅 2D CAD 图纸中提取几何特征并识别尺寸标注，通过交叉注意力与标注接地损失（AGL）将标注显式接地到几何信息，再由 LLM 生成结构化参数化 CAD 代码（SPCC），用于工业图纸数字化与零件复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化转型前积累的大量光栅格式 2D CAD 图纸难以直接用于零件复现和制造自动化。现有研究要么只处理矢量图纸，要么局限于特定领域，且未能将尺寸标注与几何信息显式关联，限制了尺寸信息在 3D 参数化 CAD 序列恢复中的利用。

Method: Drawing-Recode 采用图像编码器提取几何特征，通过独立的文本识别模块识别尺寸标注，利用交叉注意力机制和提出的 Annotation Grounding Loss（AGL）将标注显式接地到几何信息，最终将融合特征输入 LLM 生成 SPCC 格式的 CAD 代码。

Result: 实验表明 Drawing-Recode 优于现有基线方法，并且在模拟工业条件的扫描图纸上保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Drawing-Recode 有效实现了光栅 2D CAD 图纸的数字化，通过标注-几何接地提升了参数化 CAD 序列恢复的准确性，有望推动工业场景下的零件复现与制造自动化。

Classification reason: 该论文从光栅 2D CAD 图纸中提取几何与标注信息并生成参数化 CAD 代码，属于文档/图纸理解与结构化信息提取任务，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的"视觉问答与文档理解"子主题。

Importance reason: Domain-specific CAD digitization with limited overlap with the user's continuous multimodal and video/world-model priorities. Signals: CAD code generation, annotation grounding, raster-to-parametric, cross-attention, LLM-based generation

Abstract: Recovering Parametric CAD sequences from raster-format 2D Computer-Aided Design (CAD) drawings accumulated prior to digital transformation is important for part reproduction and manufacturing process automation. However, existing studies either process only vector drawings or are limited to specific domains, and fail to explicitly connect dimensional annotations to geometric information, limiting their use of dimensional information for 3D Parametric CAD sequences recovery. We propose Drawing-Recode, a framework that generates Parametric CAD sequences as CAD code from raster 2D CAD drawings. Drawing-Recode extracts geometric features via an image encoder and recognizes annotations through a separate text recognition module, then explicitly grounds annotations to geometric information using cross-attention and our proposed Annotation Grounding Loss (AGL). The resulting features are fed into a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate CAD code in the Structured Parametric CAD Code (SPCC) format. Experiments show that Drawing-Recode outperforms existing baselines and remains robust on scanned drawings resembling industrial conditions. We expect Drawing-Recode contributes to digitizing raster 2D CAD drawings in industrial settings and to part reproduction and manufacturing automation.

</details>


### [104] [Objective-Aligned Direct Answer SFT for Robust Multi-Frame Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27566)
*Site Li, Jianyi Hao, Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (49.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文在 MedFrameQA 基准上系统比较了多种多帧医学 VQA 适应方法，发现与基准最终答案目标紧密对齐的直接 decoder-only 答案 SFT（基于 MedGemma-1.5-4B）是最稳健的适应家族，在固定划分、匹配预算、重复种子和校准控制下显著提升报告准确率且跨种子稳定，并可通过事后校准修复置信度估计，且能迁移到 Qwen2.5-VL-3B 等骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧医学 VQA 领域存在多种看似合理的复杂适应机制（控制器式推理、定位感知重排、静态难负样本混合、分阶段续训等），但缺乏在严格受控条件下对这些方法进行公平比较的稳健性研究。作者希望验证一个更简单的假设：与基准最终答案目标紧密对齐的方法才是最强健的适应家族。

Method: 在 MedFrameQA 上对控制器方法、脚手架演化、静态混合监督、续训变体以及直接答案 SFT 等家族进行受控比较，控制固定划分、匹配预算、重复种子与校准。核心方法是基于 MedGemma-1.5-4B 的 decoder-only 直接答案 SFT，并辅以事后校准修复置信度估计。

Result: 直接 decoder-only 答案 SFT 在 held-out 报告准确率上较冻结基线有显著提升，且在重复种子与匹配控制下保持稳定；事后校准在不损失准确率的前提下修复了置信度估计；该方法可一致迁移到 Qwen2.5-VL-3B 等次要骨干。

Conclusion: 主要结论不是复杂辅助机制获胜，而是目标对齐的直接答案 SFT 是 MedFrameQA 上最强健的适应家族。作者希望借此建立强健的极简基线，引导社区关注根本性的稳健优化而非架构复杂性。

Classification reason: 论文研究多帧医学视觉问答（Medical VQA），属于视觉问答范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_vqa_doc（视觉问答与文档理解）。

Importance reason: Medical VQA is outside your configured priority directions and is explicitly penalized by negative keywords. Signals: medical VQA, negative keyword match, no continuous/multimodal generation focus, benchmark robustness study, low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Multi-frame medical VQA appears to reward increasingly complex adaptation: controller-style inference, localization-aware reranking, static hard-negative mixing, and staged continuation all appear plausible from first principles. We test a simpler competing hypothesis on MedFrameQA: methods that remain tightly aligned with the benchmark's final answer objective should be the strongest \emph{robust} adaptation family once evaluation is controlled across fixed splits, matched budgets, repeated seeds, and calibration. We compare controller-based methods, scaffold evolution, static mixed supervision, continuation-heavy variants, and direct answer-only supervised fine-tuning (SFT). The strongest robust family is direct decoder-only answer SFT on MedGemma-1.5-4B. Empirically, this family yields substantial improvements in held-out report accuracy over frozen baselines while remaining remarkably stable across repeated seeds and matched controls, ensuring our claims reflect true family-level robustness rather than an isolated hyperparameter peak. Furthermore, post-hoc calibration effectively repairs confidence estimation without compromising accuracy, and the core approach transfers consistently to secondary backbones like Qwen2.5-VL-3B. The main result is therefore not that a complex auxiliary mechanism wins, but that objective-aligned direct answer SFT is the strongest robust adaptation family we found for MedFrameQA. By establishing this strong, minimalist baseline, we hope to redirect community focus toward fundamentally robust optimization rather than architectural complexity.

</details>


### [105] [Sign Language Question Answering: A New Task, Benchmark, and Baseline for Sign Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27826)
*Shiwei Gan, Lichen Wang, Xiao Liu, Yafeng Yin, Kuizhuang Liu, Sanglu Lu, Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文首次提出手语问答（SLQA）任务，构建基于 PHOENIX14T 和 CSL-Daily 的 SignQA 基准，并提出带问题条件调制时序下采样模块与领域内知识迁移策略的基线模型，在各类问题类别上优于代表性视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语理解（SLU）任务如连续手语识别和手语翻译都基于预定义目标，仅学习从手语视频到 gloss 或句子的固定映射，无法充分评估模型是否真正理解手语视频的语义内容。作者提出 SLQA 任务，通过要求模型回答任意自然语言问题来更灵活全面地评估手语理解能力。

Method: 作者提出 SLQA 任务并构建 SignQA 基准，利用模板从现有 gloss 和句子标注自动生成问答对，覆盖位置推理、结构推理、视觉搜索、gloss 识别和翻译理解五类问题。同时提出基线模型，包含问题条件调制时序下采样模块（Question-Conditioned Modulated Temporal Downsampling）和领域内知识迁移策略，实现从现有 SLU 任务的知识迁移并增强问题感知的时序特征建模。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的基线模型在所有问题类别上一致优于代表性视觉语言模型，为 SLQA 未来研究建立了强基准。

Conclusion: SLQA 为手语理解提供了更灵活全面的评估框架，所构建的 SignQA 基准和基线模型为后续研究奠定了坚实基础，数据集已公开。

Classification reason: 论文提出手语问答（SLQA）新任务，要求模型回答关于手语视频的自然语言问题，属于视觉问答范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题。

Importance reason: Novel task and benchmark for sign language QA, but outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative video/image modeling. Signals: new task, benchmark, sign language, video QA, temporal downsampling

Abstract: Recent advances in sign language (SL) understanding (SLU) have led to remarkable progress in tasks such as continuous SL recognition and SL translation. However, these tasks are designed with predefined objectives, requiring models to learn a fixed mapping from sign videos to glosses or spoken-language sentences. As a result, they provide only a limited assessment of whether a model truly understands the semantic content of SL videos. To address this limitation, \textbf{we first propose a new task, Sign Language Question Answering (SLQA)}, which evaluates SL understanding by requiring models to answer arbitrary natural language questions about SL videos. Unlike previous SLU tasks, SLQA provides a more flexible and comprehensive evaluation framework that assesses multiple reasoning capabilities beyond recognition and translation. To facilitate this task, \textbf{we further construct two SignQA benchmarks} based on PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily by automatically generating question-answer pairs from existing gloss and sentence annotations using carefully designed templates. The resulting datasets cover five complementary question categories, including position reasoning, structural reasoning, visual search, gloss recognition, and translation understanding. \textbf{Finally, we propose a simple yet effective baseline model} equipped with a Question-Conditioned Modulated Temporal Downsampling module and an in-domain knowledge transfer strategy, enabling effective knowledge transfer from existing SLU tasks while enhancing question-aware temporal feature modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our baseline consistently outperforms representative vision-language models across all question categories, establishing a strong benchmark for future research on SLQA. Datasets are available at:{ }.

</details>


### [106] [MMLDSum-LLM: Multimodal Long-Document Summarization with Visual-Alignment and Keyword-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28006)
*Xianpeng Zhang, Jiahua Yang, Dongyu Chen, Lei zhang, Jian Ma, Xu guohuan, Haonan Lu, Tianhuang Su, Chuangchuang Wang, Kai Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 MMLDSum-Bench 多模态长文档摘要基准与 MMLDSum-LLM 两阶段训练框架，通过视觉对齐加权损失、关键词加权损失与 GRPO 多目标奖励，提升多模态长文档摘要的关键信息覆盖与跨模态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态长文档中关键证据稀疏分布于段落与模态之间，多模态 LLM 在摘要时容易出现关键信息遗漏与跨模态幻觉，根源在于长程依赖建模中的注意力漂移与模态间对齐的鸿沟。

Method: 提出可复现的两阶段训练框架 MMLDSum-LLM：第一阶段结合视觉对齐加权损失与关键词加权损失的监督微调；第二阶段采用 GRPO 强化学习，使用多目标奖励（关键词覆盖、图文对齐、ROUGE 与长度控制）。同时构建覆盖多领域、多上下文长度与图文模态分布的高质量基准 MMLDSum-Bench。

Result: 在 MMLDSum-Bench 上与领先闭源与开源多模态模型对比，采用统一评测协议（LLM-as-a-judge、原子声明精确率/召回率、图文对齐 ITA、ROUGE），显著提升关键信息覆盖与跨模态一致性。

Conclusion: MMLDSum-LLM 通过视觉对齐与关键词感知的加权训练及多目标 GRPO 优化，有效缓解多模态长文档摘要中的信息遗漏与跨模态幻觉，验证了该框架在关键信息覆盖与跨模态一致性上的优势。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦多模态长文档摘要，属于文档理解与摘要任务，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc），该子主题涵盖文档/图表理解与文档智能。

Importance reason: Focuses on long-document summarization with visual alignment, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: multimodal long-document summarization, visual-alignment loss, keyword-aware loss, GRPO, benchmark

Abstract: Multimodal long documents are core carriers of professional knowledge, where critical evidence is sparsely distributed across paragraphs and modalities. This easily causes key information omission and cross-modal hallucinations in summarization by multimodal LLMs. These issues stem from attention drift in long-range dependency modeling and gaps in inter-modal alignment. To address this, we introduce MMLDSum-Bench, a high-quality benchmark for multimodal long-document summarization, covering multiple domains, context-length scales, and visual-textual modality distributions. We further propose MMLDSum-LLM, a reproducible two-stage training framework that combines supervised fine-tuning with visual-alignment weighted loss and keyword-aware weighted loss, followed by GRPO with a multi-objective reward (keyword coverage, image-text alignment, ROUGE, and length control). Extensive experiments on MMLDSum-Bench, comparing against leading closed-source and open-source multimodal models under a unified evaluation protocol - including LLM-as-a-judge scoring, atomic-claim precision/recall, image-text alignment (ITA), and ROUGE - demonstrate that our approach significantly improves key-information coverage and cross-modal consistency.

</details>


### [107] [RefCaptioner: Multi-Reference Image-Grounded Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28509)
*Tengfei Liu, Yang Shi, Yuran Wang, Xiaohan Zhang, Yuqing Wen, Yuqi Tang, Qixun Wang, Zhuoran Zhang, Xuanyu Zhu, Weihong Lin, Xinlei Yu, Yujie Wei, Xinwei Long, Fengxiang Wang, Xinlong Chen, Yue Ding, Jialu Chen, Haotian Wang, Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RefCaptioner 提出多参考图像接地的视频描述生成新任务，通过两阶段后训练框架（混合数据 SFT + 分层覆盖折扣 GRPO）联合优化参考选择、短语级绑定、干扰拒绝与跨参考一致性，并构建 2 万视频语料与 MRVBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频描述生成模型能生成自然语言描述，但无法将局部视觉元素显式接地到多张参考图像，导致描述缺乏事实性与可追溯性。作者提出多参考图像接地视频描述生成任务，要求生成带短语级参考接地的忠实视频描述。

Method: 提出 RefCaptioner 两阶段后训练框架：第一阶段使用混合数据 SFT 训练基础能力；第二阶段采用分层覆盖折扣 GRPO（Hierarchical Coverage-Discounted GRPO）联合优化参考选择、短语级绑定、干扰拒绝与跨参考一致性，同时保持通用视频描述能力。构建含 20,000 视频和 171,354 参考图像的训练语料，并提出 MRVBench 评测基准。

Result: RefCaptioner 在开源模型中取得最佳综合性能，在标准视频描述基准上保持竞争力。人工评测显示其描述更受标注者偏好，并能用开源与专有视频生成器实现更忠实于源信息的视频重建。

Conclusion: 多参考图像接地视频描述生成是可行且有价值的新任务，RefCaptioner 通过两阶段后训练有效提升了描述的事实性与参考接地能力，为视频理解与生成的可追溯性提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 论文提出多参考图像接地视频描述生成任务，属于视频内容理解与描述生成，与视觉问答/视频理解类目相关。虽然更接近视频理解，但该任务本质是视频描述生成（captioning），与多模态理解方向匹配。考虑到没有更贴切的 canonical 子主题，映射到多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答与文档理解（视频描述生成与 VQA 同属视觉内容理解与语言生成）。

Importance reason: Solid but niche video captioning work with grounding, lacking strong alignment to your continuous multimodal/world-model priorities. Signals: video captioning, multi-reference grounding, GRPO post-training, MRVBench benchmark, no continuous tokens

Abstract: Existing video captioning models generate natural descriptions of video content but cannot explicitly ground local visual elements to multiple reference images. We introduce multi-reference image-grounded video captioning, a new task requiring factual video descriptions with phrase-level reference grounding, and propose RefCaptioner, a two-stage post-training framework for this task. RefCaptioner combines mixed-data SFT with Hierarchical Coverage-Discounted GRPO to jointly improve reference selection, phrase-level binding, distractor rejection, and cross-reference consistency while preserving general video-captioning ability. To support training, we construct a corpus containing $20,000$ videos and 171,354 reference images. We further introduce MRVBench, a benchmark for evaluating caption factuality and multi-reference grounding on both real-world and AI-generated videos. Experiments show that RefCaptioner achieves the best overall performance among the open-source models while remaining competitive on standard video captioning benchmarks. Human evaluation further confirms that its captions are preferred by annotators and enable more source-faithful video reconstruction with both open-source and proprietary video generators.

</details>


### [108] [MarkushGlyph and OCSRGlyph: Improved Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28532)
*Alex Andonian, Samuel G Rodriques, Andrew D White, Siddharth M Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (41.58) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 OCSRGlyph 和 MarkushGlyph 两个模型，将化学结构识别（OCSR）和 Markush 结构解析统一视为图像到文本翻译任务，并引入新的 Markush 翻译准确率评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学结构在专利和科学文献中以图像形式存在，需要转换为行符号以便程序化使用。虽然单分子 OCSR 已较成熟，但 Markush 结构（表示一族分子）的解析仍是挑战，且现有评估指标存在失败模式。

Method: 将 OCSR 和 Markush 解析均建模为图像到文本翻译问题。OCSRGlyph 通过仔细考虑立体化学提升性能；MarkushGlyph 是一个视觉语言模型，将整个 Markush 结构作为图像读取，区别于以往多阶段分别处理视觉和文本输入的系统。同时提出新的 Markush 翻译准确率评估指标。

Result: OCSRGlyph 在 OCSR 任务上达到 SOTA，优于先前方法；MarkushGlyph 能端到端读取整个 Markush 结构图像；新指标能处理先前指标的失败模式。

Conclusion: 将化学结构识别统一为图像到文本翻译问题有效，视觉语言模型可直接端到端解析 Markush 结构，且需要更鲁棒的评估指标来衡量翻译准确性。

Classification reason: 该论文将化学结构图像（包括 Markush 结构）转化为文本行符号，属于文档/图表理解与图像到文本翻译任务，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的"视觉问答与文档理解"子主题。

Importance reason: This paper is a domain-specific OCR/VLM application for chemistry, far outside the user's configured focus on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: chemical structure recognition, image-to-text translation, vision-language model, domain-specific OCR, no overlap with priority subtopics

Abstract: Chemical structures appear in patents and the scientific literature as images. For programmatic usage, such as indexing in databases or constructing machine learning model training sets, they must be transformed into line notations. The two common forms of this task are translating an image of a single molecule (optical chemical structure recognition - OCSR) and translating a Markush structure that represents a family of molecules. While prior work in the former case is quite mature, Markush structure parsing remains a challenging task. In this work, we treat both tasks as an image-to-text translation problem. We then propose OCSRGlyph, a state-of-the-art OCSR model, improving performance over prior methods by carefully considering stereochemistry. For the Markush task, we introduce MarkushGlyph, a vision-language model that reads the entire Markush structure as an image. This contrasts with prior systems, which often use multiple stages to separately process visual and text input content. Finally, we introduce a new metric for determining the accuracy of Markush structure translations, handling failure modes present in prior metrics.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-video_understanding_temporal'></div>

### [109] [ObjectStream: Latent Objects as Memory Anchors for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28312)
*Mingkang Dong, Muxin Pu, Jie Li, Bohan Guo, Songruo Chen, Bin Ren, Xu Zheng, Chen Zhao, Tianwen Qian, Mohamed Elhoseiny, Yuqian Fu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (60.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ObjectStream 提出一种无需训练的框架，将潜在对象作为记忆锚点用于流式视频理解。它从冻结的 Video-LLM 表征中诱导出空间连贯的潜在对象，跨帧链接为持久锚点，并在有界记忆预算下维护其历史，无需外部检测器或分割模型。该方法在在线流式与离线长视频基准上均显著提升性能并大幅降低内存与首token延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需要在未来问题未知前持续保留有用的视觉证据。现有方法主要依据 token 重要性、时间冗余或片段级相关性来管理不断增长的视觉上下文，但很少围绕随时间持续演化的对象来组织证据，导致对象身份、交互与状态变化等关键信息难以被有效保留。

Method: ObjectStream 是一种无需训练的框架，将潜在对象作为记忆锚点。它从冻结的 Video-LLM 表征中直接诱导出空间连贯的潜在对象，跨帧链接这些对象形成持久锚点，并在有界记忆预算下维护其历史。框架保留三种互补证据：持久对象历史、瞬时对象变化和近期视觉上下文，使现有 Video-LLM 能在不改变底层模型的情况下推理对象身份、交互与状态变化。

Result: 在在线流式评测中，ObjectStream 使 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 在 OVO-Bench 实时视觉感知上提升 10.0 分，同时将峰值 GPU 内存和 TTFT 降低约 50%。在离线长视频基准上，它超越全 token 基线并丢弃 82.5% 的视觉 token。

Conclusion: 潜在对象作为紧凑流式视频记忆的组织原则是实用且有效的，能够在显著降低计算开销的同时提升流式与长视频理解性能。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦流式视频理解与长视频记忆管理，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] video_understanding_temporal。

Importance reason: Strong efficiency and memory-anchoring contribution for streaming video understanding, but only partially aligns with the user's priority on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: streaming video understanding, training-free latent object memory, Video-LLM, memory reduction, TTFT reduction

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to continuously retain useful visual evidence before future questions are known. Existing approaches primarily manage the growing visual context according to token importance, temporal redundancy, or segment-level relevance, but rarely organize evidence around objects that persist and evolve over time. Thus, in this paper, we introduce ObjectStream, a training-free framework that treats latent objects as memory anchors for streaming video understanding. ObjectStream induces spatially coherent latent objects directly from frozen Video-LLM representations, links them across frames into persistent anchors, and maintains their histories under a bounded memory budget, without requiring external object detectors or segmentation models. Built on these anchors, ObjectStream preserves three complementary forms of evidence: persistent object histories, transient object changes, and recent visual context. This design enables existing Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) to reason over object identities, interactions, and state changes while leaving the underlying model unchanged. Extensive experiments on online streaming and offline long-video benchmarks demonstrate both effectiveness and efficiency. In online streaming evaluation, ObjectStream improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 10.0 points on OVO-Bench Real-Time Visual Perception, while reducing peak GPU mem-ory and TTFT by approximately 50%. On offline long-video benchmarks, it surpasses the full-token baseline while discarding 82.5% of visual tokens. These results highlight latent objects as a practical and effective organizing principle for compact streaming video memory.

</details>


### [110] [VisualRouter: Query-Grounded Visual Sampling for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28463)
*Haiyue Zhang, Yi Bin, Xun Jiang, Zeyu Ma, Duo Peng, Guoqing Wang, Yang Yang, Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (61.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisualRouter 是一个无需训练、即插即用的查询引导视觉采样框架，用于长视频理解。它先将查询分类为全局或局部，再分别采用相关性-覆盖混合策略或事件感知帧选择策略，在有限帧数下平衡相关性、覆盖度与多样性，显著提升多个 LVLM 在长视频基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在视频理解上取得进展，但长视频因视觉 token 数量庞大和上下文窗口有限而难以处理。视觉采样通过选择信息丰富的帧子集提供实用方案，但现有方法要么依赖相关性感知采样导致帧选择冗余、时序覆盖不足，要么采用与查询类型无关的固定采样策略。

Method: VisualRouter 首先将每个查询分类为全局或局部两类。对全局查询采用相关性-覆盖混合策略，在保留时序覆盖的同时保留查询相关的视觉证据；对局部查询采用事件感知帧选择策略，进行事件划分、片段级帧分配和事件内帧选择，在有限输入帧下联合平衡相关性、覆盖度与多样性。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上，VisualRouter 相比均匀采样在 Video-MME、LongVideoBench 和 MLVU 上分别提升 5.2%、7.7% 和 11.6%，并在相同设置下优于现有无需训练的视觉采样方法。

Conclusion: 查询引导的视觉采样能根据查询类型自适应选择帧，兼顾相关性、覆盖度与多样性，是提升长视频理解性能的有效且无需训练的即插即用方案。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦长视频理解中的视觉采样问题，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 video_understanding_temporal。

Importance reason: Solid training-free efficiency work for long-video understanding, but it is a sampling/compression method rather than a core generative or continuous-representation advance. Signals: efficient inference, video understanding, training-free, query-grounded sampling, LVLM

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved significant progress in video understanding, yet understanding long videos remains challenging due to the large number of visual tokens and limited context windows. Visual sampling provides a practical solution by selecting an informative subset of frames. However, existing methods typically either rely on relevance-aware sampling, leading to redundant frame selection and insufficient temporal coverage, or adopt a fixed sampling strategy regardless of query type. In this paper, we propose VisualRouter, a training-free and plug-and-play framework for query-grounded visual sampling. VisualRouter first classifies each query as either global or local and then applies the corresponding sampling strategy. For global queries, it employs a relevance-coverage hybrid strategy that preserves temporal coverage while retaining query-relevant visual evidence. For local queries, it adopts an event-aware frame selection strategy that performs event partitioning, segment-level frame allocation, and intra-event frame selection, jointly balancing relevance, coverage, and diversity with a limited number of input frames. Experiments show that VisualRouter consistently improves multiple LVLMs over uniform sampling, achieving gains of 5.2%, 7.7%, and 11.6% on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and outperforming existing training-free visual sampling methods under the same setting.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Frame Selection: Generative Latent Evidence Aggregation for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28516)
*Bowen Liu, Shuning Wang, Xinpeng Ding, Zhiheng Wu, Bodong Du, Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (81.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GenEvA 提出一种生成式潜证据聚合框架，将选帧后的视频信息组织为查询相关的跨帧潜证据，用于提升长视频理解中 Video-MLLM 的问答性能，仅需极小的 token 开销即可在多个基准上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解管线通常将视频压缩为少量帧或视觉 token，但仅仅保留相关视觉内容作为显式证据并不能保证跨时刻的互补线索被整合用于回答。作者提出在选帧之后增加一个潜证据接口，将选帧组织为查询相关的跨帧证据，以提升回答质量。

Method: GenEvA 使用查询条件化的证据分布来聚焦聚合相关帧，从帧特定信息中形成紧凑的跨帧潜证据。由于跨帧整合并非总是必要，同一分布决定是否插入该潜补充（Adaptive Evidence Invocation），实现任务感知的分配。

Result: 在四个基准和两个 Video-MLLM 骨干上，GenEvA 一致提升匹配帧基线。8 帧时 LLaVA-Video 四基准平均提升 +5.2 分，Qwen2.5-VL 在 LVBench 上准确率提升 +10.1 分，仅需 0.11%-0.40% 的平均视频 token 开销。

Conclusion: 生成式潜证据聚合能有效提升长视频理解中跨帧互补信息的整合，且开销极小，验证了任务感知分配与自适应证据调用的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦长视频理解，通过生成式潜证据聚合提升 Video-MLLM 的视频问答能力，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] video_understanding_temporal。

Importance reason: Solid efficiency-focused improvement for long-video MLLM understanding, but it is not aligned with the user's core continuous-model or generation priorities. Signals: long-video understanding, video-mllm, latent evidence aggregation, token efficiency, query-conditioned

Abstract: Long-video understanding commonly compresses videos into a small set of frames or visual tokens for answer generation. Existing compact pipelines focus on retaining relevant visual content as explicit evidence. Yet making evidence available does not ensure that complementary cues across moments are integrated for answering. Our key idea is to organize selected frames into query-relevant cross-frame evidence before generation. We formulate this post-selection stage as a latent evidence interface and instantiate it with GenEvA ($\textbf{Gen}erative$ $Latent$ $\textbf{Ev}idence$ $\textbf{A}ggregation$), a distribution-guided latent evidence aggregation framework. Specifically, GenEvA uses a query-conditioned evidence distribution to focus aggregation on relevant frames, forming compact cross-frame latent evidence from their frame-specific information. Since cross-frame integration is not always needed, the same distribution determines whether to insert this latent complement. Across four benchmarks and two Video-MLLM backbones, GenEvA consistently improves matched-frame baselines. At 8 frames, it raises the four-benchmark LLaVA-Video average by $+5.2$ points and Qwen2.5-VL accuracy on LVBench by $+10.1$ points. These gains require only $0.11\%$--$0.40\%$ average video-token overhead; analyses further show task-aware allocation and benefits from Adaptive Evidence Invocation.

</details>
